local AVAILABLE_ANIMATION_SETS = {}

local function resetAnimationPool()
	table.clear(AVAILABLE_ANIMATION_SETS)

	for _, set in ipairs(BOT_ANIMATION_SETS) do
		table.insert(AVAILABLE_ANIMATION_SETS, set)
	end

	for i = #AVAILABLE_ANIMATION_SETS, 2, -1 do
		local j = math.random(1, i)
		AVAILABLE_ANIMATION_SETS[i], AVAILABLE_ANIMATION_SETS[j] =
			AVAILABLE_ANIMATION_SETS[j], AVAILABLE_ANIMATION_SETS[i]
	end
end

resetAnimationPool()

local function takeAnimationSet()
	if #AVAILABLE_ANIMATION_SETS == 0 then
		resetAnimationPool()
	end

	return table.remove(AVAILABLE_ANIMATION_SETS)
end

local function loadBotAnimations(botModel, humanoid)
	local animator = humanoid:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")

	if not animator then
		animator = Instance.new("Animator")
		animator.Parent = humanoid
	end

	local set = takeAnimationSet()

	local idleAnimation1 = Instance.new("Animation")
	idleAnimation1.AnimationId =
		"rbxassetid://" .. tostring(set.idle1)

	local idleAnimation2 = Instance.new("Animation")
	idleAnimation2.AnimationId =
		"rbxassetid://" .. tostring(set.idle2)

	local walkAnimation = Instance.new("Animation")
	walkAnimation.AnimationId =
		"rbxassetid://" .. tostring(set.walk)

	local idleTrack1
	local idleTrack2
	local walkTrack

	local ok1 = pcall(function()
		idleTrack1 = animator:LoadAnimation(idleAnimation1)
	end)

	local ok2 = pcall(function()
		idleTrack2 = animator:LoadAnimation(idleAnimation2)
	end)

	local ok3 = pcall(function()
		walkTrack = animator:LoadAnimation(walkAnimation)
	end)

	idleAnimation1:Destroy()
	idleAnimation2:Destroy()
	walkAnimation:Destroy()

	if ok1 and idleTrack1 then
		idleTrack1.Looped = true
		idleTrack1.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Idle
	end

	if ok2 and idleTrack2 then
		idleTrack2.Looped = true
		idleTrack2.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Idle
	end

	if ok3 and walkTrack then
		walkTrack.Looped = true
		walkTrack.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Movement
	end

	return {
		name = set.name,
		idle1 = idleTrack1,
		idle2 = idleTrack2,
		walk = walkTrack,
		currentIdle = nil,
		nextIdleSwap = time() + math.random(6, 14),
	}
end

local function stopTrack(track, fade)
	if track and track.IsPlaying then
		track:Stop(fade or 0.18)
	end
end

local function playBotIdle(bot)
	local anim = bot.animations

	if not anim then
		return
	end

	stopTrack(anim.walk, 0.18)

	if anim.currentIdle
		and anim.currentIdle.IsPlaying
		and time() < anim.nextIdleSwap then
		return
	end

	stopTrack(anim.idle1, 0.18)
	stopTrack(anim.idle2, 0.18)

	local nextIdle

	if math.random(1, 100) <= 72 then
		nextIdle = anim.idle1
	else
		nextIdle = anim.idle2
	end

	anim.currentIdle = nextIdle
	anim.nextIdleSwap = time() + math.random(7, 16)

	if nextIdle and not nextIdle.IsPlaying then
		nextIdle:Play(0.22, 1, 1)
	end
end

local function playBotWalk(bot)
	local anim = bot.animations

	if not anim then
		return
	end

	stopTrack(anim.idle1, 0.15)
	stopTrack(anim.idle2, 0.15)
	anim.currentIdle = nil

	if anim.walk and not anim.walk.IsPlaying then
		anim.walk:Play(0.18, 1, 1)
	end

	if anim.walk then
		-- Slightly change animation speed based on each bot's real WalkSpeed.
		local speedScale = math.clamp(
			bot.humanoid.WalkSpeed / WALK_SPEED,
			0.85,
			1.4
		)

		anim.walk:AdjustSpeed(speedScale)
	end
end

local function isBotActuallyMoving(bot)
	if not bot.root or not bot.root.Parent then
		return false
	end

	local velocity = bot.root.AssemblyLinearVelocity
	local horizontalSpeed = Vector3.new(
		velocity.X,
		0,
		velocity.Z
	).Magnitude

	return horizontalSpeed > 0.75
end

--------------------------------------------------
-- WEAPON RUNTIME RESET FOR A NEW SCRIPT EXECUTION
--------------------------------------------------
-- WeaponPackRuntime is global in executor sessions. Old test builds can
-- leave a bad saved Gingerscope pose behind, so never inherit positional
-- state from a previous execution.
WeaponPackRuntime.persistentGunFallbackLocalCFrame = nil
WeaponPackRuntime.persistentGunFallbackPoseWeapon = nil
WeaponPackRuntime.persistentGunFallbackUsesRootLock = false
WeaponPackRuntime.persistentGunFallback = nil
WeaponPackRuntime.persistentGunFallbackCharacter = nil
WeaponPackRuntime.persistentGunFallbackWeapon = nil
WeaponPackRuntime.lastGunBeltLocalCFrame = nil
WeaponPackRuntime.currentReplacementPart = nil
WeaponPackRuntime.currentReplacementWeaponName = nil
WeaponPackRuntime.currentReplacementCharacter = nil
WeaponPackRuntime.gingerscopeNativeAccessory = nil
WeaponPackRuntime.gingerscopeNativeCharacter = nil

-- Clear script-owned weapon visuals left by an earlier build in this session.
if player.Character then
	for _, child in ipairs(player.Character:GetChildren()) do
		if child.Name == "HolsteredWeapon"
			or child.Name == "HolsteredGun"
			or child.Name == "HolsteredKnife"
			or child.Name == "PersistentGingerscopeAccessory"
			or child:GetAttribute("ScriptOwnedWeapon") == true
			or child:GetAttribute("BotControllerNativeGingerscope") == true then

			pcall(function()
				child:Destroy()
			end)
		end
	end
end

-- Remove any native Gingerscope accessory left by an older build.
if player.Character then
	for _, child in ipairs(player.Character:GetChildren()) do
		if child.Name == "PersistentGingerscopeAccessory"
			or child:GetAttribute("BotControllerNativeGingerscope") == true then
			pcall(function()
				child:Destroy()
			end)
		end
	end
end

-- Remove stale Gingerscope workspace copies left by older attempts.
local stalePersistentFolder =
	workspace:FindFirstChild(
		"SlaveControllerPersistentVisuals"
	)

if stalePersistentFolder then
	for _, child in ipairs(
		stalePersistentFolder:GetChildren()
	) do
		if child.Name == "PersistentGingerscopeAccessory"
			or child:GetAttribute(
				"BotControllerNativeGingerscope"
			) == true then

			pcall(function()
				child:Destroy()
			end)
		end
	end
end

-- Remove persistent visual folders left by older attempts.
for _, folderName in ipairs({
	"SlaveControllerVisuals",
	"SlaveControllerPersistentVisuals",
}) do
	local oldFolder = workspace:FindFirstChild(folderName)
	if oldFolder then
		pcall(function()
			oldFolder:Destroy()
		end)
	end
end

-- Remove the named render bindings used by previous Gingerscope builds.
pcall(function()
	RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep(
		"BotControllerGingerscopeHostMask"
	)
end)
pcall(function()
	RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep(
		"BotControllerGingerscopePoseLock"
	)
end)

--------------------------------------------------
-- ONE SLICE FONT - SAFE / NON-BLOCKING
--------------------------------------------------

WeaponPackRuntime.ONE_SLICE_FONT_URL =
	"https://db.onlinewebfonts.com/t/b33fec3eb92d695592ab8607264202b2.ttf"

WeaponPackRuntime.ONE_SLICE_FONT_FILE =
	"slave_controller_one_slice.ttf"

WeaponPackRuntime.ONE_SLICE_FAMILY_FILE =
	"slave_controller_one_slice.font"

WeaponPackRuntime.ONE_SLICE_FONT =
	nil

WeaponPackRuntime.loadOneSliceFontSafe =
	function()
		local assetGetter =
			getcustomasset
			or getsynasset

		if type(assetGetter) ~= "function"
			or type(writefile) ~= "function" then
			return nil
		end

		local fontPath =
			WeaponPackRuntime.ONE_SLICE_FONT_FILE

		local familyPath =
			WeaponPackRuntime.ONE_SLICE_FAMILY_FILE

		local fontExists =
			false

		if type(isfile) == "function" then
			local okExists,
				exists =
					pcall(
						isfile,
						fontPath
					)

			fontExists =
				okExists
				and exists == true
		end

		if not fontExists then
			local okDownload,
				fontBytes =
					pcall(function()
						return game:HttpGet(
							WeaponPackRuntime.ONE_SLICE_FONT_URL
						)
					end)

			if not okDownload
				or type(fontBytes) ~= "string"
				or #fontBytes < 1000 then
				return nil
			end

			if not pcall(
				writefile,
				fontPath,
				fontBytes
			) then
				return nil
			end
		end

		local okFontAsset,
			fontAsset =
				pcall(
					assetGetter,
					fontPath
				)

		if not okFontAsset
			or type(fontAsset) ~= "string"
			or fontAsset == "" then
			return nil
		end

		local familyJson =
			'{"name":"One Slice","faces":[{"name":"Regular","weight":400,"style":"normal","assetId":"'
			.. fontAsset
			.. '"}]}'

		if not pcall(
			writefile,
				familyPath,
				familyJson
			) then
			return nil
		end

		local okFamilyAsset,
			familyAsset =
				pcall(
					assetGetter,
					familyPath
				)

		if not okFamilyAsset
			or type(familyAsset) ~= "string"
			or familyAsset == "" then
			return nil
		end

		local okFont,
			fontFace =
				pcall(function()
					return Font.new(
						familyAsset,
						Enum.FontWeight.Regular,
						Enum.FontStyle.Normal
					)
				end)

		if okFont then
			return fontFace
		end

		return nil
	end

WeaponPackRuntime.applyOneSliceToObject =
	function(object)
		if not object
			or not WeaponPackRuntime.ONE_SLICE_FONT
			or object:GetAttribute("KeepNativeFont") == true then
			return
		end

		if object:IsA("TextLabel")
			or object:IsA("TextButton")
			or object:IsA("TextBox") then

			pcall(function()
				object.FontFace =
					WeaponPackRuntime.ONE_SLICE_FONT
			end)
		end
	end

WeaponPackRuntime.applyOneSliceToGui =
	function(root)
		if not root
			or not WeaponPackRuntime.ONE_SLICE_FONT then
			return
		end

		for _, object in ipairs(
			root:GetDescendants()
		) do
			WeaponPackRuntime.applyOneSliceToObject(
				object
			)
		end
	end

WeaponPackRuntime.startOneSliceForGui =
	function(root)
		if not root then
			return
		end

		-- Dynamic buttons / labels created later.
		root.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(object)
			task.defer(function()
				if object
					and object.Parent then

					WeaponPackRuntime.applyOneSliceToObject(
						object
					)
				end
			end)
		end)

		-- Critical fix: font download/registration never blocks UI startup.
		task.spawn(function()
			local okLoad,
				fontFace =
					pcall(
						WeaponPackRuntime.loadOneSliceFontSafe
					)

			if okLoad
				and fontFace then

				WeaponPackRuntime.ONE_SLICE_FONT =
					fontFace

				if root
					and root.Parent then

					WeaponPackRuntime.applyOneSliceToGui(
						root
					)
				end
			end
		end)
	end

WeaponPackRuntime.CHROMA_GUN_BASE = {
	["Chroma Treat"] = "Treat",
	["Chroma Snowcannon"] = "Snowcannon",
	["Chroma Watergun"] = "Watergun",
	["Chroma Blizzard"] = "Blizzard",
	["Chroma Raygun"] = "Raygun",
	["Chroma Sunrise"] = "Sunrise",
	["Chroma Vampire's Gun"] = "Vampire's Gun",
	["Chroma Constellation"] = "Constellation",
	["Chroma Evergun"] = "Evergun",
	["Chroma Bauble"] = "Bauble",
}

WeaponPackRuntime.FAILED_GUN_LOOKUPS =
	WeaponPackRuntime.FAILED_GUN_LOOKUPS or {}


local function normalizeWeaponName(value)
	value = string.lower(tostring(value or ""))
	value = value:gsub("[’']", "")
	value = value:gsub("[^%w]", "")
	return value
end

local function isWeaponNameMatch(objectName, wantedName)
	local objectNormalized = normalizeWeaponName(objectName)

	for _, alias in ipairs(
		WEAPON_ALIASES[wantedName] or {wantedName}
	) do
		if objectNormalized == normalizeWeaponName(alias) then
			return true
		end
	end

	return false
end


--------------------------------------------------
-- DEEP CURRENT-MM2 VISUAL LOOKUP
--
-- FIX2:
--   * keeps MM2's INTERNAL weapon key from Sync.Weapons
--   * tries thread-identity-safe require for Database.Sync
--   * searches getgc() tables BY KEY as well as by Name fields
--   * can READ MeshPart source visuals safely
--   * never writes MeshPart.MeshId on the player's weapon
--------------------------------------------------

WeaponPackRuntime.RUNTIME_WEAPON_VISUAL_CACHE =
	WeaponPackRuntime.RUNTIME_WEAPON_VISUAL_CACHE or {}

-- Last-resort visual sources for weapons whose mesh data is not exposed
-- by the current MM2 client. Gingerscope first tries MM2's own data;
-- this catalog asset is only used if those exact-runtime searches fail.
WeaponPackRuntime.CATALOG_VISUAL_ASSETS =
	WeaponPackRuntime.CATALOG_VISUAL_ASSETS
	or {
		["Gingerscope"] = "rbxassetid://97550774411667",
	}

WeaponPackRuntime.SYNC_MATCH_CACHE =
	WeaponPackRuntime.SYNC_MATCH_CACHE or {}

WeaponPackRuntime.MM2_SYNC =
	WeaponPackRuntime.MM2_SYNC or nil

WeaponPackRuntime.MM2_SYNC_TRIED =
	WeaponPackRuntime.MM2_SYNC_TRIED or false

function WeaponPackRuntime.assetId(value)
	if value == nil then
		return nil
	end

	local number =
		tostring(value):match("(%d+)")

	if not number then
		return nil
	end

	return "rbxassetid://" .. number
end

function WeaponPackRuntime.v3(value, defaultValue)
	if typeof(value) == "Vector3" then
		return value
	end

	if type(value) == "table" then
		local x =
			tonumber(value.X or value.x or value[1])

		local y =
			tonumber(value.Y or value.y or value[2])

		local z =
			tonumber(value.Z or value.z or value[3])

		if x and y and z then
			return Vector3.new(x, y, z)
		end
	end

	return defaultValue
end

function WeaponPackRuntime.wantedIsChroma(name)
	return string.sub(
		normalizeWeaponName(name),
		1,
		6
	) == "chroma"
end

function WeaponPackRuntime.withoutChroma(name)
	local normalized =
		normalizeWeaponName(name)

	if string.sub(normalized, 1, 6) == "chroma" then
		return string.sub(normalized, 7)
	end

	return normalized
end

function WeaponPackRuntime.safeRequire(moduleScript)
	if not moduleScript
		or not moduleScript:IsA("ModuleScript") then

		return false, nil
	end

	local setter =
		setthreadidentity
		or set_thread_identity
		or setidentity

	local getter =
		getthreadidentity
		or get_thread_identity
		or getidentity

	local oldIdentity = nil

	if type(getter) == "function" then
		pcall(function()
			oldIdentity = getter()
		end)
	end

	if type(setter) == "function" then
		pcall(function()
			setter(2)
		end)
	end

	local ok, result =
		pcall(require, moduleScript)

	if type(setter) == "function" then
		pcall(function()
			setter(oldIdentity or 8)
		end)
	end

	return ok, result
end

function WeaponPackRuntime.getMM2Sync()
	if WeaponPackRuntime.MM2_SYNC_TRIED then
		return WeaponPackRuntime.MM2_SYNC
	end

	WeaponPackRuntime.MM2_SYNC_TRIED = true

	local database =
		ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Database")

	local syncModule =
		database
		and database:FindFirstChild("Sync")

	if not syncModule then
		return nil
	end

	local ok, result =
		WeaponPackRuntime.safeRequire(
			syncModule
		)

	if ok
		and type(result) == "table" then

		WeaponPackRuntime.MM2_SYNC =
			result
	end

	return WeaponPackRuntime.MM2_SYNC
end

function WeaponPackRuntime.getSyncSource()
	local sync =
		WeaponPackRuntime.getMM2Sync()

	if type(sync) ~= "table" then
		return nil
	end

	return
		type(sync.Weapons) == "table"
			and sync.Weapons
		or type(sync.Item) == "table"
			and sync.Item
		or type(sync.Items) == "table"
			and sync.Items
		or sync
end

function WeaponPackRuntime.resolveSyncRecord(wantedName)
	local cached =
		WeaponPackRuntime.SYNC_MATCH_CACHE[
			wantedName
		]

	if cached then
		return cached
	end

	local source =
		WeaponPackRuntime.getSyncSource()

	if type(source) ~= "table" then
		return nil
	end

	local target =
		WeaponPackRuntime.withoutChroma(
			wantedName
		)

	local wantChroma =
		WeaponPackRuntime.wantedIsChroma(
			wantedName
		)

	local expectedType =
		WeaponPackRuntime.WEAPON_TYPE_HINTS[
			wantedName
		]

	local expectedYear =
		WeaponPackRuntime.WEAPON_YEAR_HINTS[
			wantedName
		]

	local bestRecord = nil
	local bestScore = -math.huge

	for key, data in pairs(source) do
		if type(data) == "table" then
			local itemName =
				tostring(
					data.ItemName
					or data.DisplayName
					or data.Name
					or key
					or ""
				)

			local sameName =
				WeaponPackRuntime.withoutChroma(
					itemName
				) == target

				or isWeaponNameMatch(
					itemName,
					wantedName
				)

			if sameName then
				local itemType =
					tostring(
						data.ItemType
						or data.Type
						or data.WeaponType
						or ""
					)

				local typeOkay =
					expectedType == nil
					or itemType == ""
					or string.lower(itemType)
						== string.lower(
							expectedType
						)

				if typeOkay then
					local chroma =
						data.Chroma == true
						or data.IsChroma == true
						or string.lower(
							tostring(
								data.Rarity or ""
							)
						) == "chroma"

					local score = 100

					if chroma == wantChroma then
						score += 120
					elseif wantChroma then
						score -= 150
					end

					local year =
						tonumber(
							data.Year
							or data.EventYear
							or data.ReleaseYear
						)

					if expectedYear
						and year == expectedYear then

						score += 45
					end

					if score > bestScore then
						bestScore = score

						bestRecord = {
							key = key,
							data = data,
							chroma = chroma,
							itemName = itemName,
						}
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end

	if bestRecord then
		WeaponPackRuntime.SYNC_MATCH_CACHE[
			wantedName
		] = bestRecord
	end

	return bestRecord
end

function WeaponPackRuntime.resolveSyncEntry(wantedName)
	local record =
		WeaponPackRuntime.resolveSyncRecord(
			wantedName
		)

	return record
		and record.data
		or nil
end

function WeaponPackRuntime.buildCandidateNames(
	wantedName,
	syncRecord
)
	local normalized = {}
	local raw = {}

	local function add(value)
		if type(value) ~= "string"
			or value == "" then

			return
		end

		raw[value] = true
		normalized[
			normalizeWeaponName(value)
		] = true
	end

	for _, alias in ipairs(
		WEAPON_ALIASES[wantedName]
		or {wantedName}
	) do
		add(alias)
	end

	add(wantedName)

	if syncRecord then
		add(tostring(syncRecord.key or ""))

		local data =
			syncRecord.data

		if type(data) == "table" then
			for _, field in ipairs({
				"ItemName",
				"DisplayName",
				"Name",
				"ModelName",
				"AssetName",
				"WeaponName",
				"ItemID",
				"ID",
			}) do
				local value =
					data[field]

				if type(value) == "string" then
					add(value)
				end
			end
		end
	end

	-- Common internal-name variants.
	local base =
		WeaponPackRuntime.withoutChroma(
			wantedName
		)

	for rawName in pairs(raw) do
		add(
			string.gsub(
				rawName,
				"%s+",
				""
			)
		)

		add(
			string.gsub(
				rawName,
				"%s+",
				"_"
			)
		)
	end

	if WeaponPackRuntime.wantedIsChroma(
		wantedName
	) then
		add("Chroma" .. base)
		add("Chroma_" .. base)
	end

	return normalized, raw
end

function WeaponPackRuntime.extractVisualTable(
	value,
	defaultType,
	wantedName,
	depth,
	seen
)
	if type(value) ~= "table" then
		return nil
	end

	depth = depth or 0
	seen = seen or {}

	if depth > 7
		or seen[value] then

		return nil
	end

	seen[value] = true

	local meshId =
		value.MeshId
		or value.MeshID
		or value.Mesh
		or value.meshId
		or value.meshID

	if meshId
		and WeaponPackRuntime.assetId(
			meshId
		) then

		local textureCandidate =
			value.TextureId
			or value.TextureID
			or value.Texture
			or value.textureId
			or value.textureID

		-- If we asked for a chroma and this visual table exposes a
		-- chroma texture, use it while keeping the same mesh.
		if wantedName
			and WeaponPackRuntime.wantedIsChroma(
				wantedName
			) then

			textureCandidate =
				value.ChromaTexture
				or value.ChromaTextureId
				or value.ChromaTextureID
				or textureCandidate
		end

		return {
			Type =
				value.Type
				or value.ItemType
				or defaultType
				or "Knife",

			Placement =
				value.Placement,

			MeshId =
				WeaponPackRuntime.assetId(
					meshId
				),

			TextureId =
				WeaponPackRuntime.assetId(
					textureCandidate
				)
				or "",

			Scale =
				WeaponPackRuntime.v3(
					value.Scale,
					Vector3.new(1, 1, 1)
				),

			Offset =
				WeaponPackRuntime.v3(
					value.Offset,
					Vector3.zero
				),

			AttachmentPosition =
				WeaponPackRuntime.v3(
					value.AttachmentPosition,
					Vector3.zero
				),

			AttachmentOrientation =
				WeaponPackRuntime.v3(
					value.AttachmentOrientation,
					Vector3.zero
				),

			Color =
				typeof(value.Color) == "Color3"
				and value.Color
				or nil,

			Material =
				typeof(value.Material) == "EnumItem"
				and value.Material
				or nil,

			Transparency =
				type(value.Transparency) == "number"
				and value.Transparency
				or nil,

			Reflectance =
				type(value.Reflectance) == "number"
				and value.Reflectance
				or nil,

			VertexColor =
				typeof(value.VertexColor) == "Vector3"
				and value.VertexColor
				or nil,

			Source = "loaded visual table",
		}
	end

	for _, key in ipairs({
		"Visual",
		"Visuals",
		"WeaponVisual",
		"WeaponVisuals",
		"MeshData",
		"Display",
		"Asset",
		"Assets",
		"Model",
	}) do
		local child =
			rawget(value, key)

		if type(child) == "table" then
			local result =
				WeaponPackRuntime.extractVisualTable(
					child,
					defaultType,
					wantedName,
					depth + 1,
					seen
				)

			if result then
				return result
			end
		end
	end

	for _, child in pairs(value) do
		if type(child) == "table" then
			local result =
				WeaponPackRuntime.extractVisualTable(
					child,
					defaultType,
					wantedName,
					depth + 1,
					seen
				)

			if result then
				return result
			end
		end
	end

	return nil
end

function WeaponPackRuntime.meshPartScale(meshPart)
	if not meshPart
		or not meshPart:IsA("MeshPart") then

		return Vector3.new(1, 1, 1)
	end

	local meshSize = nil

	pcall(function()
		meshSize = meshPart.MeshSize
	end)

	if typeof(meshSize) == "Vector3"
		and meshSize.X ~= 0
		and meshSize.Y ~= 0
		and meshSize.Z ~= 0 then

		return Vector3.new(
			meshPart.Size.X / meshSize.X,
			meshPart.Size.Y / meshSize.Y,
			meshPart.Size.Z / meshSize.Z
		)
	end

	-- Fallback if MeshSize is not readable in this client.
	return meshPart.Size
end

function WeaponPackRuntime.extractInstanceVisual(
	instance,
	defaultType
)
	if not instance then
		return nil
	end

	local part = nil

	if instance:IsA("Tool") then
		part =
			instance:FindFirstChild("Handle")
			or instance:FindFirstChildWhichIsA(
				"BasePart",
				true
			)

	elseif instance:IsA("Model") then
		part =
			instance:FindFirstChild(
				"Handle",
				true
			)
			or instance:FindFirstChildWhichIsA(
				"BasePart",
				true
			)

	elseif instance:IsA("BasePart") then
		part = instance
	end

	if not part then
		return nil
	end

	-- Best case: normal Part + SpecialMesh.
	local specialMesh =
		part:FindFirstChildWhichIsA(
			"SpecialMesh"
		)

	if specialMesh
		and WeaponPackRuntime.assetId(
			specialMesh.MeshId
		) then

		return {
			Type = defaultType or "Knife",
			MeshId =
				WeaponPackRuntime.assetId(
					specialMesh.MeshId
				),
			TextureId =
				WeaponPackRuntime.assetId(
					specialMesh.TextureId
				)
				or "",
			Scale = specialMesh.Scale,
			Offset = specialMesh.Offset,
			Color = part.Color,
			Material = part.Material,
			Transparency = part.Transparency,
			Reflectance = part.Reflectance,
			VertexColor = specialMesh.VertexColor,
			Source = "loaded SpecialMesh",
		}
	end

	-- IMPORTANT:
	-- Reading MeshPart.MeshId is safe.
	-- The old giant-weapon bug came from WRITING MeshPart.MeshId on
	-- the player's current weapon. We still never do that.
	if part:IsA("MeshPart")
		and WeaponPackRuntime.assetId(
			part.MeshId
		) then

		return {
			Type = defaultType or "Knife",
			MeshId =
				WeaponPackRuntime.assetId(
					part.MeshId
				),
			TextureId =
				WeaponPackRuntime.assetId(
					part.TextureID
				)
				or "",
			Scale =
				WeaponPackRuntime.meshPartScale(
					part
				),
			Offset = Vector3.zero,
			Color = part.Color,
			Material = part.Material,
			Transparency = part.Transparency,
			Reflectance = part.Reflectance,
			Source = "loaded MeshPart",
		}
	end

	-- A model may have a non-Handle MeshPart even if the first BasePart
	-- wasn't the visual.
	if instance:IsA("Model")
		or instance:IsA("Tool") then

		for _, descendant in ipairs(
			instance:GetDescendants()
		) do
			if descendant:IsA("MeshPart")
				and WeaponPackRuntime.assetId(
					descendant.MeshId
				) then

				return {
					Type = defaultType or "Knife",
					MeshId =
						WeaponPackRuntime.assetId(
							descendant.MeshId
						),
					TextureId =
						WeaponPackRuntime.assetId(
							descendant.TextureID
						)
						or "",
					Scale =
						WeaponPackRuntime.meshPartScale(
							descendant
						),
					Offset = Vector3.zero,
					Color = descendant.Color,
					Material = descendant.Material,
					Transparency =
						descendant.Transparency,
					Reflectance =
						descendant.Reflectance,
					Source =
						"loaded model MeshPart",
				}
			end
		end
	end

	return nil
end

function WeaponPackRuntime.findVisualInMappingTable(
	container,
	wantedName,
	expectedType,
	syncRecord
)
	if type(container) ~= "table" then
		return nil
	end

	local normalizedNames, rawNames =
		WeaponPackRuntime.buildCandidateNames(
			wantedName,
			syncRecord
		)

	-- Fast exact raw-key lookups first.
	for rawName in pairs(rawNames) do
		local value =
			rawget(
				container,
				rawName
			)

		if type(value) == "table" then
			local visual =
				WeaponPackRuntime.extractVisualTable(
					value,
					expectedType,
					wantedName
				)

			if visual then
				return visual
			end
		end
	end

	-- Then normalized string-key comparison.
	local checked = 0

	for key, value in pairs(container) do
		checked += 1

		if checked > 1800 then
			break
		end

		if type(key) == "string"
			and type(value) == "table"
			and normalizedNames[
				normalizeWeaponName(key)
			] then

			local visual =
				WeaponPackRuntime.extractVisualTable(
					value,
					expectedType,
					wantedName
				)

			if visual then
				return visual
			end
		end
	end

	-- Common nested visual-map names.
	for _, childKey in ipairs({
		"WeaponVisuals",
		"Weapons",
		"Visuals",
		"Items",
		"WeaponData",
	}) do
		local child =
			rawget(
				container,
				childKey
			)

		if type(child) == "table" then
			local visual =
				WeaponPackRuntime.findVisualInMappingTable(
					child,
					wantedName,
					expectedType,
					syncRecord
				)

			if visual then
				return visual
			end
		end
	end

	return nil
end

function WeaponPackRuntime.searchLoadedGCTables(
	wantedName,
	expectedType,
	syncRecord
)
	if type(getgc) ~= "function" then
		return nil
	end

	local ok, objects =
		pcall(getgc, true)

	if not ok
		or type(objects) ~= "table" then

		return nil
	end

	local target =
		WeaponPackRuntime.withoutChroma(
			wantedName
		)

	local wantChroma =
		WeaponPackRuntime.wantedIsChroma(
			wantedName
		)

	local checked = 0

	for _, object in ipairs(objects) do
		checked += 1

		if checked % 500 == 0 then
			task.wait()
		end

		if checked > 18000 then
			break
		end

		if type(object) == "table" then
			-- THIS is the big fix:
			-- weapon visual data is often a map:
			-- WeaponVisuals["Xenoknife"] = { MeshId = ... }
			local mapped =
				WeaponPackRuntime.findVisualInMappingTable(
					object,
					wantedName,
					expectedType,
					syncRecord
				)

			if mapped then
				mapped.Source =
					"getgc visual map"

				return mapped
			end

			-- Also support a table that is itself one item record.
			local name =
				object.ItemName
				or object.DisplayName
				or object.Name

			if type(name) == "string"
				and WeaponPackRuntime.withoutChroma(
					name
				) == target then

				local chroma =
					object.Chroma == true
					or object.IsChroma == true
					or string.lower(
						tostring(
							object.Rarity or ""
						)
					) == "chroma"

				if chroma == wantChroma
					or not wantChroma then

					local visual =
						WeaponPackRuntime.extractVisualTable(
							object,
							expectedType,
							wantedName
						)

					if visual then
						visual.Source =
							"getgc item table"

						return visual
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end

	return nil
end

function WeaponPackRuntime.searchLoadedModules(
	wantedName,
	expectedType,
	syncRecord
)
	if type(getloadedmodules)
		~= "function" then

		return nil
	end

	local ok, modules =
		pcall(getloadedmodules)

	if not ok
		or type(modules) ~= "table" then

		return nil
	end

	local normalizedNames =
		WeaponPackRuntime.buildCandidateNames(
			wantedName,
			syncRecord
		)

	local checked = 0

	for _, moduleScript in ipairs(modules) do
		checked += 1

		if checked > 1200 then
			break
		end

		if typeof(moduleScript) == "Instance"
			and moduleScript:IsA("ModuleScript")
			and moduleScript:IsDescendantOf(
				ReplicatedStorage
			) then

			local normalizedModuleName =
				normalizeWeaponName(
					moduleScript.Name
				)

			local looksRelevant =
				normalizedNames[
					normalizedModuleName
				]
				or string.find(
					normalizedModuleName,
					"weapon",
					1,
					true
				)
				or string.find(
					normalizedModuleName,
					"visual",
					1,
					true
				)

			if looksRelevant then
				local reqOk, result =
					WeaponPackRuntime.safeRequire(
						moduleScript
					)

				if reqOk
					and type(result) == "table" then

					local visual =
						WeaponPackRuntime.findVisualInMappingTable(
							result,
							wantedName,
							expectedType,
							syncRecord
						)
						or WeaponPackRuntime.extractVisualTable(
							result,
							expectedType,
							wantedName
						)

					if visual then
						visual.Source =
							"loaded ModuleScript"

						return visual
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end

	return nil
end

function WeaponPackRuntime.searchReplicatedStorage(
	wantedName,
	expectedType,
	syncRecord
)
	local normalizedNames =
		WeaponPackRuntime.buildCandidateNames(
			wantedName,
			syncRecord
		)

	local roots = {}

	for _, rootName in ipairs({
		"Weapons",
		"Weapon",
		"WeaponModels",
		"Items",
		"Assets",
		"Models",
		"Resources",
		"Inventory",
		"Database",
	}) do
		local root =
			ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild(
				rootName
			)

		if root then
			table.insert(roots, root)
		end
	end

	table.insert(
		roots,
		ReplicatedStorage
	)

	local seen = {}
	local visited = 0

	for _, root in ipairs(roots) do
		for _, object in ipairs(
			root:GetDescendants()
		) do
			if seen[object] then
				continue
			end

			seen[object] = true
			visited += 1

			if visited % 500 == 0 then
				task.wait()
			end

			if visited > 22000 then
				break
			end

			local normalizedObject =
				normalizeWeaponName(
					object.Name
				)

			if normalizedNames[
				normalizedObject
			] then

				local visual =
					WeaponPackRuntime.extractInstanceVisual(
						object,
						expectedType
					)

				if visual then
					visual.Source =
						"ReplicatedStorage model"

					return visual
				end
			end
		end
	end

	return nil
end

function WeaponPackRuntime.loadCatalogWeaponVisual(
	wantedName
)
	local asset =
		WeaponPackRuntime.CATALOG_VISUAL_ASSETS[
			wantedName
		]

	if not asset then
		return nil
	end

	local ok, objects =
		pcall(function()
			return game:GetObjects(asset)
		end)

	if not ok
		or type(objects) ~= "table" then

		return nil
	end

	local expectedType =
		WeaponPackRuntime.WEAPON_TYPE_HINTS[
			wantedName
		]
		or "Gun"

	local foundVisual = nil

	for _, object in ipairs(objects) do
		local candidate = object

		if object:IsA("Accessory") then
			candidate =
				object:FindFirstChild("Handle")
				or object:FindFirstChildWhichIsA(
					"BasePart",
					true
				)
		end

		local visual =
			WeaponPackRuntime.extractInstanceVisual(
				candidate,
				expectedType
			)

		if visual then
			visual.Type = expectedType
			visual.Placement =
				expectedType == "Gun"
				and "WaistRight"
				or "WaistLeft"
			visual.Source =
				"catalog visual fallback"

			foundVisual = visual
		end

		pcall(function()
			object:Destroy()
		end)

		if foundVisual then
			break
		end
	end

	return foundVisual
end


--------------------------------------------------
-- NATIVE GINGERSCOPE ACCESSORY
--
-- The catalog fallback is a real Roblox WAIST ACCESSORY.
-- Previous builds extracted only MeshId/TextureId/Scale and discarded
-- the Handle attachment CFrame. That lost the exact orientation/height
-- data and forced us into unreliable manual CFrame corrections.
--
-- This build keeps the Accessory intact and lets Roblox match its Handle
-- attachment to the avatar's matching Waist*Attachment.
--------------------------------------------------

WeaponPackRuntime.GINGERSCOPE_NATIVE_ASSET =
	"rbxassetid://97550774411667"

-- Keep the exact native waist-accessory position.
-- The previous native position was the one that visually matched your rig.
WeaponPackRuntime.GINGERSCOPE_BODY_INSET =
	0.06

WeaponPackRuntime.gingerscopeNativeAccessory =
	WeaponPackRuntime.gingerscopeNativeAccessory
	or nil

WeaponPackRuntime.gingerscopeNativeCharacter =
	WeaponPackRuntime.gingerscopeNativeCharacter
	or nil

function WeaponPackRuntime.destroyGingerscopeNativeAccessory()
	local accessory =
		WeaponPackRuntime.gingerscopeNativeAccessory

	if accessory and accessory.Parent then
		pcall(function()
			accessory:Destroy()
		end)
	end

	WeaponPackRuntime.gingerscopeNativeAccessory = nil
	WeaponPackRuntime.gingerscopeNativeCharacter = nil
end

function WeaponPackRuntime.setGingerscopeNativeVisible(visible)
	local accessory =
		WeaponPackRuntime.gingerscopeNativeAccessory

	if not accessory or not accessory.Parent then
		return false
	end

	for _, object in ipairs(accessory:GetDescendants()) do
		if object:IsA("BasePart") then
			object.LocalTransparencyModifier =
				visible and 0 or 1

		elseif object:IsA("Decal")
			or object:IsA("Texture") then

			object.Transparency =
				visible and 0 or 1

		elseif object:IsA("ParticleEmitter")
			or object:IsA("Trail")
			or object:IsA("Beam")
			or object:IsA("Smoke")
			or object:IsA("Fire")
			or object:IsA("Sparkles") then

			object.Enabled = visible
		end
	end

	return true
end

function WeaponPackRuntime.getNativeAccessoryHandle(accessory)
	if not accessory then
		return nil
	end

	local handle =
		accessory:FindFirstChild("Handle")

	if handle and handle:IsA("BasePart") then
		return handle
	end

	return accessory:FindFirstChildWhichIsA(
		"BasePart",
		true
	)
end

function WeaponPackRuntime.getNativeAccessoryAttachment(handle)
	if not handle then
		return nil
	end

	-- Prefer waist attachments because this asset is a Waist Accessory.
	for _, attachmentName in ipairs({
		"WaistCenterAttachment",
		"WaistFrontAttachment",
		"WaistBackAttachment",
	}) do
		local attachment =
			handle:FindFirstChild(
				attachmentName
			)

		if attachment
			and attachment:IsA("Attachment") then

			return attachment
		end
	end

	-- Fallback: use whichever attachment the UGC creator supplied.
	return handle:FindFirstChildWhichIsA(
		"Attachment"
	)
end

function WeaponPackRuntime.findCharacterAttachmentByName(
	character,
	attachmentName
)
	if not character
		or not attachmentName then

		return nil
	end

	for _, object in ipairs(character:GetDescendants()) do
		if object:IsA("Attachment")
			and object.Name == attachmentName
			and object.Parent
			and object.Parent:IsA("BasePart")
			and not object:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Accessory") then

			return object
		end
	end

	return nil
end

function WeaponPackRuntime.getGingerscopeStableFolder()
	local folder =
		workspace:FindFirstChild(
			"SlaveControllerPersistentVisuals"
		)

	if not folder then
		folder = Instance.new("Folder")
		folder.Name =
			"SlaveControllerPersistentVisuals"
		folder:SetAttribute(
			"ScriptOwnedPersistentVisuals",
			true
		)
		folder.Parent = workspace
	end

	return folder
end

function WeaponPackRuntime.isGingerscopeNativeHealthy(
	character
)
	local accessory =
		WeaponPackRuntime.gingerscopeNativeAccessory

	if not accessory
		or not accessory.Parent
		or WeaponPackRuntime.gingerscopeNativeCharacter
			~= character then

		return false
	end

	local folder =
		WeaponPackRuntime.getGingerscopeStableFolder()

	if accessory.Parent ~= folder then
		return false
	end

	local handle =
		WeaponPackRuntime.getNativeAccessoryHandle(
			accessory
		)

	if not handle
		or not handle.Parent then

		return false
	end

	local accessoryAttachment =
		WeaponPackRuntime.getNativeAccessoryAttachment(
			handle
		)

	if not accessoryAttachment then
		return false
	end

	local bodyAttachment =
		WeaponPackRuntime.findCharacterAttachmentByName(
			character,
			accessoryAttachment.Name
		)

	-- During MM2's teleport/round rebuild there can be a short moment where
	-- LowerTorso/WaistAttachment is unavailable. Keep the existing visual
	-- alive and frozen instead of declaring it dead and recreating it.
	if not bodyAttachment
		or not bodyAttachment.Parent
		or not bodyAttachment.Parent:IsA("BasePart") then

		return handle.Anchored == true
	end

	local weld =
		handle:FindFirstChild(
			"PersistentGingerscopeBodyWeld"
		)

	if not weld
		or not weld:IsA("Weld")
		or weld.Part0 ~= bodyAttachment.Parent
		or weld.Part1 ~= handle then

		return false
	end

	return true
end

function WeaponPackRuntime.bindGingerscopeToCurrentWaist(
	character,
	accessory
)
	if not character
		or not accessory then

		return false,
			"missing character/accessory"
	end

	local handle =
		WeaponPackRuntime.getNativeAccessoryHandle(
			accessory
		)

	if not handle then
		return false,
			"native accessory has no Handle"
	end

	local accessoryAttachment =
		WeaponPackRuntime.getNativeAccessoryAttachment(
			handle
		)

	if not accessoryAttachment then
		return false,
			"native accessory has no waist attachment"
	end

	local bodyAttachment =
		WeaponPackRuntime.findCharacterAttachmentByName(
			character,
			accessoryAttachment.Name
		)

	if not bodyAttachment
		or not bodyAttachment.Parent
		or not bodyAttachment.Parent:IsA("BasePart") then

		-- Preserve the exact current world pose while MM2 rebuilds the torso.
		handle.Anchored = true

		local staleWeld =
			handle:FindFirstChild(
				"PersistentGingerscopeBodyWeld"
			)

		if staleWeld then
			staleWeld:Destroy()
		end

		local staleRootWeld =
			handle:FindFirstChild(
				"PersistentGingerscopeRootWeld"
			)

		if staleRootWeld then
			staleRootWeld:Destroy()
		end

		return true,
			"waiting for waist"
	end

	local bodyPart =
		bodyAttachment.Parent

	-- Exact native Roblox waist pose.
	local nativeWorldCFrame =
		bodyAttachment.WorldCFrame
		* accessoryAttachment.CFrame:Inverse()

	local nativeLocalCFrame =
		bodyPart.CFrame:ToObjectSpace(
			nativeWorldCFrame
		)

	-- Move only a tiny amount toward the torso center in BODY LOCAL SPACE.
	-- Y and rotation remain exactly native.
	local p =
		nativeLocalCFrame.Position

	local horizontal =
		Vector3.new(
			p.X,
			0,
			p.Z
		)

	local correctedPosition =
		p

	if horizontal.Magnitude > 0.001 then
		local correctedMagnitude =
			math.max(
				0,
				horizontal.Magnitude
					- WeaponPackRuntime.GINGERSCOPE_BODY_INSET
			)

		local correctedHorizontal =
			horizontal.Unit
			* correctedMagnitude

		correctedPosition =
			Vector3.new(
				correctedHorizontal.X,
				p.Y,
				correctedHorizontal.Z
			)
	end

	local correctedLocalCFrame =
		CFrame.new(
			correctedPosition
		)
		* nativeLocalCFrame.Rotation

	for _, weldName in ipairs({
		"AccessoryWeld",
		"PersistentGingerscopeRootWeld",
		"PersistentGingerscopeBodyWeld",
	}) do
		local old =
			handle:FindFirstChild(
				weldName
			)

		if old then
			old:Destroy()
		end
	end

	-- Weld the corrected native pose directly to the torso/waist body part.
	-- This keeps the same height relative to the outfit when MM2 changes
	-- HumanoidRootPart <-> LowerTorso spacing between lobby and round.
	local weld =
		Instance.new("Weld")

	weld.Name =
		"PersistentGingerscopeBodyWeld"

	weld.Part0 =
		bodyPart

	weld.Part1 =
		handle

	weld.C0 =
		correctedLocalCFrame

	weld.C1 =
		CFrame.new()

	weld.Parent =
		handle

	handle.CFrame =
		bodyPart.CFrame
		* correctedLocalCFrame

	handle.Anchored = false
	handle.CanCollide = false
	handle.CanTouch = false
	handle.CanQuery = false
	handle.Massless = true

	return true
end

function WeaponPackRuntime.ensureGingerscopeNativeAccessory(
	character
)
	if not character
		or character ~= player.Character
		or not character.Parent then

		return false,
			"character missing"
	end

	if WeaponPackRuntime.isGingerscopeNativeHealthy(
		character
	) then

		local currentAccessory =
			WeaponPackRuntime.gingerscopeNativeAccessory

		local currentHandle =
			WeaponPackRuntime.getNativeAccessoryHandle(
				currentAccessory
			)

		-- If we were temporarily frozen while the torso was rebuilding,
		-- bind back to the newly-created waist as soon as it exists.
		if currentHandle
			and currentHandle.Anchored then

			WeaponPackRuntime.bindGingerscopeToCurrentWaist(
				character,
				currentAccessory
			)
		end

		WeaponPackRuntime.setGingerscopeNativeVisible(
			true
		)

		return true
	end

	-- Before rebuilding the whole accessory, try repairing the existing one.
	local existingAccessory =
		WeaponPackRuntime.gingerscopeNativeAccessory

	if existingAccessory
		and existingAccessory.Parent then

		local repaired =
			WeaponPackRuntime.bindGingerscopeToCurrentWaist(
				character,
				existingAccessory
			)

		if repaired then
			WeaponPackRuntime.setGingerscopeNativeVisible(
				true
			)

			return true
		end
	end

	WeaponPackRuntime.destroyGingerscopeNativeAccessory()

	local folder =
		WeaponPackRuntime.getGingerscopeStableFolder()

	-- Remove stale copies from BOTH old Character-parented builds
	-- and the stable workspace folder.
	for _, container in ipairs({
		character,
		folder,
	}) do
		for _, child in ipairs(
			container:GetChildren()
		) do
			if child.Name ==
					"PersistentGingerscopeAccessory"
				or child:GetAttribute(
					"BotControllerNativeGingerscope"
				) == true then

				pcall(function()
					child:Destroy()
				end)
			end
		end
	end

	local ok, objects =
		pcall(function()
			return game:GetObjects(
				WeaponPackRuntime.GINGERSCOPE_NATIVE_ASSET
			)
		end)

	if not ok
		or type(objects) ~= "table"
		or #objects == 0 then

		return false,
			"could not load native Gingerscope waist accessory"
	end

	local accessory = nil

	for _, object in ipairs(objects) do
		if object:IsA("Accessory") then
			accessory = object
			break
		end

		local descendant =
			object:FindFirstChildWhichIsA(
				"Accessory",
				true
			)

		if descendant then
			accessory = descendant
			accessory.Parent = nil
			break
		end
	end

	for _, object in ipairs(objects) do
		if object ~= accessory
			and not (
				accessory
				and accessory:IsDescendantOf(object)
			) then

			pcall(function()
				object:Destroy()
			end)
		end
	end

	if not accessory then
		return false,
			"catalog asset did not return an Accessory"
	end

	accessory.Name =
		"PersistentGingerscopeAccessory"

	accessory:SetAttribute(
		"ScriptOwnedWeapon",
		true
	)

	accessory:SetAttribute(
		"BotControllerNativeGingerscope",
		true
	)

	accessory:SetAttribute(
		"OwnerUserId",
		player.UserId
	)

	local handle =
		WeaponPackRuntime.getNativeAccessoryHandle(
			accessory
		)

	if not handle then
		accessory:Destroy()
		return false,
			"native accessory has no Handle"
	end

	handle.CanCollide = false
	handle.CanTouch = false
	handle.CanQuery = false
	handle.Massless = true
	handle.Anchored = false

	local accessoryAttachment =
		WeaponPackRuntime.getNativeAccessoryAttachment(
			handle
		)

	if not accessoryAttachment then
		accessory:Destroy()
		return false,
			"native waist accessory has no attachment"
	end

	local bodyAttachment =
		WeaponPackRuntime.findCharacterAttachmentByName(
			character,
			accessoryAttachment.Name
		)

	if not bodyAttachment then
		accessory:Destroy()
		return false,
			"character is missing " ..
			tostring(accessoryAttachment.Name)
	end

	-- Parent OUTSIDE Character before welding.
	-- This is the key round-persistence change.
	accessory.Parent = folder

	local oldWeld =
		handle:FindFirstChild(
			"AccessoryWeld"
		)

	if oldWeld then
		oldWeld:Destroy()
	end

	-- Parent is stable workspace storage; pose is owned by the waist weld.
	local bindOk, bindReason =
		WeaponPackRuntime.bindGingerscopeToCurrentWaist(
			character,
			accessory
		)

	if not bindOk then
		accessory:Destroy()

		return false,
			bindReason
			or "could not bind Gingerscope to waist"
	end

	WeaponPackRuntime.gingerscopeNativeAccessory =
		accessory

	WeaponPackRuntime.gingerscopeNativeCharacter =
		character

	WeaponPackRuntime.setGingerscopeNativeVisible(
		true
	)

	return true
end


-- Strict Gingerscope visual isolation:
--   * NEVER modifies a knife.
--   * NEVER mesh-swaps/re-equips an MM2 weapon.
--   * Hides only containers that are definitely guns:
--       - Tool/model/accessory name says gun/revolver, OR
--       - it contains MM2's GunBelt attachment.
--   * Explicitly restores definite knife containers in case an older
--     generic mask touched them earlier in the session.
-- Gingerscope visual isolation is implemented later, after the script's
-- full MM2 original-weapon detector is defined. That detector is more robust
-- than the earlier name-only Gun/Revolver filter.


function WeaponPackRuntime.resolveRuntimeWeaponVisual(
	wantedName
)
	local cached =
		WeaponPackRuntime.RUNTIME_WEAPON_VISUAL_CACHE[
			wantedName
		]

	if cached then
		return cached
	end

	local expectedType =
		WeaponPackRuntime.WEAPON_TYPE_HINTS[
			wantedName
		]
		or "Knife"

	local syncRecord =
		WeaponPackRuntime.resolveSyncRecord(
			wantedName
		)

	local visual = nil

	-- Fast path: MM2's Sync.Weapons entry may already contain mesh data.
	if syncRecord
		and type(syncRecord.data) == "table" then

		visual =
			WeaponPackRuntime.extractVisualTable(
				syncRecord.data,
				expectedType,
				wantedName
			)

		if visual then
			visual.Source = "MM2 Sync.Weapons"
		end
	end

	-- Only run the heavier lookups for a weapon that has no verified fallback.
	if not visual then
		visual =
			WeaponPackRuntime.searchLoadedGCTables(
				wantedName,
				expectedType,
				syncRecord
			)
	end

	if not visual then
		visual =
			WeaponPackRuntime.searchLoadedModules(
				wantedName,
				expectedType,
				syncRecord
			)
	end

	if not visual then
		visual =
			WeaponPackRuntime.searchReplicatedStorage(
				wantedName,
				expectedType,
				syncRecord
			)
	end

	if not visual then
		visual =
			WeaponPackRuntime.loadCatalogWeaponVisual(
				wantedName
			)
	end

	if visual and visual.MeshId then
		visual.Type =
			visual.Type
			or expectedType

		visual.Placement =
			visual.Placement
			or (
				expectedType == "Gun"
				and "WaistRight"
				or "WaistLeft"
			)

		WeaponPackRuntime.RUNTIME_WEAPON_VISUAL_CACHE[
			wantedName
		] = visual

		return visual
	end

	return nil
end


local function findActualWeaponAsset(wantedName)
	local syncRecord =
		WeaponPackRuntime.resolveSyncRecord(
			wantedName
		)

	local normalizedNames =
		WeaponPackRuntime.buildCandidateNames(
			wantedName,
			syncRecord
		)

	local function matches(object)
		return object
			and normalizedNames[
				normalizeWeaponName(
					object.Name
				)
			] == true
	end

	local function searchContainer(container)
		if not container then
			return nil
		end

		for _, object in ipairs(
			container:GetDescendants()
		) do
			if matches(object)
				and (
					object:IsA("Tool")
					or object:IsA("Model")
					or object:IsA("MeshPart")
					or object:IsA("Part")
				) then

				return object
			end
		end

		return nil
	end

	return
		searchContainer(player.Character)
		or searchContainer(
			player:FindFirstChildOfClass(
				"Backpack"
			)
		)
		or searchContainer(
			ReplicatedStorage
		)
end

function WeaponPackRuntime.makeVisualTool(name, visual)
	if not visual or not visual.MeshId then return nil end
	local tool = Instance.new("Tool")
	tool.Name = name
	tool.RequiresHandle = true
	tool.CanBeDropped = false

	local handle = Instance.new("Part")
	handle.Name = "Handle"
	handle.Size = Vector3.new(1,1,1)
	handle.CanCollide = false
	handle.CanTouch = false
	handle.CanQuery = false
	handle.Massless = true
	handle.Parent = tool

	local mesh = Instance.new("SpecialMesh")
	mesh.MeshType = Enum.MeshType.FileMesh
	mesh.MeshId = visual.MeshId
	mesh.TextureId = visual.TextureId or ""
	mesh.Scale = visual.Scale or Vector3.new(1,1,1)
	mesh.Offset = visual.Offset or Vector3.zero
	mesh.Parent = handle
	return tool
end

local function makeFallbackVisualTool(name)
	return WeaponPackRuntime.makeVisualTool(name,FALLBACK_WEAPON_VISUALS[name])
end

local function cloneWeaponAsTool(wantedName)
	-- PERFORMANCE:
	-- Bot cosmetics never trigger the deep search.
	-- They use verified or already-cached visuals only.
	local exact =
		FALLBACK_WEAPON_VISUALS[
			wantedName
		]

	if exact then
		return WeaponPackRuntime.makeVisualTool(
			wantedName,
			exact
		)
	end

	local cached =
		WeaponPackRuntime.RUNTIME_WEAPON_VISUAL_CACHE[
			wantedName
		]

	if cached then
		return WeaponPackRuntime.makeVisualTool(
			wantedName,
			cached
		)
	end

	local baseName =
		WeaponPackRuntime.CHROMA_GUN_BASE[
			wantedName
		]

	local base =
		baseName
		and FALLBACK_WEAPON_VISUALS[
			baseName
		]

	if base then
		return WeaponPackRuntime.makeVisualTool(
			wantedName,
			base
		)
	end

	return nil
end

--------------------------------------------------
-- DRAGGING
--------------------------------------------------

local dragging = false
local dragStart
local startPosition

main.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
	if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
		dragging = true
		dragStart = input.Position
		startPosition = main.Position
	end
end)

UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
	if dragging and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement then
		local delta = input.Position - dragStart

		main.Position = UDim2.new(
			startPosition.X.Scale,
			startPosition.X.Offset + delta.X,
			startPosition.Y.Scale,
			startPosition.Y.Offset + delta.Y
		)
	end
end)

UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
	if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
		dragging = false
	end
end)

local function createButton(text, position, size, parentOverride)
	local button = Instance.new("TextButton")

	button.Size = size or UDim2.new(1, -36, 0, 42)
	button.Position = position
	button.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(14, 7, 10)
	button.BorderSizePixel = 0
	button.Text = text
	button.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(240, 226, 230)
	button.TextSize = 15
	button.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	button.AutoButtonColor = true
	button.Parent = parentOverride or main

	local c = Instance.new("UICorner")
	c.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 3)
	c.Parent = button

	local s = Instance.new("UIStroke")
	s.Color = Color3.fromRGB(105, 7, 22)
	s.Thickness = 1.25
	s.Transparency = 0.05
	s.Parent = button

	return button
end

local function setActiveTab(tabName)
	local botsActive =
		tabName == "BOTS"
	local weaponsActive =
		tabName == "WEAPONS"
	local spawnerActive =
		tabName == "SPAWNER"
	local tradeActive =
		tabName == "TRADE"
	local spawnerTradeActive =
		tabName == "SPAWNER_TRADE"
		or spawnerActive
		or tradeActive

	botsPage.Visible =
		botsActive

	weaponsPage.Visible =
		weaponsActive

	WeaponPackRuntime.ControllerUI.SpawnerTradePage.Visible =
		spawnerTradeActive

	WeaponPackRuntime.ControllerUI.SpawnerPage.Visible =
		false

	WeaponPackRuntime.ControllerUI.TradePage.Visible =
		false

	botsTabButton.BackgroundColor3 =
		botsActive
		and Color3.fromRGB(92, 7, 20)
		or Color3.fromRGB(14, 7, 10)

	weaponsTabButton.BackgroundColor3 =
		weaponsActive
		and Color3.fromRGB(92, 7, 20)
		or Color3.fromRGB(14, 7, 10)

	WeaponPackRuntime.ControllerUI.SpawnerTradeTabButton.BackgroundColor3 =
		spawnerTradeActive
		and Color3.fromRGB(92, 7, 20)
		or Color3.fromRGB(14, 7, 10)

end

botsTabButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
	setActiveTab("BOTS")
end)

weaponsTabButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
	setActiveTab("WEAPONS")
end)

WeaponPackRuntime.ControllerUI.SpawnerTradeTabButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
	setActiveTab("SPAWNER_TRADE")
end)
