WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.LoadModules = function()
	if WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.ModulesLoaded then
		return true
	end

	if not WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner
		or not WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner.LoadModules
		or not WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner.LoadModules() then

		warn(
			"[BAP Trade] Could not load MM2 inventory modules."
		)

		return false
	end

	local modules =
		ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild(
			"Modules"
		)

	if not modules then
		return false
	end

	local okTrade, tradeModule =
		pcall(function()
			return require(
				modules:WaitForChild(
					"TradeModule",
					5
				)
			)
		end)

	local okItem, itemModule =
		pcall(function()
			return require(
				modules:WaitForChild(
					"ItemModule",
					5
				)
			)
		end)

	if not okTrade
		or type(tradeModule) ~= "table"
		or not okItem
		or type(itemModule) ~= "table" then

		warn(
			"[BAP Trade] Could not load MM2 trade modules."
		)

		return false
	end

	WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.TradeModule =
		tradeModule

	WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.ItemModule =
		itemModule

	WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.InventoryModule =
		WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner.InventoryModule

	WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.ProfileData =
		WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner.ProfileData

	WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.Sync =
		WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner.Sync

	WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.ModulesLoaded =
		true

	return true
end

WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.PickFakeName = function()
	local names =
		WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.FakeNames

	WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.CurrentFakeName =
		names[
			math.random(
				1,
				#names
			)
		]
		or "RandomPlayer"

	return WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.CurrentFakeName
end

WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.CleanupOverlays = function()
	local tradeModule =
		WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.TradeModule

	local tradeGui =
		tradeModule
		and tradeModule.GUI
		and tradeModule.GUI.TradeGUI

	if not tradeGui then
		return
	end

	for _, descendant in ipairs(
		tradeGui:GetDescendants()
	) do
		if descendant.Name
				== "ClientPlaceholderTradeRemove"
			or descendant.Name
				== "ClientPlaceholderTradeToggle"
			or descendant.Name
				== "ClientPlaceholderTradeAcceptBridge"
			or descendant.Name
				== "ClientPlaceholderTradeAcceptOverlay"
			or descendant.Name
				== "ClientPlaceholderTradeActionOverlay"
			or descendant.Name
				== "CartiHubFakeTradeWatermark" then

			descendant:Destroy()
		end
	end
end

WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.GetOwnedTable = function(itemType)
	if WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner
		and WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner.GetProfileOwnedTable then

		return WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner.GetProfileOwnedTable(
			itemType
		)
	end

	return nil
end

WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.TextHasEvoToken = function(value)
	local textValue =
		tostring(
			value or ""
		):lower()

	return textValue == "evo"
		or textValue:find(
			"^evo[%s_%-%./]"
		) ~= nil
		or textValue:find(
			"[%s_%-%./]evo[%s_%-%./]"
		) ~= nil
		or textValue:find(
			"[%s_%-%./]evo$"
		) ~= nil
end

WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.CanTradeItem = function(itemId, itemType)
	local sync =
		WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.Sync

	local data =
		sync
		and sync[itemType]
		and sync[itemType][itemId]

	if type(data) ~= "table" then
		return false
	end

	if data.Tradeable == false
		or data.Tradable == false
		or data.NotTradeable == true
		or data.Untradeable == true
		or data.Untradable == true
		or data.IsEvo == true
		or data.Evo ~= nil
		or data.Evolution ~= nil
		or data.EvoData ~= nil
		or data.EvoBase ~= nil
		or data.EvoBaseID ~= nil
		or data.EvoIndex ~= nil
		or data.EvoLevel ~= nil
		or data.EvoLevels ~= nil
		or data.EvoXP ~= nil
		or data.EvolutionData ~= nil then

		return false
	end

	return not WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.TextHasEvoToken(
		itemId
	)
		and not WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.TextHasEvoToken(
			data.ItemName
		)
		and not WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.TextHasEvoToken(
			data.Name
		)
end

WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.CopyItemData = function(itemId, itemType, amount)
	local sync =
		WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.Sync

	local source =
		sync
		and sync[itemType]
		and sync[itemType][itemId]

	if not source then
		return nil
	end

	local data = {}

	for key, value in pairs(
		source
	) do
		data[key] =
			value
	end

	data.DataType =
		itemType

	data.Amount =
		amount or 1

	return data
end

WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.ClearOfferSlots = function(offerFrame)
	if not offerFrame
		or not offerFrame:FindFirstChild(
			"Container"
		) then

		return
	end

	for index = 1, 4 do
		local slot =
			offerFrame.Container:FindFirstChild(
				"NewItem" .. index
			)

		if slot then
			pcall(function()
				WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.ItemModule.DisplayItem(
					slot,
					nil
				)
			end)

			slot.Visible =
				false
		end
	end
end

WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.DrawOfferSlots = function(offerFrame, offerList)
	WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.ClearOfferSlots(
		offerFrame
	)

	for index, offer in ipairs(
		offerList
	) do
		local slot =
			offerFrame
			and offerFrame:FindFirstChild(
				"Container"
			)
			and offerFrame.Container:FindFirstChild(
				"NewItem" .. index
			)

		local itemData =
			WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.CopyItemData(
				offer.ItemID,
				offer.ItemType,
				offer.Amount
			)

		if slot
			and itemData then

			pcall(function()
				WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.ItemModule.DisplayItem(
					slot,
					itemData
				)
			end)

			slot.Visible =
				true
		end
	end
end

WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.ApplyInventoryDelta = function(itemId, itemType, delta)
	local owned =
		WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.GetOwnedTable(
			itemType
		)

	if not owned then
		return
	end

	local current =
		tonumber(
			owned[itemId]
		) or 0

	local hasFakeStack =
		WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner
		and WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner.GetFakeInventoryAmount
		and WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner.GetFakeInventoryAmount(
			itemId,
			itemType
		) > 0

	local nextAmount

	if hasFakeStack
		and WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner.AddFakeInventoryAmount then

		nextAmount =
			WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner.AddFakeInventoryAmount(
				itemId,
				itemType,
				delta
			)
	else
		nextAmount =
			current
			+ delta

		if nextAmount > 0 then
			owned[itemId] =
				nextAmount
		else
			owned[itemId] =
				nil
		end
	end

	local tradeModule =
		WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.TradeModule

	local tradeInventory =
		tradeModule
		and tradeModule.TradeInventory

	local entry =
		tradeInventory
		and tradeInventory.Data
		and tradeInventory.Data[itemType]
		and tradeInventory.Data[itemType].Current
		and tradeInventory.Data[itemType].Current[itemId]

	if entry then
		entry.Amount =
			math.max(
				0,
				(tonumber(entry.Amount) or current)
				+ delta
			)

		if entry.Frame then
			if entry.Amount <= 0 then
				entry.Frame.Visible =
					false
			else
				local itemData =
					WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.CopyItemData(
						itemId,
						itemType,
						entry.Amount
					)

				if itemData then
					pcall(function()
						WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.ItemModule.DisplayItem(
							entry.Frame,
							itemData,
							nil,
							true
						)
					end)
				end

				entry.Frame.Visible =
					true
			end
		end
	end
end

WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.RestoreLocalOffer = function()
	for _, offer in ipairs(
		WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.LocalOffer
	) do
		WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.ApplyInventoryDelta(
			offer.ItemID,
			offer.ItemType,
			offer.Amount or 1
		)
	end

	table.clear(
		WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.LocalOffer
	)
end

WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.SetAcceptContainerOverlayVisible = function(visible)
	local tradeModule =
		WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.TradeModule

	local actions =
		tradeModule
		and tradeModule.GUI
		and tradeModule.GUI.Actions

	local accept =
		actions
		and actions:FindFirstChild(
			"Accept"
		)

	local overlay =
		accept
		and accept:FindFirstChild(
			"ClientPlaceholderTradeActionOverlay"
		)

	if overlay
		and overlay:IsA(
			"GuiObject"
		) then

		overlay.Visible =
			visible == true
	end
end

WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.SetAcceptState = function(mode)
	local tradeModule =
		WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.TradeModule

	local gui =
		tradeModule
		and tradeModule.GUI

	local actions =
		gui
		and gui.Actions

	local accept =
		actions
		and actions:FindFirstChild(
			"Accept"
		)

	if not accept then
		return
	end

	WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.LocalAcceptMode =
		mode

	if accept:FindFirstChild(
		"Confirm"
	) then
		accept.Confirm.Visible =
			mode == "Confirm"
	end

	if accept:FindFirstChild(
		"Cancel"
	) then
		accept.Cancel.Visible =
			mode == "Waiting"
			or mode == "BothAccepted"
	end

	if accept:FindFirstChild(
		"Cooldown"
	)
		and mode ~= "Cooldown" then

		accept.Cooldown.Visible =
			false
	end

	if accept:FindFirstChild(
		"AddItem"
	) then
		accept.AddItem.Visible =
			mode == "Cooldown"
	end

	if gui.YourOffer
		and gui.YourOffer:FindFirstChild(
			"Accepted"
		) then

		gui.YourOffer.Accepted.Visible =
			mode == "Waiting"
			or mode == "BothAccepted"
	end

	if gui.TheirOffer
		and gui.TheirOffer:FindFirstChild(
			"Accepted"
		) then

		gui.TheirOffer.Accepted.Visible =
			WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.OtherAccepted == true
			or mode == "BothAccepted"
	end

	WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.SetAcceptContainerOverlayVisible(
		mode == "Accept"
	)
end

WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.HandleAcceptClick = function()
	if #WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.LocalOffer == 0 then
		return
	end

	-- The fake other player must accept first.
	-- After the 6-second cooldown ends, their acceptance happens
	-- after a randomized 1.0-1.5 second delay.
	if not WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.OtherAccepted then
		return
	end

	if WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.LocalAcceptMode
			== "Accept"
		and time()
			>= WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.CooldownEndsAt then

		WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.LocalConfirmStartedAt =
			time()

		WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.SetAcceptState(
			"Confirm"
		)
	end
end

WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.ApplyNativeAcceptVisual = function()
	local tradeModule =
		WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.TradeModule

	local actions =
		tradeModule
		and tradeModule.GUI
		and tradeModule.GUI.Actions

	local accept =
		actions
		and actions:FindFirstChild(
			"Accept"
		)

	local actionButton =
		accept
		and accept:FindFirstChild(
			"ActionButton"
		)

	if not actionButton
		or not actionButton:IsA(
			"GuiObject"
		) then

		return
	end

	-- Cache MM2's untouched native enabled button BEFORE changing anything.
	-- V23 only cached colors, which meant our fake gray geometry/text styling
	-- leaked into the green enabled state after the timer ended.
	if WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.NativeAcceptStyle == nil then
		WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.NativeAcceptStyle = {
			Size =
				actionButton.Size,
			Position =
				actionButton.Position,
			AnchorPoint =
				actionButton.AnchorPoint,
			BackgroundColor3 =
				actionButton.BackgroundColor3,
			BackgroundTransparency =
				actionButton.BackgroundTransparency,
			BorderColor3 =
				actionButton.BorderColor3,
			BorderSizePixel =
				actionButton.BorderSizePixel,
			ZIndex =
				actionButton.ZIndex,
			AutoButtonColor =
				actionButton:IsA("GuiButton")
				and actionButton.AutoButtonColor
				or nil,
			Text =
				actionButton:IsA("TextButton")
				and actionButton.Text
				or nil,
			Font =
				actionButton:IsA("TextButton")
				and actionButton.Font
				or nil,
			TextSize =
				actionButton:IsA("TextButton")
				and actionButton.TextSize
				or nil,
			TextColor3 =
				actionButton:IsA("TextButton")
				and actionButton.TextColor3
				or nil,
			TextTransparency =
				actionButton:IsA("TextButton")
				and actionButton.TextTransparency
				or nil,
			TextStrokeColor3 =
				actionButton:IsA("TextButton")
				and actionButton.TextStrokeColor3
				or nil,
			TextStrokeTransparency =
				actionButton:IsA("TextButton")
				and actionButton.TextStrokeTransparency
				or nil,
		}

		WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.NativeAcceptCornerRadii =
			{}

		for _, child in ipairs(
			actionButton:GetChildren()
		) do
			if child:IsA(
				"UICorner"
			) then
				WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.NativeAcceptCornerRadii[
					child
				] =
					child.CornerRadius
			end
		end
	end

	local hasOffer =
		#WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.LocalOffer > 0

	local ready =
		hasOffer
		and WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.LocalAcceptMode
			== "Accept"
		and time()
			>= WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.CooldownEndsAt

	if ready then
		-- Restore MM2's REAL enabled Accept button 1:1.
		-- Do not keep any of the screenshot-matching gray overrides.
		local nativeStyle =
			WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.NativeAcceptStyle

		actionButton.Size =
			nativeStyle.Size

		actionButton.Position =
			nativeStyle.Position

		actionButton.AnchorPoint =
			nativeStyle.AnchorPoint

		actionButton.BackgroundColor3 =
			nativeStyle.BackgroundColor3

		actionButton.BackgroundTransparency =
			nativeStyle.BackgroundTransparency

		actionButton.BorderColor3 =
			nativeStyle.BorderColor3

		actionButton.BorderSizePixel =
			nativeStyle.BorderSizePixel

		actionButton.ZIndex =
			nativeStyle.ZIndex

		if actionButton:IsA(
			"GuiButton"
		)
			and nativeStyle.AutoButtonColor ~= nil then

			actionButton.AutoButtonColor =
				nativeStyle.AutoButtonColor
		end

		if actionButton:IsA(
			"TextButton"
		) then

			actionButton.Text =
				nativeStyle.Text

			actionButton.Font =
				nativeStyle.Font

			actionButton.TextSize =
				nativeStyle.TextSize

			actionButton.TextColor3 =
				nativeStyle.TextColor3

			actionButton.TextTransparency =
				nativeStyle.TextTransparency

			actionButton.TextStrokeColor3 =
				nativeStyle.TextStrokeColor3

			actionButton.TextStrokeTransparency =
				nativeStyle.TextStrokeTransparency
		end

		for corner, radius in pairs(
			WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.NativeAcceptCornerRadii
			or {}
		) do
			if corner
				and corner.Parent == actionButton then

				corner.CornerRadius =
					radius
			end
		end

		return
	end

	if hasOffer then
		-- While the 6-second cooldown is active, MM2's Cooldown frame
		-- is shown instead of ActionButton. Do not restyle the hidden button.
		return
	end

	-- Empty-offer disabled state: match the user's supplied gray reference.
	-- Only this state is customized.
	actionButton.AnchorPoint =
		Vector2.new(
			0.5,
			0.5
		)

	actionButton.Position =
		UDim2.new(
			0.5,
			0,
			0.5,
			0
		)

	actionButton.Size =
		UDim2.new(
			0,
			231,
			0,
			50
		)

	actionButton.BackgroundColor3 =
		Color3.fromRGB(
			91,
			91,
			91
		)

	actionButton.BackgroundTransparency =
		0

	actionButton.BorderColor3 =
		Color3.fromRGB(
			29,
			29,
			29
		)

	actionButton.BorderSizePixel =
		3

	if actionButton:IsA(
		"GuiButton"
	) then
		actionButton.AutoButtonColor =
			false
	end

	for _, child in ipairs(
		actionButton:GetChildren()
	) do
		if child:IsA(
			"UICorner"
		) then
			child.CornerRadius =
				UDim.new(
					0,
					0
				)
		end
	end

	if actionButton:IsA(
		"TextButton"
	) then
		actionButton.Text =
			"ACCEPT"

		actionButton.Font =
			Enum.Font.SourceSansBold

		actionButton.TextSize =
			31

		actionButton.TextColor3 =
			Color3.fromRGB(
				255,
				255,
				255
			)

		actionButton.TextTransparency =
			0

		actionButton.TextStrokeColor3 =
			Color3.fromRGB(
				29,
				29,
				29
			)

		actionButton.TextStrokeTransparency =
			0.25
	end
end

WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.WakeAcceptActionButton = function()
	local tradeModule =
		WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.TradeModule

	local actions =
		tradeModule
		and tradeModule.GUI
		and tradeModule.GUI.Actions

	local accept =
		actions
		and actions:FindFirstChild(
			"Accept"
		)

	if not accept then
		return
	end

	accept.Visible =
		true

	if accept:FindFirstChild(
		"Cooldown"
	)
		and WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.LocalAcceptMode
			~= "Cooldown" then

		accept.Cooldown.Visible =
			false
	end

	if accept:FindFirstChild(
		"AddItem"
	)
		and WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.LocalAcceptMode
			~= "Cooldown" then

		accept.AddItem.Visible =
			false
	end

	if accept:FindFirstChild(
		"Confirm"
	)
		and WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.LocalAcceptMode
			~= "Confirm" then

		accept.Confirm.Visible =
			false
	end

	if accept:FindFirstChild(
		"Cancel"
	)
		and WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.LocalAcceptMode
			~= "Waiting"
		and WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.LocalAcceptMode
			~= "BothAccepted" then

		accept.Cancel.Visible =
			false
	end

	local actionButton =
		accept:FindFirstChild(
			"ActionButton"
		)

	if actionButton
		and actionButton:IsA(
			"GuiObject"
		) then

		actionButton.Visible =
			true

		WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.ApplyNativeAcceptVisual()
	end

	if actionButton
		and actionButton:IsA(
			"GuiButton"
		) then

		actionButton.Active =
			true

		actionButton.Selectable =
			true

		actionButton.AutoButtonColor =
			true
	end

	local containerOverlay =
		accept:FindFirstChild(
			"ClientPlaceholderTradeActionOverlay"
		)

	if not containerOverlay then
		containerOverlay =
			Instance.new(
				"TextButton"
			)

		containerOverlay.Name =
			"ClientPlaceholderTradeActionOverlay"

		containerOverlay.BackgroundTransparency =
			1

		containerOverlay.BorderSizePixel =
			0

		containerOverlay.Text =
			""

		containerOverlay.Size =
			UDim2.fromScale(
				1,
				1
			)

		containerOverlay.Position =
			UDim2.fromScale(
				0,
				0
			)

		containerOverlay.Parent =
			accept

		containerOverlay.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
			WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.HandleAcceptClick()
		end)
	end

	if containerOverlay:IsA(
		"GuiObject"
	) then
		containerOverlay.Visible =
			WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.LocalAcceptMode
				== "Accept"

		containerOverlay.BackgroundTransparency =
			1

		containerOverlay.ZIndex =
			accept.ZIndex + 200

		containerOverlay.Size =
			UDim2.fromScale(
				1,
				1
			)

		containerOverlay.Position =
			UDim2.fromScale(
				0,
				0
			)
	end

	if containerOverlay:IsA(
		"GuiButton"
	) then
		containerOverlay.Active =
			true

		containerOverlay.Selectable =
			true

		containerOverlay.AutoButtonColor =
			false
	end

	local overlay =
		actionButton
		and actionButton:FindFirstChild(
			"ClientPlaceholderTradeActionOverlay"
		)

	if actionButton
		and not overlay then

		overlay =
			Instance.new(
				"TextButton"
			)

		overlay.Name =
			"ClientPlaceholderTradeActionOverlay"

		overlay.BackgroundTransparency =
			1

		overlay.BorderSizePixel =
			0

		overlay.Text =
			""

		overlay.Size =
			UDim2.fromScale(
				1,
				1
			)

		overlay.Position =
			UDim2.fromScale(
				0,
				0
			)

		overlay.Parent =
			actionButton

		overlay.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
			WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.HandleAcceptClick()
		end)
	end

	if overlay
		and overlay:IsA(
			"GuiObject"
		) then

		overlay.Visible =
			WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.LocalAcceptMode
				== "Accept"

		overlay.BackgroundTransparency =
			1

		overlay.ZIndex =
			actionButton.ZIndex + 100

		overlay.Size =
			UDim2.fromScale(
				1,
				1
			)

		overlay.Position =
			UDim2.fromScale(
				0,
				0
			)
	end

	if overlay
		and overlay:IsA(
			"GuiButton"
		) then

		overlay.Active =
			true

		overlay.Selectable =
			true

		overlay.AutoButtonColor =
			false
	end
end

WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.ResetAcceptUi = function()
	WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.CooldownSequence += 1
	WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.OtherAcceptSequence += 1
	WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.OtherAccepted =
		false
	WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.CooldownEndsAt =
		0

	WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.LocalConfirmStartedAt =
		0

	WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.SetAcceptState(
		"Accept"
	)

	WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.WakeAcceptActionButton()

	task.defer(
		WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.WakeAcceptActionButton
	)

	local tradeModule =
		WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.TradeModule

	local actions =
		tradeModule
		and tradeModule.GUI
		and tradeModule.GUI.Actions

	if actions
		and actions:FindFirstChild(
			"oldconfirm"
		) then

		actions.oldconfirm.Visible =
			false
	end
end

WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.StartAcceptCooldown = function(seconds)
	local tradeModule =
		WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.TradeModule

	local actions =
		tradeModule
		and tradeModule.GUI
		and tradeModule.GUI.Actions

	local accept =
		actions
		and actions:FindFirstChild(
			"Accept"
		)

	local cooldown =
		accept
		and accept:FindFirstChild(
			"Cooldown"
		)

	local title =
		cooldown
		and cooldown:FindFirstChild(
			"Title"
		)

	if not cooldown then
		return
	end

	WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.CooldownSequence += 1

	local sequence =
		WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.CooldownSequence

	WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.CooldownEndsAt =
		time()
		+ seconds

	WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.LocalAcceptMode =
		"Cooldown"

	if accept:FindFirstChild(
		"Confirm"
	) then
		accept.Confirm.Visible =
			false
	end

	if accept:FindFirstChild(
		"Cancel"
	) then
		accept.Cancel.Visible =
			false
	end

	if tradeModule.GUI.YourOffer
		and tradeModule.GUI.YourOffer:FindFirstChild(
			"Accepted"
		) then

		tradeModule.GUI.YourOffer.Accepted.Visible =
			false
	end

	cooldown.Visible =
		true

	if accept:FindFirstChild(
		"ActionButton"
	)
		and accept.ActionButton:IsA(
			"GuiObject"
		) then

		accept.ActionButton.Visible =
			false
	end

	WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.SetAcceptContainerOverlayVisible(
		false
	)

	task.spawn(function()
		while sequence
			== WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.CooldownSequence do

			local remaining =
				math.max(
					0,
					math.ceil(
						WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.CooldownEndsAt
						- time()
					)
				)

			if title then
				title.Text =
					("Please wait (%d) before accepting."):format(
						remaining
					)
			end

			if remaining <= 0 then
				break
			end

			task.wait(
				0.2
			)
		end

		if sequence
			== WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.CooldownSequence
			and WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.Active then

			WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.CooldownEndsAt =
				0

			WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.SetAcceptState(
				"Accept"
			)

			-- Cooldown is finished. Make the fake other player
			-- accept first after a randomized 1.0-1.5 second delay.
			WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.OtherAccepted =
				false

			WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.WakeAcceptActionButton()

			local otherSequence =
				WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.OtherAcceptSequence

			task.delay(
				math.random(
					10,
					15
				) / 10,
				function()
					if otherSequence
							~= WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.OtherAcceptSequence
						or not WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.Active
						or WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.LocalAcceptMode
							~= "Accept"
						or #WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.LocalOffer == 0 then

						return
					end

					WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.OtherAccepted =
						true

					if WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.TradeModule
						and WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.TradeModule.GUI
						and WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.TradeModule.GUI.TheirOffer
						and WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.TradeModule.GUI.TheirOffer:FindFirstChild(
							"Accepted"
						) then

						WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.TradeModule.GUI.TheirOffer.Accepted.Visible =
							true
					end
				end
			)
			WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.ApplyNativeAcceptVisual()

			-- Re-apply once on the next frame as MM2 may update the
			-- same ActionButton immediately after the cooldown ends.
			task.defer(function()
				if WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.Active
					and WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.LocalAcceptMode
						== "Accept" then

					WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.WakeAcceptActionButton()
					WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.ApplyNativeAcceptVisual()
				end
			end)
		end
	end)
end

WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.InstallOfferRemoveButtons = function()
	local tradeModule =
		WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.TradeModule

	local yourOffer =
		tradeModule
		and tradeModule.GUI
		and tradeModule.GUI.YourOffer

	if not yourOffer
		or not yourOffer:FindFirstChild(
			"Container"
		) then

		return
	end

	for index = 1, 4 do
		local slot =
			yourOffer.Container:FindFirstChild(
				"NewItem" .. index
			)

		if slot then
			local oldOverlay =
				slot:FindFirstChild(
					"ClientPlaceholderTradeRemove"
				)

			if oldOverlay then
				oldOverlay:Destroy()
			end

			if WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.LocalOffer[
				index
			] then

				local overlay =
					Instance.new(
						"TextButton"
					)

				overlay.Name =
					"ClientPlaceholderTradeRemove"

				overlay.BackgroundTransparency =
					1

				overlay.BorderSizePixel =
					0

				overlay.Text =
					""

				overlay.Size =
					UDim2.fromScale(
						1,
						1
					)

				overlay.Position =
					UDim2.fromScale(
						0,
						0
					)

				overlay.ZIndex =
					slot.ZIndex + 50

				overlay.Parent =
					slot

				overlay.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
					local removed =
						table.remove(
							WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.LocalOffer,
							index
						)

					if removed then
						WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.ApplyInventoryDelta(
							removed.ItemID,
							removed.ItemType,
							removed.Amount or 1
						)
					end

					WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.RedrawLocalTrade()
				end)
			end
		end
	end
end

WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.RedrawLocalTrade = function()
	local tradeModule =
		WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.TradeModule

	if not tradeModule
		or not tradeModule.GUI then

		return
	end

	WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.DrawOfferSlots(
		tradeModule.GUI.YourOffer,
		WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.LocalOffer
	)

	WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.DrawOfferSlots(
		tradeModule.GUI.TheirOffer,
		WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.TheirOffer
	)

	WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.ResetAcceptUi()

	if tradeModule.GUI.TheirOffer
		and tradeModule.GUI.TheirOffer:FindFirstChild(
			"Username"
		) then

		tradeModule.GUI.TheirOffer.Username.Text =
			"("
			.. tostring(
				WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.CurrentFakeName
			)
			.. ")"
	end

	WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.InstallOfferRemoveButtons()
end

WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.ToggleLocalOffer = function(itemId, itemType)
	if not WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.CanTradeItem(
		itemId,
		itemType
	) then

		return
	end

	for _, offer in ipairs(
		WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.LocalOffer
	) do
		if offer.ItemID == itemId
			and offer.ItemType == itemType then

			offer.Amount =
				(tonumber(offer.Amount) or 1)
				+ 1

			WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.ApplyInventoryDelta(
				itemId,
				itemType,
				-1
			)

			WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.RedrawLocalTrade()

			WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.StartAcceptCooldown(
				6
			)

			return
		end
	end

	if #WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.LocalOffer >= 4 then
		return
	end

	table.insert(
		WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.LocalOffer,
		{
			ItemID = itemId,
			Amount = 1,
			ItemType = itemType,
		}
	)

	WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.ApplyInventoryDelta(
		itemId,
		itemType,
		-1
	)

	WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.RedrawLocalTrade()

	WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.StartAcceptCooldown(
		6
	)
end

WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.AddOverlayClickTarget = function(parent, itemId, itemType)
	if not parent
		or not parent:IsA(
			"GuiObject"
		) then

		return
	end

	local oldOverlay =
		parent:FindFirstChild(
			"ClientPlaceholderTradeToggle"
		)

	if oldOverlay then
		oldOverlay:Destroy()
	end

	local overlay =
		Instance.new(
			"TextButton"
		)

	overlay.Name =
		"ClientPlaceholderTradeToggle"

	overlay.BackgroundTransparency =
		1

	overlay.BorderSizePixel =
		0

	overlay.Text =
		""

	overlay.Size =
		UDim2.fromScale(
			1,
			1
		)

	overlay.Position =
		UDim2.fromScale(
			0,
			0
		)

	overlay.ZIndex =
		parent.ZIndex + 100

	overlay.Parent =
		parent

	overlay.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.ToggleLocalOffer(
			itemId,
			itemType
		)
	end)
end

WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.AddActionOverlay = function(parent, callback)
	if not parent
		or not parent:IsA(
			"GuiObject"
		) then

		return
	end

	local oldOverlay =
		parent:FindFirstChild(
			"ClientPlaceholderTradeActionOverlay"
		)

	if oldOverlay then
		oldOverlay:Destroy()
	end

	local overlay =
		Instance.new(
			"TextButton"
		)

	overlay.Name =
		"ClientPlaceholderTradeActionOverlay"

	overlay.BackgroundTransparency =
		1

	overlay.BorderSizePixel =
		0

	overlay.Text =
		""

	overlay.Size =
		UDim2.fromScale(
			1,
			1
		)

	overlay.Position =
		UDim2.fromScale(
			0,
			0
		)

	overlay.ZIndex =
		parent.ZIndex + 100

	overlay.Parent =
		parent

	overlay.MouseButton1Click:Connect(
		callback
	)
end

WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.ScheduleOtherSideAccept = function()
	WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.OtherAcceptSequence += 1

	local sequence =
		WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.OtherAcceptSequence

	if WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.OtherAccepted then
		WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.SetAcceptState(
			"BothAccepted"
		)

		task.delay(
			1,
			function()
				if sequence
						~= WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.OtherAcceptSequence
					or WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.LocalAcceptMode
						~= "BothAccepted"
					or not WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.Active then

					return
				end

				WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.CompleteTrade()
			end
		)

		return
	end

	-- Fallback only: if another code path reaches this before their
	-- accepted state exists, keep the old one-second fake accept behavior.
	task.delay(
		1,
		function()
			if sequence
					~= WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.OtherAcceptSequence
				or WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.LocalAcceptMode
					~= "Waiting"
				or not WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.Active then

				return
			end

			WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.OtherAccepted =
				true

			local tradeModule =
				WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.TradeModule

			if tradeModule
				and tradeModule.GUI
				and tradeModule.GUI.TheirOffer
				and tradeModule.GUI.TheirOffer:FindFirstChild(
					"Accepted"
				) then

				tradeModule.GUI.TheirOffer.Accepted.Visible =
					true
			end

			WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.SetAcceptState(
				"BothAccepted"
			)

			task.delay(
				1,
				function()
					if sequence
							~= WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.OtherAcceptSequence
						or WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.LocalAcceptMode
							~= "BothAccepted"
						or not WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.Active then

						return
					end

					WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.CompleteTrade()
				end
			)
		end
	)
end

WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.InstallTradeActionButtons = function()
	local tradeModule =
		WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.TradeModule

	local actions =
		tradeModule
		and tradeModule.GUI
		and tradeModule.GUI.Actions

	if not actions then
		return
	end

	local accept =
		actions:FindFirstChild(
			"Accept"
		)

	local decline =
		actions:FindFirstChild(
			"Decline"
		)

	if accept then
		WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.AddActionOverlay(
			accept,
			WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.HandleAcceptClick
		)

		WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.AddActionOverlay(
			accept:FindFirstChild(
				"ActionButton"
			),
			WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.HandleAcceptClick
		)

		local confirmButton =
			accept:FindFirstChild(
				"Confirm"
			)
			and accept.Confirm:FindFirstChild(
				"ActionButton"
			)

		WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.AddActionOverlay(
			confirmButton,
			function()
				if WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.LocalAcceptMode
						== "Confirm"
					and time()
						- WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.LocalConfirmStartedAt
						>= 0.4 then

					WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.SetAcceptState(
						"Waiting"
					)

					WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.ScheduleOtherSideAccept()
				end
			end
		)

		local cancelButton =
			accept:FindFirstChild(
				"Cancel"
			)
			and accept.Cancel:FindFirstChild(
				"ActionButton"
			)

		WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.AddActionOverlay(
			cancelButton,
			WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.ResetAcceptUi
		)
	end

	if decline then
		WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.AddActionOverlay(
			decline:FindFirstChild(
				"ActionButton"
			),
			function()
				WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.Close(
					true
				)

				if WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.Enabled then
					WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.ScheduleNextTrade()
				end
			end
		)
	end
end

WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.InstallInventoryToggleButtons = function()
	local tradeModule =
		WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.TradeModule

	local tradeInventory =
		tradeModule
		and tradeModule.TradeInventory

	if not tradeInventory
		or not tradeInventory.Data then

		return
	end

	for itemType, categories in pairs(
		tradeInventory.Data
	) do
		for _, items in pairs(
			categories
		) do
			for itemId, entry in pairs(
				items
			) do
				local frame =
					entry.Frame

				local actionButton =
					frame
					and frame:FindFirstChild(
						"Container"
					)
					and frame.Container:FindFirstChild(
						"ActionButton"
					)

				if actionButton
					and actionButton:IsA(
						"GuiObject"
					) then

					WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.AddOverlayClickTarget(
						actionButton,
						itemId,
						itemType
					)

				elseif frame
					and frame:IsA(
						"GuiObject"
					) then

					WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.AddOverlayClickTarget(
						frame,
						itemId,
						itemType
					)
				end
			end
		end
	end
end

WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.InstallSearchFilter = function()
	if WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.SearchConnection then
		pcall(function()
			WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.SearchConnection:Disconnect()
		end)

		WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.SearchConnection =
			nil
	end

	local tradeModule =
		WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.TradeModule

	local searchText =
		tradeModule
		and tradeModule.GUI
		and tradeModule.GUI.TradeGUI
		and tradeModule.GUI.TradeGUI:FindFirstChild(
			"Container"
		)
		and tradeModule.GUI.TradeGUI.Container:FindFirstChild(
			"Items"
		)
		and tradeModule.GUI.TradeGUI.Container.Items:FindFirstChild(
			"Tabs"
		)
		and tradeModule.GUI.TradeGUI.Container.Items.Tabs:FindFirstChild(
			"Search"
		)
		and tradeModule.GUI.TradeGUI.Container.Items.Tabs.Search:FindFirstChild(
			"Container"
		)
		and tradeModule.GUI.TradeGUI.Container.Items.Tabs.Search.Container:FindFirstChild(
			"SearchText"
		)

	if not searchText
		or not searchText:IsA(
			"TextBox"
		) then

		return
	end

	local function applyFilter()
		local inventory =
			tradeModule.TradeInventory

		if not inventory
			or not inventory.Data then

			return
		end

		local query =
			tostring(
				searchText.Text or ""
			):lower()

		for _, itemTypeData in pairs(
			inventory.Data
		) do
			for _, entry in pairs(
				itemTypeData.Current or {}
			) do
				if entry.Frame then
					local itemName =
						tostring(
							entry.Name or ""
						):lower()

					entry.Frame.Visible =
						query == ""
						or itemName:find(
							query,
							1,
							true
						) ~= nil
				end
			end
		end
	end

	WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.SearchConnection =
		searchText:GetPropertyChangedSignal(
			"Text"
		):Connect(
			applyFilter
		)

	applyFilter()
end

WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.EnforceFrame = function()
	if not WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.Active then
		return
	end

	local tradeModule =
		WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.TradeModule

	local tradeGui =
		tradeModule
		and tradeModule.GUI
		and tradeModule.GUI.TradeGUI

	if not tradeGui then
		return
	end

	if not tradeGui.Enabled then
		tradeGui.Enabled =
			true
	end

	if tradeGui:FindFirstChild(
		"Container"
	) then
		tradeGui.Container.Visible =
			true
	end

	if WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.LocalAcceptMode
			== "Waiting"
		or WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.LocalAcceptMode
			== "BothAccepted" then

		return
	end

	WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.DrawOfferSlots(
		tradeModule.GUI.YourOffer,
		WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.LocalOffer
	)

	WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.DrawOfferSlots(
		tradeModule.GUI.TheirOffer,
		WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.TheirOffer
	)

	if tradeModule.GUI.TheirOffer
		and tradeModule.GUI.TheirOffer:FindFirstChild(
			"Username"
		) then

		tradeModule.GUI.TheirOffer.Username.Text =
			"("
			.. tostring(
				WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.CurrentFakeName
			)
			.. ")"
	end

	WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.InstallOfferRemoveButtons()

	if WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.LocalAcceptMode
		== "Accept" then

		WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.WakeAcceptActionButton()
	else
		WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.SetAcceptContainerOverlayVisible(
			false
		)
	end
end

WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.StartGuard = function()
	WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.GuardSequence += 1

	local sequence =
		WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.GuardSequence

	for _, connection in ipairs(
		WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.GuardConnections
	) do
		pcall(function()
			connection:Disconnect()
		end)
	end

	table.clear(
		WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.GuardConnections
	)

	local tradeModule =
		WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.TradeModule

	local tradeGui =
		tradeModule
		and tradeModule.GUI
		and tradeModule.GUI.TradeGUI

	if tradeGui then
		table.insert(
			WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.GuardConnections,
			tradeGui:GetPropertyChangedSignal(
				"Enabled"
			):Connect(function()
				if WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.Active then
					task.defer(
						WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.EnforceFrame
					)
				end
			end)
		)

		if tradeGui:FindFirstChild(
			"Container"
		) then
			table.insert(
				WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.GuardConnections,
				tradeGui.Container:GetPropertyChangedSignal(
					"Visible"
				):Connect(function()
					if WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.Active then
						task.defer(
							WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.EnforceFrame
						)
					end
				end)
			)
		end
	end

	task.spawn(function()
		while WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.Active
			and sequence
				== WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.GuardSequence do

			WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.EnforceFrame()

			task.wait(
				0.08
			)
		end
	end)
end

WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.StopGuard = function()
	WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.GuardSequence += 1

	for _, connection in ipairs(
		WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.GuardConnections
	) do
		pcall(function()
			connection:Disconnect()
		end)
	end

	table.clear(
		WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.GuardConnections
	)

	if WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.SearchConnection then
		pcall(function()
			WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.SearchConnection:Disconnect()
		end)

		WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.SearchConnection =
			nil
	end
end

WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.ScheduleNextTrade = function()
	WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.ReopenSequence =
		(WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.ReopenSequence or 0)
		+ 1

	local sequence =
		WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.ReopenSequence

	task.delay(
		2,
		function()
			if sequence
					~= WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.ReopenSequence
				or not WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.Enabled
				or WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.Active
				or not WeaponPackRuntime.ControllerUI
				or not WeaponPackRuntime.ControllerUI.AutoTradeButton
				or not WeaponPackRuntime.ControllerUI.AutoTradeButton.Parent then

				return
			end

			WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.Open()
		end
	)
end

WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.Close = function(restoreOffer)
	if not WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.ModulesLoaded then
		return
	end

	local tradeModule =
		WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.TradeModule

	local tradeGui =
		tradeModule
		and tradeModule.GUI
		and tradeModule.GUI.TradeGUI

	if restoreOffer then
		WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.RestoreLocalOffer()
	end

	WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.Active =
		false

	WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.OtherAcceptSequence += 1
	WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.CooldownSequence += 1

	WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.StopGuard()

	if tradeGui then
		tradeGui.Enabled =
			false
	end

	WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.CleanupOverlays()

	if tradeModule then
		tradeModule.TradeInventory =
			nil
	end

	table.clear(
		WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.LocalOffer
	)

	table.clear(
		WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.TheirOffer
	)

	WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.LocalAcceptMode =
		"Accept"

	WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.LocalConfirmStartedAt =
		0

	WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.OtherAccepted =
		false

	WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.ClearOfferSlots(
		tradeModule
		and tradeModule.GUI
		and tradeModule.GUI.YourOffer
	)

	WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.ClearOfferSlots(
		tradeModule
		and tradeModule.GUI
		and tradeModule.GUI.TheirOffer
	)
end

WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.CompleteTrade = function()
	if not WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.Active then
		return
	end

	-- Completed fake trade: keep AUTO TRADE ON and reopen after 2 seconds.
	WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.Close(
		false
	)

	if WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner
		and WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner.RefreshMainInventoryNow then

		task.defer(
			WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner.RefreshMainInventoryNow
		)
	end

	WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.ScheduleNextTrade()
end

WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.Open = function()
	if WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.Active then
		return true
	end

	if not WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.LoadModules() then
		return false
	end

	local tradeModule =
		WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.TradeModule

	local inventoryModule =
		WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.InventoryModule

	local profileData =
		WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.ProfileData

	local tradeGui =
		tradeModule
		and tradeModule.GUI
		and tradeModule.GUI.TradeGUI

	local tradeContainer =
		tradeGui
		and tradeGui:FindFirstChild(
			"Container"
		)

	if not tradeGui
		or not tradeContainer
		or not tradeContainer:FindFirstChild(
			"Items"
		)
		or not tradeContainer.Items:FindFirstChild(
			"Main"
		) then

		warn(
			"[BAP Trade] MM2 trade GUI is not ready."
		)

		return false
	end

	WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.CleanupOverlays()

	WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.Active =
		true

	WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.PickFakeName()

	WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.RestoreLocalOffer()

	table.clear(
		WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.TheirOffer
	)

	WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.ResetAcceptUi()

	for _, categoryName in ipairs({
		"Weapons",
		"Pets",
	}) do
		local category =
			tradeContainer.Items.Main:FindFirstChild(
				categoryName
			)

		if category
			and category:FindFirstChild(
				"Items"
			)
			and category.Items:FindFirstChild(
				"Container"
			) then

			for _, section in ipairs(
				category.Items.Container:GetChildren()
			) do
				local container =
					section:FindFirstChild(
						"Container"
					)

				if container then
					container:ClearAllChildren()
				end
			end
		end
	end

	local okInventory, generatedInventory =
		pcall(function()
			return inventoryModule.GenerateInventory(
				tradeContainer.Items,
				profileData,
				"Trading",
				tradeModule.GUI.ItemsLayout
			)
		end)

	if not okInventory
		or type(generatedInventory) ~= "table" then

		WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.Active =
			false

		warn(
			"[BAP Trade] Could not generate MM2 trade inventory: "
			.. tostring(
				generatedInventory
			)
		)

		return false
	end

	tradeModule.TradeInventory =
		generatedInventory

	pcall(function()
		tradeModule.ConnectOfferButtons(
			tradeModule.TradeInventory
		)
	end)

	WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.InstallSearchFilter()

	WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.RedrawLocalTrade()

	if tradeModule.GUI.TheirOffer
		and tradeModule.GUI.TheirOffer:FindFirstChild(
			"Username"
		) then

		tradeModule.GUI.TheirOffer.Username.Text =
			"("
			.. tostring(
				WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.CurrentFakeName
			)
			.. ")"
	end

	tradeGui.Enabled =
		true

	task.defer(
		WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.InstallInventoryToggleButtons
	)

	task.delay(
		0.5,
		WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.InstallInventoryToggleButtons
	)

	WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.InstallTradeActionButtons()

	WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.WakeAcceptActionButton()

	WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.EnforceFrame()

	task.defer(
		WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.WakeAcceptActionButton
	)

	task.defer(
		WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.EnforceFrame
	)

	WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.StartGuard()

	return true
end

WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.SetEnabled = function(enabled)
	WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.Enabled =
		enabled == true

	if not WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.Enabled then
		WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.ReopenSequence =
			(WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.ReopenSequence or 0)
			+ 1
	end

	WeaponPackRuntime.ControllerUI.AutoTradeEnabled =
		WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.Enabled

	WeaponPackRuntime.ControllerUI.AutoTradeButton.Text =
		WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.Enabled
		and "ON"
		or "OFF"

	if WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.Enabled then
		task.defer(function()
			if WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.Enabled then
				WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.Open()
			end
		end)

		return
	end

	if WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.Active then
		WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.Close(
			true
		)
	end
end

WeaponPackRuntime.ControllerUI.AutoTradeButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
	WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.SetEnabled(
		not WeaponPackRuntime.FakeTrade.Enabled
	)
end)

WeaponPackRuntime.ControllerUI.RealisticSpawnerButton = createButton(
	"REALISTIC SPAWNER",
	UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 48),
	UDim2.new(0, 300, 0, 56),
	WeaponPackRuntime.ControllerUI.SpawnerTradePage
)
WeaponPackRuntime.ControllerUI.RealisticSpawnerButton.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0)
WeaponPackRuntime.ControllerUI.RealisticSpawnerButton.TextSize = 16
WeaponPackRuntime.ControllerUI.AutoTradeButton.TextSize = 16

--------------------------------------------------
-- REALISTIC SPAWNER
-- Uses MM2's normal inventory UI and client-side fake inventory amounts.
-- Each supported weapon gets its configured realistic amount.
--------------------------------------------------

WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner = WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner or {}
WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner.FakeInventoryAmounts =
	WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner.FakeInventoryAmounts or {}

WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner.GetProfileOwnedTable = function(itemType)
	if itemType == "Weapons" or itemType == "Item" then
		return WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner.ProfileData
			and WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner.ProfileData.Weapons
			and WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner.ProfileData.Weapons.Owned
	elseif itemType == "Pets" then
		return WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner.ProfileData
			and WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner.ProfileData.Pets
			and WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner.ProfileData.Pets.Owned
	end

	local bucket =
		WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner.ProfileData
		and WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner.ProfileData[itemType]

	return bucket and bucket.Owned
end

WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner.GetFakeInventoryKey = function(itemId, itemType)
	local bucket =
		itemType == "Item"
		and "Weapons"
		or tostring(itemType)

	return bucket .. ":" .. tostring(itemId)
end

WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner.GetFakeInventoryAmount = function(itemId, itemType)
	return tonumber(
		WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner.FakeInventoryAmounts[
			WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner.GetFakeInventoryKey(
				itemId,
				itemType
			)
		]
	) or 0
end

WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner.SetFakeInventoryAmount = function(itemId, itemType, amount)
	local key =
		WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner.GetFakeInventoryKey(
			itemId,
			itemType
		)

	local owned =
		WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner.GetProfileOwnedTable(
			itemType
		)

	amount =
		math.max(
			0,
			math.floor(
				tonumber(amount) or 0
			)
		)

	if amount > 0 then
		WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner.FakeInventoryAmounts[
			key
		] = amount

		if owned then
			owned[itemId] = amount
		end
	else
		WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner.FakeInventoryAmounts[
			key
		] = nil

		if owned then
			owned[itemId] = nil
		end
	end

	return amount
end

WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner.AddFakeInventoryAmount = function(itemId, itemType, delta)
	return WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner.SetFakeInventoryAmount(
		itemId,
		itemType,
		WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner.GetFakeInventoryAmount(
			itemId,
			itemType
		) + (tonumber(delta) or 0)
	)
end

WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner.ClearMainInventoryContainers = function()
	local inventoryModule =
		WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner.InventoryModule

	local inventoryGui =
		inventoryModule
		and inventoryModule.GUI
		and inventoryModule.GUI.MyInventory

	if not inventoryGui
		or not inventoryGui.Main then

		return false
	end

	local okBlank, blank =
		pcall(function()
			return inventoryModule.CreateBlankInventoryTable()
		end)

	if not okBlank
		or type(blank) ~= "table" then

		return false
	end

	for itemType, categories in pairs(blank) do
		local typeFrame =
			inventoryGui.Main:FindFirstChild(
				itemType
			)

		local itemsContainer =
			typeFrame
			and typeFrame:FindFirstChild("Items")
			and typeFrame.Items:FindFirstChild("Container")

		if itemsContainer then
			for categoryName in pairs(categories) do
				local categoryFrame =
					itemsContainer:FindFirstChild(
						categoryName
					)

				if not categoryFrame
					and itemsContainer:FindFirstChild("Holiday") then

					local holiday =
						itemsContainer.Holiday:FindFirstChild(
							"Container"
						)

					categoryFrame =
						holiday
						and holiday:FindFirstChild(
							categoryName
						)
				end

				local container =
					categoryFrame
					and categoryFrame:FindFirstChild(
						"Container"
					)

				if container then
					container:ClearAllChildren()
				end
			end
		end
	end

	return true
end

WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner.RefreshMainInventoryNow = function()
	local inventoryModule =
		WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner.InventoryModule

	local profileData =
		WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner.ProfileData

	local inventoryGui =
		inventoryModule
		and inventoryModule.GUI
		and inventoryModule.GUI.MyInventory

	if not inventoryGui
		or not inventoryGui.Main then

		return false
	end

	if not WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner.ClearMainInventoryContainers() then
		return false
	end

	local ok, newInventory =
		pcall(function()
			return inventoryModule.GenerateInventory(
				inventoryGui,
				profileData
			)
		end)

	if not ok then
		warn(
			"[BAP Spawner] Main inventory refresh failed: "
			.. tostring(newInventory)
		)

		return false
	end

	inventoryModule.MyInventory =
		newInventory

	pcall(function()
		inventoryModule.ConnectEquipButtons()
	end)

	pcall(function()
		inventoryModule.UpdateMyEquip()
	end)

	return true
end

WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner.LoadModules = function()
	if WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner.ModulesLoaded then
		return true
	end

	local modules =
		ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild(
			"Modules"
		)

	local database =
		ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild(
			"Database"
		)

	if not modules
		or not database then

		return false
	end

	local okInventory, inventoryModule =
		pcall(function()
			return require(
				modules:WaitForChild(
					"InventoryModule",
					5
				)
			)
		end)

	local okProfile, profileData =
		pcall(function()
			return require(
				modules:WaitForChild(
					"ProfileData",
					5
				)
			)
		end)

	local okSync, sync =
		pcall(function()
			return require(
				database:WaitForChild(
					"Sync",
					5
				)
			)
		end)

	if not okInventory
		or type(inventoryModule) ~= "table"
		or not okProfile
		or type(profileData) ~= "table"
		or not okSync
		or type(sync) ~= "table" then

		warn(
			"[BAP Spawner] Could not load MM2 inventory modules."
		)

		return false
	end

	WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner.InventoryModule =
		inventoryModule

	WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner.ProfileData =
		profileData

	WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner.Sync =
		sync

	WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner.ModulesLoaded =
		true

	return true
end

WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner.IsSpawnerRarity = function(data)
	return type(data) == "table"
		and (
			data.Rarity == "Godly"
			or data.Rarity == "Ancient"
		)
end

-- Keep the realistic spawner from adding the unwanted/placeholder cards.
-- The Evo weapons shown by the user are specifically their GODLY variants;
-- Ancient/other variants with the same display name are intentionally kept.
WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner.NormalizeItemText = function(value)
	return tostring(value or "")
		:lower()
		:gsub("[^%w%?]", "")
end

WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner.IsExcludedItem = function(itemId, data)
	if type(data) ~= "table" then
		return false
	end

	local idText =
		WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner.NormalizeItemText(
			itemId
		)

	local nameText =
		WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner.NormalizeItemText(
			data.ItemName
			or data.Name
			or ""
		)

	-- Keep the two black placeholder ??? cards out.
	if idText == "???"
		or nameText == "???"
		or idText == "chroma???"
		or nameText == "chroma???" then

		return true
	end

	-- Keep Nik's Scythe out.
	if idText == "niksscythe"
		or nameText == "niksscythe" then

		return true
	end

	-- Keep the same pictured Godly/Evo variants deleted as before.
	-- Other variants with the same display name are still allowed.
	if data.Rarity == "Godly" then
		if nameText == "synthwave"
			or nameText == "reaver"
			or nameText == "icecrusher"
			or nameText == "gingerscythe" then

			return true
		end
	end

	return false
end

WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner.IsChromaItem = function(itemId, data)
	if type(data) ~= "table" then
		return false
	end

	if data.Chroma == true
		or data.IsChroma == true then

		return true
	end

	local idText =
		WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner.NormalizeItemText(
			itemId
		)

	local nameText =
		WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner.NormalizeItemText(
			data.ItemName
			or data.Name
			or data.DisplayName
			or ""
		)

	return string.sub(idText, 1, 6) == "chroma"
		or string.sub(nameText, 1, 6) == "chroma"
end

WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner.MatchesItemName = function(itemId, data, aliases)
	if type(data) ~= "table" then
		return false
	end

	local candidates = {
		itemId,
		data.ItemName,
		data.Name,
		data.DisplayName,
		data.WeaponName,
	}

	for _, candidate in ipairs(candidates) do
		local normalized =
			WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner.NormalizeItemText(
				candidate
			)

		-- Chroma records often share the same display ItemName as the
		-- normal weapon, but this also handles names with "Chroma" prefixed.
		if string.sub(normalized, 1, 6) == "chroma" then
			normalized =
				string.sub(
					normalized,
					7
				)
		end

		for _, alias in ipairs(aliases) do
			if normalized == alias then
				return true
			end
		end
	end

	return false
end

WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner.GetSpawnAmount = function(itemId, data)
	local chroma =
		WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner.IsChromaItem(
			itemId,
			data
		)

	local matches =
		WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner.MatchesItemName

	-- The specifically deleted Godly/Evo variants are filtered above.
	-- Whatever variants of these four remain in the list should be exactly 1.
	if matches(itemId, data, {"synthwave"})
		or matches(itemId, data, {"gingerscythe"})
		or matches(itemId, data, {"icecrusher"})
		or matches(itemId, data, {"reaver"}) then

		return 1
	end

	if matches(
		itemId,
		data,
		{
			"travelersgun",
			"travellersgun",
			"travelergun",
			"travellergun",
		}
	) then
		if chroma then
			return 3
		end

		return math.random(25, 38)
	end

	if matches(
		itemId,
		data,
		{
			"vampiregun",
			"vampiresgun",
		}
	) then
		if chroma then
			return math.random(24, 33)
		end

		return math.random(48, 63)
	end

	if matches(itemId, data, {"evergreen"}) then
		if chroma then
			return math.random(4, 7)
		end

		return math.random(43, 52)
	end

	if matches(itemId, data, {"evergun"}) then
		if chroma then
			return math.random(4, 7)
		end

		return math.random(42, 55)
	end

	if matches(
		itemId,
		data,
		{
			"gingerscope",
			"gingerscope",
		}
	) then

		return math.random(42, 53)
	end

	if chroma
		and matches(itemId, data, {"sunrise"}) then

		return math.random(52, 56)
	end

	if chroma
		and matches(itemId, data, {"sunset"}) then

		return math.random(52, 56)
	end

	if chroma
		and matches(
			itemId,
			data,
			{
				"raygun",
				"raygun",
			}
		) then

		return math.random(37, 44)
	end

	if chroma
		and matches(itemId, data, {"alienbeam"}) then

		return math.random(36, 43)
	end

	if chroma
		and matches(
			itemId,
			data,
			{
				"heartwand",
			}
		) then

		return math.random(56, 67)
	end

	if chroma
		and matches(itemId, data, {"sands"}) then

		return math.random(74, 87)
	end

	if chroma
		and matches(itemId, data, {"beachy"}) then

		return math.random(73, 86)
	end

	if chroma
		and matches(itemId, data, {"constellation"}) then

		return math.random(37, 44)
	end

	-- Everything else.
	return math.random(78, 127)
end

WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner.PurgeExcludedTrackedItems = function()
	local sync =
		WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner.Sync

	if type(sync) ~= "table" then
		return false
	end

	local changed = false

	for key, fakeAmount in pairs(
		WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner.FakeInventoryAmounts
	) do
		local itemType, itemId =
			string.match(
				tostring(key),
				"^([^:]+):(.+)$"
			)

		local data =
			itemId
			and (
				(type(sync.Weapons) == "table" and sync.Weapons[itemId])
				or (type(sync.Item) == "table" and sync.Item[itemId])
			)

		if itemId
			and WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner.IsExcludedItem(
				itemId,
				data
			) then

			WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner.FakeInventoryAmounts[key] = nil

			local owned =
				WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner.GetProfileOwnedTable(
					itemType
				)

			-- Only clear the client entry when it still equals the amount
			-- this spawner itself injected. No server inventory is touched.
			if owned
				and tonumber(owned[itemId]) == tonumber(fakeAmount) then

				owned[itemId] = nil
			end

			changed = true
		end
	end

	return changed
end

WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner.SpawnAll = function()
	if not WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner.LoadModules() then
		return 0
	end

	local sync =
		WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner.Sync

	WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner.PurgeExcludedTrackedItems()

	local spawnedCount = 0
	local seen = {}

	local function collect(container, itemType)
		for itemId, data in pairs(container or {}) do
			if WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner.IsSpawnerRarity(data)
				and not WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner.IsExcludedItem(
					itemId,
					data
				)
				and not seen[itemId] then

				seen[itemId] = true

				if WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner.GetProfileOwnedTable(
					itemType
				) then
					local amount =
						WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner.GetSpawnAmount(
							itemId,
							data
						)

					WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner.SetFakeInventoryAmount(
						itemId,
						itemType,
						amount
					)

					spawnedCount += 1
				end
			end
		end
	end

	collect(
		sync.Weapons,
		"Weapons"
	)

	collect(
		sync.Item,
		"Item"
	)

	WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner.RefreshMainInventoryNow()

	print(
		("[BAP Spawner] Assigned realistic amounts to %d weapons"):format(
			spawnedCount
		)
	)

	return spawnedCount
end

WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner.WatchId =
	(WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner.WatchId or 0) + 1

WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner.StartWatcher = function()
	local watchId =
		WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner.WatchId

	task.spawn(function()
		while WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner
			and WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner.WatchId == watchId do

			if WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner.ModulesLoaded then
				local corrected = false

				for key, amount in pairs(
					WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner.FakeInventoryAmounts
				) do
					local itemType, itemId =
						string.match(
							key,
							"^([^:]+):(.+)$"
						)

					local owned =
						itemType
						and WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner.GetProfileOwnedTable(
							itemType
						)

					if owned
						and tonumber(owned[itemId]) ~= tonumber(amount) then

						owned[itemId] = amount
						corrected = true
					end
				end

				if corrected then
					WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner.RefreshMainInventoryNow()
				end
			end

			task.wait(0.35)
		end
	end)
end

WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner.StartWatcher()

WeaponPackRuntime.ControllerUI.RealisticSpawnerButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
	WeaponPackRuntime.ControllerUI.RealisticSpawnerButton.Text =
		"SPAWNING..."

	local count =
		WeaponPackRuntime.RealisticSpawner.SpawnAll()

	if count > 0 then
		WeaponPackRuntime.ControllerUI.RealisticSpawnerButton.Text =
			"SPAWNED "
			.. tostring(count)
			.. " ITEMS"
	else
		WeaponPackRuntime.ControllerUI.RealisticSpawnerButton.Text =
			"SPAWNER FAILED"
	end

	task.delay(2, function()
		if WeaponPackRuntime.ControllerUI
			and WeaponPackRuntime.ControllerUI.RealisticSpawnerButton
			and WeaponPackRuntime.ControllerUI.RealisticSpawnerButton.Parent then

			WeaponPackRuntime.ControllerUI.RealisticSpawnerButton.Text =
				"REALISTIC SPAWNER"
		end
	end)
end)

WeaponPackRuntime.ControllerUI.ApplyScale = function(value)
    value = math.clamp(value, 0.70, 1.30)
    WeaponPackRuntime.ControllerUI.ScaleValue = value
    WeaponPackRuntime.ControllerUI.Scale.Scale = value
end

WeaponPackRuntime.ControllerUI.Minus.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    WeaponPackRuntime.ControllerUI.ApplyScale(WeaponPackRuntime.ControllerUI.ScaleValue - 0.10)
end)

WeaponPackRuntime.ControllerUI.Plus.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    WeaponPackRuntime.ControllerUI.ApplyScale(WeaponPackRuntime.ControllerUI.ScaleValue + 0.10)
end)

WeaponPackRuntime.ControllerUI.SetCollapsed = function(collapsed)
    WeaponPackRuntime.ControllerUI.Collapsed = collapsed
    if collapsed then
        WeaponPackRuntime.ControllerUI.LastTab = WeaponPackRuntime.ControllerUI.TradePage.Visible and "TRADE" or (WeaponPackRuntime.ControllerUI.SpawnerPage.Visible and "SPAWNER" or (WeaponPackRuntime.ControllerUI.SpawnerTradePage.Visible and "SPAWNER_TRADE" or (weaponsPage.Visible and "WEAPONS" or "BOTS")))
        botsTabButton.Visible = false
        weaponsTabButton.Visible = false
        WeaponPackRuntime.ControllerUI.SpawnerTradeTabButton.Visible = false
        botsPage.Visible = false
        weaponsPage.Visible = false
        WeaponPackRuntime.ControllerUI.SpawnerTradePage.Visible = false
        WeaponPackRuntime.ControllerUI.SpawnerPage.Visible = false
        WeaponPackRuntime.ControllerUI.TradePage.Visible = false
        leftThorn.Visible = false
        rightThorn.Visible = false
        main.Size = UDim2.new(0, 360, 0, 88)
        WeaponPackRuntime.ControllerUI.Close.Text = "□"
    else
        main.Size = UDim2.new(0, 360, 0, 540)
        botsTabButton.Visible = true
        weaponsTabButton.Visible = true
        WeaponPackRuntime.ControllerUI.SpawnerTradeTabButton.Visible = true
        leftThorn.Visible = true
        rightThorn.Visible = true
        setActiveTab(WeaponPackRuntime.ControllerUI.LastTab)
        WeaponPackRuntime.ControllerUI.Close.Text = "X"
    end
end

WeaponPackRuntime.ControllerUI.Close.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    WeaponPackRuntime.ControllerUI.SetCollapsed(not WeaponPackRuntime.ControllerUI.Collapsed)
end)

local weaponButtons = {}

-- Forward declaration is required because the weapon-list buttons are
-- created before the equip function's implementation appears below.
local equipPlayerWeapon

local function rebuildWeaponButtons()
	for _, button in ipairs(weaponButtons) do
		button:Destroy()
	end

	table.clear(weaponButtons)

	for _, weaponName in ipairs(WEAPON_LIST) do
		local weaponType =
			WeaponPackRuntime.WEAPON_TYPE_HINTS[
				weaponName
			]
			or (
				weaponName == "Harvester"
				and "Gun"
				or "Knife"
			)

		if not WeaponPackRuntime.PLAYER_WEAPON_EQUIP_DISABLED[
			weaponName
		]
			and weaponType
			== WeaponPackRuntime.SelectedWeaponCategory then

			local button = createButton(
				weaponName,
				UDim2.new(),
				UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 32),
				weaponListFrame
			)

			button.TextSize = 13

			button.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
				weaponBox.Text = weaponName

				weaponStatus.Text =
					"Loading " ..
					weaponName ..
					"..."

				weaponStatus.TextColor3 =
					Color3.fromRGB(
						200,
						200,
						210
					)

				if not equipPlayerWeapon then
					weaponStatus.Text =
						"Weapon equip function not ready"

					weaponStatus.TextColor3 =
						Color3.fromRGB(
							255,
							105,
							105
						)

					return
				end

				local ok, err =
					pcall(function()
						equipPlayerWeapon(
							weaponName
						)
					end)

				if not ok then
					weaponStatus.Text =
						"Equip error: " ..
						tostring(err)

					weaponStatus.TextColor3 =
						Color3.fromRGB(
							255,
							105,
							105
						)
				end
			end)

			table.insert(
				weaponButtons,
				button
			)
		end
	end

	local gunActive =
		WeaponPackRuntime.SelectedWeaponCategory
		== "Gun"

	WeaponPackRuntime.GunTabButton.BackgroundColor3 =
		gunActive
		and Color3.fromRGB(65, 65, 80)
		or Color3.fromRGB(45, 45, 55)

	WeaponPackRuntime.KnifeTabButton.BackgroundColor3 =
		(not gunActive)
		and Color3.fromRGB(65, 65, 80)
		or Color3.fromRGB(45, 45, 55)

	weaponListTitle.Text =
		gunActive
		and "GUNS"
		or "KNIVES"

	weaponListFrame.CanvasPosition =
		Vector2.new(0, 0)
end

WeaponPackRuntime.GunTabButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
	WeaponPackRuntime.SelectedWeaponCategory =
		"Gun"

	rebuildWeaponButtons()
end)

WeaponPackRuntime.KnifeTabButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
	WeaponPackRuntime.SelectedWeaponCategory =
		"Knife"

	rebuildWeaponButtons()
end)


--------------------------------------------------
-- HOLSTERED WEAPON VISUALS
--------------------------------------------------

local function clearHolsteredWeapon(character, modelName)
	if not character then
		return
	end

	local wantedModelName =
		modelName or "HolsteredWeapon"

	local old =
		character:FindFirstChild(
			wantedModelName
		)

	if old then
		old:Destroy()
	end
end

local function getHolsterTorso(character)
	return character
		and (
			character:FindFirstChild("LowerTorso")
			or character:FindFirstChild("Torso")
			or character:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
		)
end

local function prepareWeaponVisual(
	source,
	weaponName,
	modelName
)
	if not source then
		return nil
	end

	local model = Instance.new("Model")

	model.Name =
		modelName
		or "HolsteredWeapon"

	model:SetAttribute(
		"WeaponName",
		weaponName
	)

	if source:IsA("Tool") or source:IsA("Model") then
		for _, child in ipairs(source:GetChildren()) do
			child.Parent = model
		end
		source:Destroy()

	elseif source:IsA("BasePart") then
		source.Parent = model
	else
		source:Destroy()
		model:Destroy()
		return nil
	end

	local root =
		model:FindFirstChild("Handle", true)
		or model:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)

	if not root then
		model:Destroy()
		return nil
	end

	root.Name = "Handle"
	model.PrimaryPart = root

	for _, obj in ipairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
		if obj:IsA("BasePart") then
			obj.Anchored = false
			obj.CanCollide = false
			obj.CanTouch = false
			obj.CanQuery = false
			obj.Massless = true
		end
	end

	-- Keep every visual part connected to the Handle.
	for _, obj in ipairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
		if obj:IsA("BasePart") and obj ~= root then
			local weld = Instance.new("WeldConstraint")
			weld.Part0 = root
			weld.Part1 = obj
			weld.Parent = root
		end
	end

	return model
end

-- Gingerscope uses a UGC fallback mesh whose local axes are different
-- from MM2 Harvester/Icepiercer. Keep its correction separate so tuning it
-- never changes the working guns.
local GINGERSCOPE_HIP_OFFSET =
	-- Rotation is already correct. This offset only moves the gun
	-- off the middle of the avatar and onto the right hip.
	CFrame.new(0.72, 0.02, 0.28)
	* CFrame.Angles(
		math.rad(90),
		math.rad(0),
		math.rad(90)
	)

-- IMPORTANT: every Gingerscope visual path must use THIS function.
-- This prevents the gun from jumping to a different orientation when
-- MM2 deletes the round-owned gun and the persistent backup takes over.
local function getGingerscopeWorldCFrame(targetWorldCFrame)
	return targetWorldCFrame * GINGERSCOPE_HIP_OFFSET
end

local WEAPON_HOLSTER_DATA = {
	["Harvester"] = {
		Placement = "WaistRight",

		-- Exact attachment transform from the weapon visual data.
		AttachmentPosition = Vector3.new(
			0.1299028992652893,
			0.000002229222218375071,
			0.07500199228525162
		),

		AttachmentOrientation = Vector3.new(
			-0.0026997302193194628,
			-60,
			90.00231170654297
		),

		-- Small final correction so it hugs the hip instead of crossing the torso.
		Correction = CFrame.new(0.10, -0.16, 0.02)
			* CFrame.Angles(
				math.rad(0),
				math.rad(0),
				math.rad(-8)
			),
	},

	["Gingerscope"] = {

	["Chroma Sweet"] = {

		Placement = "WaistRight",
		Correction = CFrame.new(0.08, -0.18, 0.04)
			* CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(-5), math.rad(-10)),

local function getPlacementAttachment(character, placement)
	if not character then
		return nil, nil
	end

	local lowerTorso =
		character:FindFirstChild("LowerTorso")
		or character:FindFirstChild("Torso")

	if not lowerTorso then
		return nil, nil
	end

	if placement == "WaistLeft" then
		return lowerTorso, lowerTorso:FindFirstChild("KnifeBelt")
	elseif placement == "WaistRight" then
		return lowerTorso, lowerTorso:FindFirstChild("GunBelt")
	end

	return lowerTorso, nil
end

local function buildAttachmentCFrame(data)
	if not data then
		return CFrame.new()
	end

	local pos = data.AttachmentPosition or Vector3.zero
	local orient = data.AttachmentOrientation or Vector3.zero

	return CFrame.new(pos)
		* CFrame.Angles(
			math.rad(orient.X),
			math.rad(orient.Y),
			math.rad(orient.Z)
		)
end

local function getFallbackHipCFrame(placement)
	-- Used only if this game's character has no GunBelt/KnifeBelt attachment.
	if placement == "WaistLeft" then
		return CFrame.new(-0.72, -0.52, 0.18)
			* CFrame.Angles(
				math.rad(0),
				math.rad(90),
				math.rad(-12)
			)
	end

	return CFrame.new(0.72, -0.52, 0.18)
		* CFrame.Angles(
			math.rad(0),
			math.rad(-90),
			math.rad(12)
		)
end

local WEAPON_ATTACHMENT_NAMES = {
	GunBelt = true,
	KnifeBelt = true,
	KnifeBack = true,
}

local BODY_PART_NAMES = {
	Head = true,
	HumanoidRootPart = true,
	UpperTorso = true,
	LowerTorso = true,
	Torso = true,
	LeftUpperArm = true,
	LeftLowerArm = true,
	LeftHand = true,
	RightUpperArm = true,
	RightLowerArm = true,
	RightHand = true,
	LeftUpperLeg = true,
	LeftLowerLeg = true,
	LeftFoot = true,
	RightUpperLeg = true,
	RightLowerLeg = true,
	RightFoot = true,
	["Left Arm"] = true,
	["Right Arm"] = true,
	["Left Leg"] = true,
	["Right Leg"] = true,
}

local function isInsideScriptedWeapon(instance)
	local current = instance

	while current do
		-- A knife that THIS script has replaced is not an "original MM2 knife"
		-- anymore. Keep it visible when Gingerscope hides the real MM2 weapons.
		local replacementName =
			current:GetAttribute(
				"BotControllerWeaponReplacement"
			)

		if WeaponPackRuntime.selectedKnifeName
			and replacementName == WeaponPackRuntime.selectedKnifeName
			and WeaponPackRuntime.WEAPON_TYPE_HINTS[
				replacementName
			] == "Knife" then

			return true
		end

		if current.Name == "HolsteredWeapon"
			or current.Name == "HolsteredGun"
			or current.Name == "HolsteredKnife"
			or current.Name == "PersistentGunFallback"
			or current.Name == "PersistentGingerscopeAccessory"
			or current:GetAttribute("ScriptOwnedWeapon") == true
			or current:GetAttribute("BotControllerNativeGingerscope") == true then

			return true
		end

		current = current.Parent
	end

	return false
end

local function nameLooksLikeRealWeapon(name)
	local normalized =
		string.lower(tostring(name or ""))

	for _, hint in ipairs(REAL_WEAPON_NAME_HINTS) do
		if string.find(normalized, hint, 1, true) then
			return true
		end
	end

	return false
end

local function getOriginalWeaponContainerFromAttachment(attachment, character)
	if not attachment
		or not attachment:IsA("Attachment")
		or not WEAPON_ATTACHMENT_NAMES[attachment.Name] then

		return nil
	end

	local parent = attachment.Parent

	if not parent then
		return nil
	end

	-- Ignore the body's own GunBelt / KnifeBelt / KnifeBack attachment.
	if parent:IsA("BasePart")
		and parent.Parent == character
		and BODY_PART_NAMES[parent.Name] then

		return nil
	end

	local accessory =
		attachment:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Accessory")

	if accessory
		and accessory:IsDescendantOf(character) then

		return accessory
	end

	local tool =
		attachment:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Tool")

	if tool
		and tool:IsDescendantOf(character) then

		return tool
	end

	local current = parent

	while current
		and current.Parent
		and current.Parent ~= character do

		if current.Parent:IsA("Model")
			or current.Parent:IsA("Folder") then

			current = current.Parent
		else
			break
		end
	end

	if current
		and current.Parent == character
		and not BODY_PART_NAMES[current.Name] then

		return current
	end

	return parent
end

local function getOriginalWeaponContainerByName(obj, character)
	if not obj
		or isInsideScriptedWeapon(obj) then

		return nil
	end

	local tool =
		obj:IsA("Tool") and obj
		or obj:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Tool")

	if tool
		and tool:IsDescendantOf(character) then

		return tool
	end

	local accessory =
		obj:IsA("Accessory") and obj
		or obj:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Accessory")

	if accessory
		and accessory:IsDescendantOf(character)
		and nameLooksLikeRealWeapon(accessory.Name) then

		return accessory
	end

	local current = obj

	while current
		and current ~= character do

		if nameLooksLikeRealWeapon(current.Name)
			and not BODY_PART_NAMES[current.Name] then

			return current
		end

		current = current.Parent
	end

	return nil
end

local function getAssetNumber(value)
	if not value then
		return nil
	end

	return tostring(value):match("(%d+)")
end

local function isKnownMM2WeaponMesh(obj)
	if isInsideScriptedWeapon(obj) then
		return false
	end

	if obj:IsA("MeshPart") then
		local id = getAssetNumber(obj.MeshId)
		return id and MM2_WEAPON_MESH_IDS[id] == true
	end

	if obj:IsA("SpecialMesh") then
		local id = getAssetNumber(obj.MeshId)
		return id and MM2_WEAPON_MESH_IDS[id] == true
	end

	return false
end

local function makeOriginalWeaponInvisible(container)
	if not container
		or not container.Parent
		or isInsideScriptedWeapon(container) then

		return
	end

	local function hideObject(obj)
		if obj:IsA("BasePart") then
			-- Keep the real weapon attached so its welds don't break/freeze,
			-- but hide it locally.
			obj.LocalTransparencyModifier = 1
			obj.CanCollide = false
			obj.CanTouch = false
			obj.CanQuery = false

		elseif obj:IsA("Decal")
			or obj:IsA("Texture") then

			obj.Transparency = 1

		elseif obj:IsA("Trail")
			or obj:IsA("Beam")
			or obj:IsA("ParticleEmitter")
			or obj:IsA("Smoke")
			or obj:IsA("Fire")
			or obj:IsA("Sparkles") then

			obj.Enabled = false
		end
	end

	hideObject(container)

	for _, descendant in ipairs(container:GetDescendants()) do
		if not isInsideScriptedWeapon(descendant) then
			hideObject(descendant)
		end
	end
end

local SAFE_AVATAR_ATTACHMENT_NAMES = {
	HairAttachment = true,
	HatAttachment = true,
	FaceFrontAttachment = true,
	FaceCenterAttachment = true,
	NeckAttachment = true,
	BodyFrontAttachment = true,
	BodyBackAttachment = true,
	LeftCollarAttachment = true,
	RightCollarAttachment = true,
	LeftShoulderAttachment = true,
	RightShoulderAttachment = true,
}

local ORIGINAL_WEAPON_RENDER_BIND =
	"BotControllerOriginalWeaponMask"

local function getAccessoryAncestor(instance)
	if not instance then
		return nil
	end

	return instance:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Accessory")
end

local function accessoryHasAttachment(accessory, lookup)
	if not accessory then
		return false
	end

	for _, obj in ipairs(accessory:GetDescendants()) do
		if obj:IsA("Attachment")
			and lookup[obj.Name] then

			return true
		end
	end

	return false
end

local function isSafeAvatarAccessory(instance)
	local accessory =
		instance:IsA("Accessory") and instance
		or getAccessoryAncestor(instance)

	if not accessory then
		return false
	end

	-- Preserve normal avatar hair / hat / face / body accessories.
	if accessoryHasAttachment(
		accessory,
		SAFE_AVATAR_ATTACHMENT_NAMES
	) then
		return true
	end

	return false
end

local function restoreSafeAvatarAccessoryVisuals(character)
	if not character then
		return
	end

	for _, obj in ipairs(character:GetDescendants()) do
		if obj:IsA("BasePart")
			and isSafeAvatarAccessory(obj)
			and not isInsideScriptedWeapon(obj) then

			-- This specifically undoes the old aggressive script if it is
			-- still running in the same executor session.
			obj.LocalTransparencyModifier = 0
		end
	end
end
