local function hideOriginalWeaponPart(part)
	if not part
		or not part.Parent
		or not part:IsA("BasePart")
		or isInsideScriptedWeapon(part) then

		return
	end

	part.LocalTransparencyModifier = 1
	part.CanCollide = false
	part.CanTouch = false
	part.CanQuery = false

	for _, obj in ipairs(part:GetDescendants()) do
		if obj:IsA("Decal")
			or obj:IsA("Texture") then

			obj.Transparency = 1

		elseif obj:IsA("Trail")
			or obj:IsA("Beam")
			or obj:IsA("ParticleEmitter")
			or obj:IsA("Smoke")
			or obj:IsA("Fire")
			or obj:IsA("Sparkles") then

			obj.Enabled = false
		end
	end
end

function WeaponPackRuntime.isCharacterBodyPart(
	part,
	character
)
	if not part
		or not character
		or not part:IsA("BasePart")
		or part.Parent ~= character then

		return false
	end

	local name =
		part.Name

	return
		name == "Head"
		or name == "HumanoidRootPart"
		or name == "UpperTorso"
		or name == "LowerTorso"
		or name == "Torso"
		or name == "LeftUpperArm"
		or name == "LeftLowerArm"
		or name == "LeftHand"
		or name == "RightUpperArm"
		or name == "RightLowerArm"
		or name == "RightHand"
		or name == "LeftUpperLeg"
		or name == "LeftLowerLeg"
		or name == "LeftFoot"
		or name == "RightUpperLeg"
		or name == "RightLowerLeg"
		or name == "RightFoot"
		or name == "Left Arm"
		or name == "Right Arm"
		or name == "Left Leg"
		or name == "Right Leg"
end

local function isOriginalWeaponPart(part, character)
	if not part
		or not part:IsA("BasePart")
		or not character
		or isInsideScriptedWeapon(part)
		or WeaponPackRuntime.isCharacterBodyPart(part, character)
		or isSafeAvatarAccessory(part) then

		return false
	end

	-- Anything inside an equipped Tool is definitely an original weapon.
	local tool =
		part:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Tool")

	if tool
		and tool:IsDescendantOf(character) then

		return true
	end

	-- Accessories using the game's weapon belt/back attachments are weapons.
	local accessory =
		getAccessoryAncestor(part)

	if accessory
		and accessory:IsDescendantOf(character)
		and accessoryHasAttachment(
			accessory,
			WEAPON_ATTACHMENT_NAMES
		) then

		return true
	end

	-- Custom MM2 display models are commonly parented directly under
	-- Character without being Tools/Accessories.
	if part:IsDescendantOf(character) then
		local top = part

		while top.Parent
			and top.Parent ~= character do

			top = top.Parent
		end

		if top.Parent == character
			and not BODY_PART_NAMES[top.Name]
			and not top:IsA("Accessory") then

			return true
		end
	end

	return false
end

local function hideCharacterOriginalWeaponParts(character)
	if not character then
		return
	end

	for _, obj in ipairs(character:GetDescendants()) do
		if obj:IsA("BasePart")
			and isOriginalWeaponPart(
				obj,
				character
			) then

			hideOriginalWeaponPart(obj)
		end
	end
end

local function hideConnectedOriginalWeaponParts(character)
	if not character then
		return
	end

	-- Check all physical parts connected to the actual avatar body.
	-- This catches custom displays even if MM2 parents the weapon elsewhere.
	for _, bodyPart in ipairs(character:GetChildren()) do
		if bodyPart:IsA("BasePart")
			and BODY_PART_NAMES[bodyPart.Name] then

			local ok, connected =
				pcall(function()
					return bodyPart:GetConnectedParts(true)
				end)

			if ok and connected then
				for _, part in ipairs(connected) do
					if part
						and part:IsA("BasePart")
						and not isInsideScriptedWeapon(part)
						and not WeaponPackRuntime.isCharacterBodyPart(part, character)
						and not isSafeAvatarAccessory(part) then

						hideOriginalWeaponPart(part)
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end
end

local function removeOriginalWeaponDisplays(character)
	if not character then
		return
	end

	-- First restore legitimate avatar cosmetics in case an older version of
	-- this script is still trying to hide them.
	restoreSafeAvatarAccessoryVisuals(character)

	-- Hide only non-body weapon/display geometry.
	hideCharacterOriginalWeaponParts(character)
	hideConnectedOriginalWeaponParts(character)
end


-- Gingerscope uses its own visible native waist accessory.
-- Hide ALL original MM2 weapon geometry (gun + knife) while preserving
-- normal avatar body parts/accessories. This uses the script's mature
-- original-weapon detector instead of relying on container names.
WeaponPackRuntime.GINGERSCOPE_HIDDEN_PART_STATE =
	WeaponPackRuntime.GINGERSCOPE_HIDDEN_PART_STATE
	or setmetatable(
		{},
		{__mode = "k"}
	)

function WeaponPackRuntime.hardHideGingerscopeOriginalPart(
	part
)
	if not part
		or not part.Parent
		or not part:IsA("BasePart")
		or isInsideScriptedWeapon(part) then

		return
	end

	local state =
		WeaponPackRuntime.GINGERSCOPE_HIDDEN_PART_STATE[
			part
		]

	if not state then
		state = {
			Transparency =
				part.Transparency,
		}

		WeaponPackRuntime.GINGERSCOPE_HIDDEN_PART_STATE[
			part
		] = state
	end

	pcall(function()
		part.LocalTransparencyModifier = 1
		part.Transparency = 1
	end)

	for _, object in ipairs(
		part:GetDescendants()
	) do
		if object:IsA("Decal")
			or object:IsA("Texture") then

			pcall(function()
				object.Transparency = 1
			end)

		elseif object:IsA("ParticleEmitter")
			or object:IsA("Trail")
			or object:IsA("Beam")
			or object:IsA("Smoke")
			or object:IsA("Fire")
			or object:IsA("Sparkles") then

			pcall(function()
				object.Enabled = false
			end)
		end
	end
end

function WeaponPackRuntime.restoreGingerscopeHardHiddenParts()
	for part, state in pairs(
		WeaponPackRuntime.GINGERSCOPE_HIDDEN_PART_STATE
	) do
		if part
			and part.Parent then

			pcall(function()
				part.LocalTransparencyModifier = 0
				part.Transparency =
					state.Transparency
					or 0
			end)
		end
	end

	WeaponPackRuntime.GINGERSCOPE_HIDDEN_PART_STATE =
		setmetatable(
			{},
			{__mode = "k"}
		)
end

function WeaponPackRuntime.enforceGingerscopeVisualIsolation(
	character
)
	if not character
		or not character.Parent then

		return
	end

	-- Keep normal avatar cosmetics visible.
	restoreSafeAvatarAccessoryVisuals(
		character
	)

	-- 1) Character descendants.
	for _, object in ipairs(
		character:GetDescendants()
	) do
		if object:IsA("BasePart")
			and not WeaponPackRuntime.isCharacterBodyPart(
				object,
				character
			)
			and not isInsideScriptedWeapon(object) then

			local tool =
				object:FindFirstAncestorOfClass(
					"Tool"
				)

			local definitelyWeapon =
				isOriginalWeaponPart(
					object,
					character
				)
				or isKnownMM2WeaponMesh(
					object
				)
				or (
					tool
					and tool:IsDescendantOf(
						character
					)
				)

			if definitelyWeapon then
				WeaponPackRuntime.hardHideGingerscopeOriginalPart(
					object
				)
			end
		end
	end

	-- 2) Parts physically welded/connected to the avatar, even when MM2
	-- parents the round-start gun outside Character.
	for _, bodyPart in ipairs(
		character:GetChildren()
	) do
		if bodyPart:IsA("BasePart")
			and BODY_PART_NAMES[
				bodyPart.Name
			] then

			local ok, connected =
				pcall(function()
					return bodyPart:GetConnectedParts(
						true
					)
				end)

			if ok
				and connected then

				for _, part in ipairs(
					connected
				) do
					if part
						and part:IsA("BasePart")
						and not WeaponPackRuntime.isCharacterBodyPart(
							part,
							character
						)
						and not isInsideScriptedWeapon(
							part
						) then

						local lowerName =
							string.lower(
								tostring(
									part.Name
								)
							)

						local parentName =
							string.lower(
								tostring(
									part.Parent
									and part.Parent.Name
									or ""
								)
							)

						local weaponLike =
							isKnownMM2WeaponMesh(
								part
							)
							or nameLooksLikeRealWeapon(
								lowerName
							)
							or nameLooksLikeRealWeapon(
								parentName
							)
							or part:FindFirstAncestorOfClass(
								"Tool"
							) ~= nil

						if weaponLike then
							WeaponPackRuntime.hardHideGingerscopeOriginalPart(
								part
							)
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end
end

function WeaponPackRuntime.disconnectPlayerWeaponPersistence()
	if WeaponPackRuntime.playerWeaponPersistenceConnection then
		WeaponPackRuntime.playerWeaponPersistenceConnection:Disconnect()
		WeaponPackRuntime.playerWeaponPersistenceConnection =
			nil
	end

	if WeaponPackRuntime.playerWeaponRemovingConnection then
		WeaponPackRuntime.playerWeaponRemovingConnection:Disconnect()
		WeaponPackRuntime.playerWeaponRemovingConnection =
			nil
	end
end

function WeaponPackRuntime.restoreOriginalWeaponVisibility(
	character
)
	if not character then
		return
	end

	for _, object in ipairs(
		character:GetDescendants()
	) do
		if object:IsA("BasePart")
			and not WeaponPackRuntime.isCharacterBodyPart(
				object,
				character
			)
			and not isInsideScriptedWeapon(object) then

			local tool =
				object:FindFirstAncestorOfClass(
					"Tool"
				)

			local shouldRestore =
				isOriginalWeaponPart(
					object,
					character
				)
				or isKnownMM2WeaponMesh(object)
				or (
					tool
					and tool:IsDescendantOf(character)
				)

			if shouldRestore then
				pcall(function()
					object.LocalTransparencyModifier = 0
				end)
			end
		end
	end
end

local function stopRealWeaponHideLoop(character)
	local connection =
		weaponHideConnections[character]

	if connection then
		connection:Disconnect()
		weaponHideConnections[character] = nil
	end

	pcall(function()
		RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep(
			ORIGINAL_WEAPON_RENDER_BIND
		)
	end)
end

local function startRealWeaponHideLoop(character)
	if not character then
		return
	end

	stopRealWeaponHideLoop(character)

	removeOriginalWeaponDisplays(character)

	-- Refresh detection in case MM2 recreates its weapon display.
	local nextScan = 0

	weaponHideConnections[character] =
		RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
			if not character.Parent
				or not character:FindFirstChild("HolsteredWeapon") then

				stopRealWeaponHideLoop(character)
				return
			end

			if time() >= nextScan then
				nextScan = time() + 0.20
				removeOriginalWeaponDisplays(character)
			end
		end)

	-- Final visual pass every rendered frame.
	pcall(function()
		RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep(
			ORIGINAL_WEAPON_RENDER_BIND
		)
	end)

	RunService:BindToRenderStep(
		ORIGINAL_WEAPON_RENDER_BIND,
		10000,
		function()
			if not character.Parent
				or not character:FindFirstChild("HolsteredWeapon") then

				return
			end

			-- Restore legitimate avatar accessories first.
			restoreSafeAvatarAccessoryVisuals(character)

			-- Then hide only detected original weapon parts.
			hideCharacterOriginalWeaponParts(character)
			hideConnectedOriginalWeaponParts(character)
		end
	)
end

local function unequipRealHeldTools(character)
	if not character then
		return
	end

	local humanoid =
		character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

	if humanoid then
		pcall(function()
			humanoid:UnequipTools()
		end)
	end
end

local function findBodyPlacementAttachment(character, placement)
	if not character then
		return nil
	end

	local wantedName

	if placement == "WaistRight" then
		wantedName = "GunBelt"
	elseif placement == "WaistLeft" then
		wantedName = "KnifeBelt"
	elseif placement == "Back" then
		wantedName = "KnifeBack"
	end

	if not wantedName then
		return nil
	end

	-- Recursive lookup supports custom rigs where the attachment is
	-- not directly under LowerTorso.
	local attachment =
		character:FindFirstChild(wantedName, true)

	if attachment
		and attachment:IsA("Attachment")
		and attachment.Parent
		and attachment.Parent:IsA("BasePart") then

		return attachment
	end

	return nil
end

local function getFallbackBodyAnchor(character)
	return character:FindFirstChild("LowerTorso")
		or character:FindFirstChild("Torso")
		or character:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
		or character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
end

local function getFallbackBeltWorldCFrame(anchor, placement)
	if not anchor then
		return nil
	end

	if placement == "WaistLeft" then
		return anchor.CFrame
			* CFrame.new(-0.62, -0.45, 0.16)
	end

	if placement == "Back" then
		return anchor.CFrame
			* CFrame.new(0, 0, 0.48)
	end

	return anchor.CFrame
		* CFrame.new(0.62, -0.45, 0.16)
end

WeaponPackRuntime.syncBotWeaponAttachments =
	function(botCharacter)
		if not botCharacter
			or botCharacter == player.Character then

			return
		end

		local playerCharacter =
			player.Character

		if not playerCharacter then
			return
		end

		local attachmentMap = {
			{
				name = "GunBelt",
				playerPart =
					playerCharacter:FindFirstChild(
						"LowerTorso"
					)
					or playerCharacter:FindFirstChild(
						"Torso"
					),
				botPart =
					botCharacter:FindFirstChild(
						"LowerTorso"
					)
					or botCharacter:FindFirstChild(
						"Torso"
					),
			},

			{
				name = "KnifeBelt",
				playerPart =
					playerCharacter:FindFirstChild(
						"LowerTorso"
					)
					or playerCharacter:FindFirstChild(
						"Torso"
					),
				botPart =
					botCharacter:FindFirstChild(
						"LowerTorso"
					)
					or botCharacter:FindFirstChild(
						"Torso"
					),
			},

			{
				name = "KnifeBack",
				playerPart =
					playerCharacter:FindFirstChild(
						"UpperTorso"
					)
					or playerCharacter:FindFirstChild(
						"Torso"
					),
				botPart =
					botCharacter:FindFirstChild(
						"UpperTorso"
					)
					or botCharacter:FindFirstChild(
						"Torso"
					),
			},
		}

		for _, info in ipairs(
			attachmentMap
		) do
			local source =
				info.playerPart
				and info.playerPart:FindFirstChild(
					info.name
				)

			if source
				and source:IsA("Attachment")
				and info.botPart then

				local target =
					info.botPart:FindFirstChild(
						info.name
					)

				if not target
					or not target:IsA("Attachment") then

					if target then
						target:Destroy()
					end

					target =
						Instance.new(
							"Attachment"
						)

					target.Name =
						info.name

					target.Parent =
						info.botPart
				end

				-- CFrame contains the exact local position + rotation
				-- used by MM2 on the player's real rig.
				target.CFrame =
					source.CFrame

				-- Keep the attachment vector properties identical too.
				target.Axis =
					source.Axis

				target.SecondaryAxis =
					source.SecondaryAxis
			end
		end
	end

local function holsterWeapon(character, weaponName, slotModelName)
	if not character then
		return false, "character missing"
	end

	-- Bots are normal Roblox avatar rigs and do not naturally contain
	-- MM2's GunBelt / KnifeBelt / KnifeBack attachments.
	-- Copy the exact attachment transforms from the player's MM2 rig.
	if character ~= player.Character then
		WeaponPackRuntime.syncBotWeaponAttachments(
			character
		)
	end

	local exactVisual =
		FALLBACK_WEAPON_VISUALS[weaponName]

	local weaponType =
		WeaponPackRuntime.WEAPON_TYPE_HINTS[weaponName]
		or (exactVisual and exactVisual.Type)
		or "Knife"

	local holsterModelName =
		slotModelName
		or (
			character ~= player.Character
			and (
				weaponType == "Gun"
				and "HolsteredGun"
				or "HolsteredKnife"
			)
			or "HolsteredWeapon"
		)

	local data =
		WEAPON_HOLSTER_DATA[weaponName]
		or exactVisual
		or {
			Placement = weaponType == "Gun" and "WaistRight" or "WaistLeft",
			AttachmentPosition = Vector3.zero,
			AttachmentOrientation = Vector3.zero,
		}

	local source =
		cloneWeaponAsTool(weaponName)

	if not source then
		return false, "weapon visual not found"
	end

	local model =
		prepareWeaponVisual(
			source,
			weaponName,
			holsterModelName
		)

	if not model
		or not model.PrimaryPart then

		return false, "weapon has no visual part"
	end

	local bodyAttachment =
		findBodyPlacementAttachment(
			character,
			data.Placement
		)

	local fallbackAnchor =
		getFallbackBodyAnchor(character)

	if not bodyAttachment
		and not fallbackAnchor then

		model:Destroy()
		return false, "body anchor missing"
	end

	clearHolsteredWeapon(character, holsterModelName)

	-- Only the real player has MM2's original weapon display/Tools.
	-- Bot models do not need this cleanup.
	if character == player.Character then
		unequipRealHeldTools(
			character
		)
	end

	model.Parent = character

	local root =
		model.PrimaryPart

	-- These values describe the attachment ON THE WEAPON.
	-- To make that attachment coincide with GunBelt/KnifeBelt,
	-- the weapon attachment transform must be INVERTED.
	local weaponAttachmentCFrame =
		buildAttachmentCFrame(data)

	local targetWorldCFrame

	if bodyAttachment then
		targetWorldCFrame =
			bodyAttachment.WorldCFrame
	else
		targetWorldCFrame =
			getFallbackBeltWorldCFrame(
				fallbackAnchor,
				data.Placement
			)
	end

	local finalWorldCFrame =
		targetWorldCFrame
		* weaponAttachmentCFrame:Inverse()

	-- Harvester/Icepiercer use MM2's long-gun axis.
	if weaponName == "Harvester"
		or weaponName == "Icepiercer" then

		finalWorldCFrame =
			finalWorldCFrame
			* CFrame.Angles(
				math.rad(90),
				0,
				0
			)

	-- The Gingerscope UGC fallback has different local axes.
	elseif weaponName == "Gingerscope" then
		finalWorldCFrame =
			getGingerscopeWorldCFrame(
				targetWorldCFrame
			)
	end

	root.CFrame =
		finalWorldCFrame

	-- Weld to whichever body part owns the placement attachment.
	local weldPart =
		bodyAttachment
		and bodyAttachment.Parent
		or fallbackAnchor

	local weld = Instance.new("WeldConstraint")
	weld.Name = "HolsterWeld"
	weld.Part0 = weldPart
	weld.Part1 = root
	weld.Parent = root

	-- Only the actual player has an original MM2 weapon display
	-- that needs masking. Bots use only our cosmetic weapon.
	if character == player.Character then
		startRealWeaponHideLoop(
			character
		)
	end

	return true
end


--------------------------------------------------
-- PLAYER WEAPON: REPLACE CURRENT WEAPON MESH ONLY
--
-- IMPORTANT:
-- Only edits an existing SpecialMesh.
-- It NEVER changes MeshPart.MeshId because that can make the
-- replacement mesh enormous due to MeshPart.Size.
--------------------------------------------------

local function getWeaponVisualData(
	weaponName,
	allowDeepSearch
)
	local visual =
		FALLBACK_WEAPON_VISUALS[
			weaponName
		]
		or WeaponPackRuntime.RUNTIME_WEAPON_VISUAL_CACHE[
			weaponName
		]

	if not visual and allowDeepSearch then
		visual =
			WeaponPackRuntime.resolveRuntimeWeaponVisual(
				weaponName
			)
	end

	if not visual then
		return nil
	end

	return {
		MeshId = visual.MeshId,
		TextureId = visual.TextureId,
		Scale = visual.Scale,
		Offset =
			visual.Offset
			or Vector3.zero,
		Type =
			visual.Type
			or WeaponPackRuntime.WEAPON_TYPE_HINTS[
				weaponName
			]
			or "Knife",
		Placement =
			visual.Placement
			or (
				WeaponPackRuntime.WEAPON_TYPE_HINTS[
					weaponName
				] == "Gun"
				and "WaistRight"
				or "WaistLeft"
			),
		AttachmentPosition =
			visual.AttachmentPosition,
		AttachmentOrientation =
			visual.AttachmentOrientation,
		Color = visual.Color,
		Material = visual.Material,
		Transparency =
			visual.Transparency,
		Reflectance =
			visual.Reflectance,
		VertexColor =
			visual.VertexColor,
		Source =
			visual.Source
			or (
				FALLBACK_WEAPON_VISUALS[
					weaponName
				]
				and "verified exact"
				or "runtime resolved"
			),
	}
end

local PLAYER_BODY_NAMES = {
	Head = true,
	HumanoidRootPart = true,
	UpperTorso = true,
	LowerTorso = true,
	Torso = true,

	LeftUpperArm = true,
	LeftLowerArm = true,
	LeftHand = true,
	RightUpperArm = true,
	RightLowerArm = true,
	RightHand = true,

	LeftUpperLeg = true,
	LeftLowerLeg = true,
	LeftFoot = true,
	RightUpperLeg = true,
	RightLowerLeg = true,
	RightFoot = true,

	["Left Arm"] = true,
	["Right Arm"] = true,
	["Left Leg"] = true,
	["Right Leg"] = true,
}

local SAFE_AVATAR_ATTACHMENTS_FOR_SWAP = {
	HairAttachment = true,
	HatAttachment = true,
	FaceFrontAttachment = true,
	FaceCenterAttachment = true,
	NeckAttachment = true,
	BodyFrontAttachment = true,
	BodyBackAttachment = true,
	LeftCollarAttachment = true,
	RightCollarAttachment = true,
	LeftShoulderAttachment = true,
	RightShoulderAttachment = true,
}

local function isPlayerBodyPart(part, character)
	return part
		and part:IsA("BasePart")
		and part.Parent == character
		and PLAYER_BODY_NAMES[part.Name] == true
end

local function isNormalAvatarAccessoryForSwap(part)
	local accessory =
		part
		and part:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Accessory")

	if not accessory then
		return false
	end

	for _, obj in ipairs(accessory:GetDescendants()) do
		if obj:IsA("Attachment")
			and SAFE_AVATAR_ATTACHMENTS_FOR_SWAP[obj.Name] then

			return true
		end
	end

	return false
end

local function getPartSpecialMesh(part)
	if not part
		or not part:IsA("BasePart") then

		return nil
	end

	return part:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("SpecialMesh")
end

local function connectedToCharacterBody(part, character)
	if not part
		or not character then

		return false
	end

	if part:IsDescendantOf(character) then
		return true
	end

	local ok, connected =
		pcall(function()
			return part:GetConnectedParts(true)
		end)

	if ok and connected then
		for _, other in ipairs(connected) do
			if isPlayerBodyPart(other, character) then
				return true
			end
		end
	end

	return false
end

WeaponPackRuntime.findPlacementWeaponPart =
	function(character, weaponType)
		if not character then
			return nil, nil
		end

		local wantedAttachments

		if weaponType == "Gun" then
			wantedAttachments = {
				GunBelt = true,
			}
		else
			wantedAttachments = {
				KnifeBelt = true,
				KnifeBack = true,
			}
		end

		for _, attachment in ipairs(
			character:GetDescendants()
		) do
			if attachment:IsA("Attachment")
				and wantedAttachments[
					attachment.Name
				] then

				local parent =
					attachment.Parent

				-- Skip MM2's BODY attachment itself.
				if parent
					and parent:IsA("BasePart")
					and parent.Parent == character
					and PLAYER_BODY_NAMES[
						parent.Name
					] then

					continue
				end

				local part = nil

				if parent
					and parent:IsA("BasePart") then

					part = parent
				end

				if not part then
					local accessory =
						attachment:FindFirstAncestorOfClass(
							"Accessory"
						)

					if accessory then
						part =
							accessory:FindFirstChild(
								"Handle"
							)
							or accessory:FindFirstChildWhichIsA(
								"BasePart",
								true
							)
					end
				end

				if not part then
					local tool =
						attachment:FindFirstAncestorOfClass(
							"Tool"
						)

					if tool then
						part =
							tool:FindFirstChild(
								"Handle"
							)
							or tool:FindFirstChildWhichIsA(
								"BasePart",
								true
							)
					end
				end

				if not part then
					local current =
						attachment.Parent

					while current
						and current ~= character do

						if current:IsA("Model") then
							part =
								current:FindFirstChild(
									"Handle",
									true
								)
								or current:FindFirstChildWhichIsA(
									"BasePart",
									true
								)

							if part then
								break
							end
						end

						current =
							current.Parent
					end
				end

				if part
					and not isPlayerBodyPart(
						part,
						character
					)
					and not isNormalAvatarAccessoryForSwap(
						part
					)
					and not part:FindFirstAncestor(
						"HolsteredWeapon"
					)
					and not part:FindFirstAncestor(
						"HolsteredGun"
					)
					and not part:FindFirstAncestor(
						"HolsteredKnife"
					)
					and not part:FindFirstAncestor(
						"PersistentGunFallback"
					) then

					local mesh =
						getPartSpecialMesh(
							part
						)

					if mesh then
						return part, mesh
					end
				end
			end
		end

		return nil, nil
	end

WeaponPackRuntime.partHasWeaponPlacementAttachment =
	function(part, weaponType)
		if not part then
			return false
		end

		local wanted =
			weaponType == "Gun"
			and "GunBelt"
			or nil

		if wanted then
			for _, object in ipairs(
				part:GetDescendants()
			) do
				if object:IsA("Attachment")
					and object.Name == wanted then

					return true
				end
			end
		else
			for _, object in ipairs(
				part:GetDescendants()
			) do
				if object:IsA("Attachment")
					and (
						object.Name == "KnifeBelt"
						or object.Name == "KnifeBack"
					) then

					return true
				end
			end
		end

		return false
	end

local function getCurrentWeaponSpecialMeshCandidates(character, weaponType)
	local candidates = {}

	if not character then
		return candidates
	end

	--------------------------------------------------
	-- 0. EXACT MM2 PLACEMENT ATTACHMENT
	-- Highest priority and especially important when MM2
	-- recreates the gun after a round ends.
	--------------------------------------------------

	local placementPart,
		placementMesh =
			WeaponPackRuntime.findPlacementWeaponPart(
				character,
				weaponType
			)

	if placementPart
		and placementMesh then

		table.insert(
			candidates,
			{
				part = placementPart,
				mesh = placementMesh,
				score = 10000,
				exactPlacement = true,
			}
		)
	end

	local torso =
		character:FindFirstChild("LowerTorso")
		or character:FindFirstChild("Torso")
		or character:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
		or character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

	--------------------------------------------------
	-- 1. EQUIPPED TOOL HANDLE
	--------------------------------------------------

	for _, child in ipairs(character:GetChildren()) do
		if child:IsA("Tool") then
			local handle =
				child:FindFirstChild("Handle")
				or child:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)

			local mesh =
				getPartSpecialMesh(handle)

			if handle and mesh then
				local lowerToolName =
					string.lower(
						tostring(child.Name)
					)

				local lowerHandleName =
					string.lower(
						tostring(handle.Name)
					)

				local toolLooksGun =
					string.find(
						lowerToolName,
						"gun",
						1,
						true
					)
					or string.find(
						lowerToolName,
						"revolver",
						1,
						true
					)
					or string.find(
						lowerHandleName,
						"gun",
						1,
						true
					)

				local toolLooksKnife =
					string.find(
						lowerToolName,
						"knife",
						1,
						true
					)
					or string.find(
						lowerToolName,
						"blade",
						1,
						true
					)

				local plausibleTool =
					weaponType == "Gun"
					and not toolLooksKnife
					or weaponType ~= "Gun"
					and not toolLooksGun

				if plausibleTool then
					table.insert(
						candidates,
						{
							part = handle,
							mesh = mesh,
							score =
								toolLooksGun
								and 2600
								or 2000,
						}
					)
				end
			end
		end
	end

	--------------------------------------------------
	-- 2. CHARACTER-PARENTED DISPLAY PARTS
	--------------------------------------------------

	for _, part in ipairs(character:GetDescendants()) do
		if part:IsA("BasePart")
			and not isPlayerBodyPart(part, character)
			and not isNormalAvatarAccessoryForSwap(part)
			and not isInsideScriptedWeapon(part) then

			local mesh =
				getPartSpecialMesh(part)

			if mesh
				and part ~= placementPart then

				local score = 0
				local lowerName =
					string.lower(
						tostring(part.Name)
					)

				local parentName =
					string.lower(
						tostring(
							part.Parent
							and part.Parent.Name
							or ""
						)
					)

				if string.find(lowerName, "gun", 1, true)
					or string.find(parentName, "gun", 1, true)
					or string.find(lowerName, "revolver", 1, true)
					or string.find(parentName, "revolver", 1, true) then

					score += 500
				end

				if string.find(lowerName, "knife", 1, true)
					or string.find(parentName, "knife", 1, true)
					or string.find(lowerName, "blade", 1, true)
					or string.find(parentName, "blade", 1, true) then

					score += 350
				end

				if lowerName == "handle" then
					score += 100
				end

				if torso then
					local localPos =
						torso.CFrame:PointToObjectSpace(
							part.Position
						)

					local distance =
						Vector3.new(
							localPos.X,
							localPos.Y,
							localPos.Z
						).Magnitude

					score +=
						math.max(
							0,
							250 - distance * 40
						)

					-- Harvester is a gun, so strongly prefer the
					-- right side where MM2 normally displays guns.
					if weaponType == "Gun" then
						if localPos.X > 0 then
							score += 450
						else
							score -= 300
						end
					else
						if localPos.X < 0 then
							score += 450
						else
							score -= 300
						end
					end
				end

				-- Never use the visible knife as a fallback host for
				-- a missing gun (or vice versa) after round transitions.
				local placementMatch =
					WeaponPackRuntime.partHasWeaponPlacementAttachment(
						part,
						weaponType
					)

				local plausible = true

				if torso
					and not placementMatch then

					local localPos =
						torso.CFrame:PointToObjectSpace(
							part.Position
						)

					local lowerName =
						string.lower(
							tostring(
								part.Name
							)
						)

					local parentName =
						string.lower(
							tostring(
								part.Parent
								and part.Parent.Name
								or ""
							)
						)

					if weaponType == "Gun" then
						local looksGun =
							string.find(
								lowerName,
								"gun",
								1,
								true
							)
							or string.find(
								parentName,
								"gun",
								1,
								true
							)
							or string.find(
								lowerName,
								"revolver",
								1,
								true
							)
							or string.find(
								parentName,
								"revolver",
								1,
								true
							)

						if localPos.X <= 0
							and not looksGun then

							plausible = false
						end
					else
						local looksKnife =
							string.find(
								lowerName,
								"knife",
								1,
								true
							)
							or string.find(
								parentName,
								"knife",
								1,
								true
							)
							or string.find(
								lowerName,
								"blade",
								1,
								true
							)
							or string.find(
								parentName,
								"blade",
								1,
								true
							)

						if localPos.X >= 0
							and not looksKnife then

							plausible = false
						end
					end
				end

				if plausible then
					table.insert(
						candidates,
						{
							part = part,
							mesh = mesh,
							score = score,
						}
					)
				end
			end
		end
	end

	--------------------------------------------------
	-- 3. WORKSPACE-PARENTED BUT PHYSICALLY CONNECTED DISPLAY
	--------------------------------------------------

	if torso then
		for _, part in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
			if part:IsA("BasePart")
				and not part:IsDescendantOf(character)
				and not isInsideScriptedWeapon(part)
				and not isNormalAvatarAccessoryForSwap(part) then

				local mesh =
					getPartSpecialMesh(part)

				if mesh
					and (part.Position - torso.Position).Magnitude <= 8
					and connectedToCharacterBody(part, character) then

					local localPos =
						torso.CFrame:PointToObjectSpace(
							part.Position
						)

					local score = 600

					if weaponType == "Gun" then
						if localPos.X > 0 then
							score += 500
						else
							score -= 300
						end
					else
						if localPos.X < 0 then
							score += 500
						else
							score -= 300
						end
					end

					table.insert(
						candidates,
						{
							part = part,
							mesh = mesh,
							score = score,
						}
					)
				end
			end
		end
	end

	table.sort(
		candidates,
		function(a, b)
			return a.score > b.score
		end
	)

	return candidates
end

local ORIGINAL_WEAPON_MESH_STATE = {}

local function isOrientationBodyPart(part, character)
	return part
		and part:IsA("BasePart")
		and part.Parent == character
		and PLAYER_BODY_NAMES[part.Name] == true
end

local function findWeaponBodyJoint(part, character)
	if not part
		or not character then

		return nil
	end

	-- Find the joint that directly connects THIS weapon part
	-- to the avatar body. We do not touch internal weapon welds.
	for _, obj in ipairs(character:GetDescendants()) do
		if obj:IsA("Weld")
			or obj:IsA("Motor6D") then

			if obj.Part0 == part
				and isOrientationBodyPart(
					obj.Part1,
					character
				) then

				return obj

			elseif obj.Part1 == part
				and isOrientationBodyPart(
					obj.Part0,
					character
				) then

				return obj
			end
		end
	end

	-- Some games parent the weapon joint under the weapon model.
	local parent =
		part.Parent

	if parent then
		for _, obj in ipairs(parent:GetDescendants()) do
			if obj:IsA("Weld")
				or obj:IsA("Motor6D") then

				if obj.Part0 == part
					and isOrientationBodyPart(
						obj.Part1,
						character
					) then

					return obj

				elseif obj.Part1 == part
					and isOrientationBodyPart(
						obj.Part0,
						character
					) then

					return obj
				end
			end
		end
	end

	return nil
end

local function saveOriginalWeaponMeshState(part, mesh, character)
	if not part
		or not mesh then
		return
	end

	local state =
		ORIGINAL_WEAPON_MESH_STATE[part]

	if not state then
		state = {
			MeshId = mesh.MeshId,
			TextureId = mesh.TextureId,
			Scale = mesh.Scale,
			Offset = mesh.Offset,
			MeshType = mesh.MeshType,
			VertexColor = mesh.VertexColor,

			Color = part.Color,
			Material = part.Material,
			Transparency = part.Transparency,
			Reflectance = part.Reflectance,
		}

		ORIGINAL_WEAPON_MESH_STATE[part] =
			state
	end

	-- Save the original body joint only once.
	if not state.Joint then
		local joint =
			findWeaponBodyJoint(
				part,
				character
			)

		if joint then
			state.Joint = joint
			state.JointC0 = joint.C0
			state.JointC1 = joint.C1
		end
	end
end

local function restoreOriginalWeaponMeshes()
	local restored = 0

	for part, state in pairs(
		ORIGINAL_WEAPON_MESH_STATE
	) do

		if part
			and part.Parent then

			local mesh =
				part:FindFirstChildWhichIsA(
					"SpecialMesh"
				)

			if mesh then
				pcall(function()
					mesh.MeshType =
						state.MeshType

					mesh.MeshId =
						state.MeshId

					mesh.TextureId =
						state.TextureId

					mesh.Scale =
						state.Scale

					mesh.Offset =
						state.Offset

					mesh.VertexColor =
						state.VertexColor
						or Vector3.new(1, 1, 1)

					part.Color =
						state.Color
						or part.Color

					part.Material =
						state.Material
						or part.Material

					part.Transparency =
						state.Transparency
						or 0

					part.Reflectance =
						state.Reflectance
						or 0

					part.LocalTransparencyModifier = 0

					part:SetAttribute(
						"BotControllerWeaponReplacement",
						nil
					)

					part:SetAttribute(
						"BotControllerHarvesterSidewaysFixed",
						nil
					)

					part:SetAttribute(
						"BotControllerIcepiercerSidewaysFixed",
						nil
					)

					part:SetAttribute(
						"BotControllerCelestialBackFixed",
						nil
					)

					restored += 1
				end)
			end

			-- Restore the weapon's original orientation too.
			local joint =
				state.Joint

			if joint
				and joint.Parent then

				pcall(function()
					joint.C0 =
						state.JointC0

					joint.C1 =
						state.JointC1
				end)
			end
		end
	end

	table.clear(
		ORIGINAL_WEAPON_MESH_STATE
	)

	WeaponPackRuntime.currentReplacementPart =
		nil

	WeaponPackRuntime.currentReplacementWeaponName =
		nil

	WeaponPackRuntime.currentReplacementCharacter =
		nil

	return restored
end

local function getHarvesterTargetRotation(character)
	if not character then
		return nil
	end

	local gunBelt =
		character:FindFirstChild(
			"GunBelt",
			true
		)

	if not gunBelt
		or not gunBelt:IsA("Attachment") then

		return nil
	end

	-- Exact Harvester attachment transform from your weapon data.
	local harvesterAttachment =
		CFrame.new(
			0.1299028992652893,
			0.000002229222218375071,
			0.07500199228525162
		)
		* CFrame.Angles(
			math.rad(
				-0.0026997302193194628
			),
			math.rad(-60),
			math.rad(
				90.00231170654297
			)
		)

	-- This is the same orientation relationship that made
	-- Harvester sit correctly in the earlier version.
	local target =
		gunBelt.WorldCFrame
		* harvesterAttachment:Inverse()

	return target.Rotation
end

local function orientLongGunCorrectly(
	part,
	character,
	weaponName,
	visual
)
	if (
		weaponName ~= "Harvester"
		and weaponName ~= "Icepiercer"
	)
		or not part
		or not character
		or not visual then

		return
	end

	local attributeName =
		weaponName == "Harvester"
		and "BotControllerHarvesterSidewaysFixed"
		or "BotControllerIcepiercerSidewaysFixed"

	if part:GetAttribute(
		attributeName
	) == true then

		return
	end

	local gunBelt =
		character:FindFirstChild(
			"GunBelt",
			true
		)

	if not gunBelt
		or not gunBelt:IsA("Attachment") then

		return
	end

	local joint =
		findWeaponBodyJoint(
			part,
			character
		)

	if not joint then
		return
	end

	local attachmentPosition =
		visual.AttachmentPosition
		or Vector3.zero

	local attachmentOrientation =
		visual.AttachmentOrientation
		or Vector3.zero

	local weaponAttachment =
		CFrame.new(
			attachmentPosition
		)
		* CFrame.Angles(
			math.rad(
				attachmentOrientation.X
			),
			math.rad(
				attachmentOrientation.Y
			),
			math.rad(
				attachmentOrientation.Z
			)
		)

	local desired =
		gunBelt.WorldCFrame
		* weaponAttachment:Inverse()

	-- Harvester/Icepiercer use the same long-gun correction.
	desired =
		desired
		* CFrame.Angles(
			math.rad(90),
			0,
			0
		)

	-- Solve only the weapon-side joint offset.
	-- The character/body side is NEVER changed.
	if joint.Part1 == part then
		joint.C1 =
			desired:ToObjectSpace(
				joint.Part0.CFrame
				* joint.C0
			)

	elseif joint.Part0 == part then
		joint.C0 =
			desired:ToObjectSpace(
				joint.Part1.CFrame
				* joint.C1
			)
	else
		return
	end

	part:SetAttribute(
		attributeName,
		true
	)
end


local function placeVerifiedBackWeapon(
	part,
	character,
	weaponName,
	visual
)
	if weaponName ~= "Celestial"
		or not part
		or not character
		or not visual then

		return
	end

	if part:GetAttribute(
		"BotControllerCelestialBackFixed"
	) == true then

		return
	end

	local knifeBack =
		character:FindFirstChild(
			"KnifeBack",
			true
		)

	if not knifeBack
		or not knifeBack:IsA("Attachment") then

		return
	end

	local joint =
		findWeaponBodyJoint(
			part,
			character
		)

	if not joint then
		return
	end

	local attachmentPosition =
		visual.AttachmentPosition
		or Vector3.zero

	local attachmentOrientation =
		visual.AttachmentOrientation
		or Vector3.zero

	local weaponAttachment =
		CFrame.new(
			attachmentPosition
		)
		* CFrame.Angles(
			math.rad(
				attachmentOrientation.X
			),
			math.rad(
				attachmentOrientation.Y
			),
			math.rad(
				attachmentOrientation.Z
			)
		)

	local desiredWeaponCFrame =
		knifeBack.WorldCFrame
		* weaponAttachment:Inverse()

	-- Change ONLY the weapon side of the joint.
	if joint.Part1 == part then
		joint.C1 =
			desiredWeaponCFrame:ToObjectSpace(
				joint.Part0.CFrame
				* joint.C0
			)

	elseif joint.Part0 == part then
		joint.C0 =
			desiredWeaponCFrame:ToObjectSpace(
				joint.Part1.CFrame
				* joint.C1
			)
	else
		return
	end

	part:SetAttribute(
		"BotControllerCelestialBackFixed",
		true
	)
end

local function placeGunUsingExactAttachment(
	part,
	character,
	weaponName,
	visual
)
	if not part
		or not character
		or not visual
		or weaponName == "Harvester"
		or weaponName == "Icepiercer"
		or weaponName == "Gingerscope"
		or visual.Type ~= "Gun"
		or not visual.AttachmentPosition
		or not visual.AttachmentOrientation then

		return
	end

	local gunBelt =
		character:FindFirstChild(
			"GunBelt",
			true
		)

	if not gunBelt
		or not gunBelt:IsA("Attachment") then

		return
	end

	local joint =
		findWeaponBodyJoint(
			part,
			character
		)

	if not joint then
		return
	end

	local orientation =
		visual.AttachmentOrientation

	local weaponAttachment =
		CFrame.new(
			visual.AttachmentPosition
		)
		* CFrame.Angles(
			math.rad(orientation.X),
			math.rad(orientation.Y),
			math.rad(orientation.Z)
		)

	local desired =
		gunBelt.WorldCFrame
		* weaponAttachment:Inverse()

	-- Weapon side only. Never rotate the avatar/body side.
	if joint.Part1 == part then
		joint.C1 =
			desired:ToObjectSpace(
				joint.Part0.CFrame
				* joint.C0
			)
	elseif joint.Part0 == part then
		joint.C0 =
			desired:ToObjectSpace(
				joint.Part1.CFrame
				* joint.C1
			)
	end
end

local function applyVisualToExistingSpecialMesh(
	candidate,
	visual,
	weaponName
)
	if not candidate
		or not candidate.part
		or not candidate.part.Parent
		or not candidate.mesh
		or not candidate.mesh.Parent then

		return false
	end

	local part =
		candidate.part

	local mesh =
		candidate.mesh

	local character =
		player.Character

	-- Save the exact original mesh AND the weapon-side joint.
	saveOriginalWeaponMeshState(
		part,
		mesh,
		character
	)

	-- Replace only the existing SpecialMesh.
	mesh.MeshType =
		Enum.MeshType.FileMesh

	mesh.MeshId =
		visual.MeshId

	mesh.TextureId =
		visual.TextureId or ""

	-- Use Harvester's REAL scale.
	-- No negative/mirrored scale.
	mesh.Scale =
		visual.Scale
		or Vector3.new(1, 1, 1)

	mesh.Offset =
		visual.Offset
		or Vector3.zero

	if visual.VertexColor then
		mesh.VertexColor =
			visual.VertexColor
	end

	if visual.Color then
		part.Color =
			visual.Color
	end

	if visual.Material then
		part.Material =
			visual.Material
	end

	if visual.Transparency ~= nil then
		part.Transparency =
			visual.Transparency
	else
		part.Transparency = 0
	end

	if visual.Reflectance ~= nil then
		part.Reflectance =
			visual.Reflectance
	end

	part.LocalTransparencyModifier = 0

	part:SetAttribute(
		"BotControllerWeaponReplacement",
		weaponName
	)

	-- Keep an exact pointer to the REAL MM2 host.
	-- Persistence can now tell the difference between the actual
	-- gun and our own workspace backup visual.
	WeaponPackRuntime.currentReplacementPart =
		part

	WeaponPackRuntime.currentReplacementWeaponName =
		weaponName

	WeaponPackRuntime.currentReplacementCharacter =
		character

	-- Turn only the existing weapon sideways.
	-- Does not modify the character's body weld side.
	orientLongGunCorrectly(
		part,
		character,
		weaponName,
		visual
	)

	placeVerifiedBackWeapon(
		part,
		character,
		weaponName,
		visual
	)

	placeGunUsingExactAttachment(
		part,
		character,
		weaponName,
		visual
	)

	return true
end

local function replaceCurrentEquippedWeaponMesh(character, weaponName, allowDeepSearch)
	if not character then
		return false, "character missing"
	end

	-- Gingerscope is special: its fallback source is a real Waist Accessory.
	-- Do NOT strip it into a SpecialMesh and do NOT rotate the MM2 host.
	-- Just remember the real MM2 gun host, hide it locally, and attach the
	-- complete Gingerscope accessory using its creator-supplied attachment.
	if weaponName == "Gingerscope" then
		-- Stop the older generic original-weapon mask if it was active.
		-- Gingerscope has its own strict gun-only mask below.
		stopRealWeaponHideLoop(character)

		WeaponPackRuntime.currentReplacementPart =
			nil

		WeaponPackRuntime.currentReplacementWeaponName =
			nil

		WeaponPackRuntime.currentReplacementCharacter =
			nil

		WeaponPackRuntime.destroyPersistentGunFallback()

		local nativeOk, nativeReason =
			WeaponPackRuntime.ensureGingerscopeNativeAccessory(
				character
			)

		if not nativeOk then
			return false,
				nativeReason
				or "native Gingerscope accessory failed"
		end

		WeaponPackRuntime.enforceGingerscopeVisualIsolation(
			character
		)

		return true
	end

	local visual =
		getWeaponVisualData(weaponName, allowDeepSearch)

	if not visual then
		return false,
			"no SpecialMesh data for " ..
			tostring(weaponName)
	end

	-- Remove only old cosmetic weapons made by earlier script versions.
	local oldScriptWeapon =
		character:FindFirstChild("HolsteredWeapon")

	if oldScriptWeapon then
		oldScriptWeapon:Destroy()
	end

	local candidates =
		getCurrentWeaponSpecialMeshCandidates(
			character,
			visual.Type or "Gun"
		)

	if #candidates == 0 then
		return false,
			"current weapon SpecialMesh not found"
	end

	-- Only modify the single best-matching CURRENT weapon.
	local best =
		candidates[1]

	local success =
		applyVisualToExistingSpecialMesh(
			best,
			visual,
			weaponName
		)

	if not success then
		return false,
			"could not edit current weapon SpecialMesh"
	end

	-- For Gingerscope the persistent cosmetic is deliberately the ONLY
	-- visible gun. The MM2-owned host remains available internally but hidden.
	if weaponName == "Gingerscope" then
		if WeaponPackRuntime.ensurePersistentGunFallback then
			WeaponPackRuntime.ensurePersistentGunFallback(
				character,
				weaponName,
				true
			)
		end

		if WeaponPackRuntime.hideCurrentGunHost then
			WeaponPackRuntime.hideCurrentGunHost(
				character,
				weaponName
			)
		end

	else
		-- Other guns retain the existing ready-but-hidden backup behavior.
		if WeaponPackRuntime.setPersistentGunFallbackVisible then
			WeaponPackRuntime.setPersistentGunFallbackVisible(
				false
			)
		end
	end

	return true
end

local function scanWeapons()
	table.clear(availableWeaponNames)

	for _, name in ipairs(WEAPON_LIST) do
		if not WeaponPackRuntime.PLAYER_WEAPON_EQUIP_DISABLED[
			name
		] then

			table.insert(
				availableWeaponNames,
				name
			)
		end
	end

	weaponStatus.Text =
		"Loaded " ..
		#availableWeaponNames ..
		" verified weapons"

	weaponStatus.TextColor3 =
		Color3.fromRGB(
			90,
			235,
			130
		)
end

equipPlayerWeapon = function(name)
	local character =
		player.Character

	if not character then
		return
	end

	local wantedName = nil

	for _, configuredName in ipairs(WEAPON_LIST) do
		if not WeaponPackRuntime.PLAYER_WEAPON_EQUIP_DISABLED[
			configuredName
		]
			and isWeaponNameMatch(
				name,
				configuredName
			) then

			wantedName =
				configuredName

			break
		end
	end

	if not wantedName then
		weaponStatus.Text =
			"Weapon not in configured list"

		weaponStatus.TextColor3 =
			Color3.fromRGB(255, 105, 105)

		return
	end

	weaponStatus.Text =
		"Loading " .. wantedName .. "..."

	weaponStatus.TextColor3 =
		Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 210)

	local wantedType =
		WeaponPackRuntime.WEAPON_TYPE_HINTS[
			wantedName
		]
		or (
			wantedName == "Harvester"
			and "Gun"
			or "Knife"
		)

	local previousSlotName =
		wantedType == "Gun"
		and WeaponPackRuntime.selectedGunName
		or WeaponPackRuntime.selectedKnifeName

	-- SLOT-SPECIFIC SWITCH CLEANUP:
	-- Changing a gun must never remove the selected knife.
	-- Changing a knife must never remove the selected gun.
	if previousSlotName
		and previousSlotName ~= wantedName then

		if wantedType == "Gun" then
			-- Only remove gun cosmetics/persistence.
			WeaponPackRuntime.selectedGunName = nil

			WeaponPackRuntime.destroyGingerscopeNativeAccessory()
			WeaponPackRuntime.destroyPersistentGunFallback()

			clearHolsteredWeapon(
				character,
				"HolsteredGun"
			)

			-- If Gingerscope had hidden MM2's real gun, reveal weapon hosts
			-- before the next gun visual searches for its host.
			WeaponPackRuntime.restoreOriginalWeaponVisibility(
				character
			)

		else
			-- Only remove the knife cosmetic slot.
			WeaponPackRuntime.selectedKnifeName = nil

			clearHolsteredWeapon(
				character,
				"HolsteredKnife"
			)
		end

		-- Do NOT call restoreOriginalWeaponMeshes() here.
		-- The next weapon in the SAME slot overwrites the same MM2 host,
		-- while the opposite slot stays untouched.
		RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
	end

	-- Make the new slot selection visible to the render masks BEFORE
	-- we modify the MM2 host. This prevents Gingerscope's mask from hiding
	-- a knife during the tiny interval while it is being replaced.
	if wantedType == "Gun" then
		WeaponPackRuntime.selectedGunName =
			wantedName
	else
		WeaponPackRuntime.selectedKnifeName =
			wantedName
	end

	if wantedType == "Gun"
		and wantedName ~= "Gingerscope" then

		WeaponPackRuntime.restoreGingerscopeHardHiddenParts()
		WeaponPackRuntime.destroyGingerscopeNativeAccessory()
	end

	if wantedName == "Gingerscope" then
		WeaponPackRuntime.persistentGunFallbackLocalCFrame = nil
		WeaponPackRuntime.persistentGunFallbackPoseWeapon = nil
		WeaponPackRuntime.persistentGunFallbackUsesRootLock = false
		WeaponPackRuntime.destroyPersistentGunFallback()
	end

	local success, reason =
		replaceCurrentEquippedWeaponMesh(
			character,
			wantedName,
			true
		)

	if not success then
		-- Roll back only the slot that failed.
		if wantedType == "Gun" then
			WeaponPackRuntime.selectedGunName =
				previousSlotName
		else
			WeaponPackRuntime.selectedKnifeName =
				previousSlotName
		end

		weaponStatus.Text =
			"Could not load " ..
			wantedName ..
			": " ..
			tostring(reason)

		weaponStatus.TextColor3 =
			Color3.fromRGB(255, 105, 105)

		return
	end

	if WeaponPackRuntime.persistentGunFallbackPoseWeapon
		and WeaponPackRuntime.persistentGunFallbackPoseWeapon
			~= wantedName then

		WeaponPackRuntime.persistentGunFallbackLocalCFrame =
			nil

		WeaponPackRuntime.persistentGunFallbackPoseWeapon =
			nil
	end

	selectedWeaponName =
		wantedName

	WeaponPackRuntime.bindPlayerWeaponPersistence(
		character
	)

	local equippedVisual =
		getWeaponVisualData(
			wantedName
		)

	if wantedName == "Gingerscope" then
		WeaponPackRuntime.destroyPersistentGunFallback()

		local nativeOk, nativeReason =
			WeaponPackRuntime.ensureGingerscopeNativeAccessory(
				character
			)

		if not nativeOk then
			weaponStatus.Text =
				"Could not attach Gingerscope: " ..
				tostring(nativeReason)

			weaponStatus.TextColor3 =
				Color3.fromRGB(255, 105, 105)

			return
		end

		WeaponPackRuntime.enforceGingerscopeVisualIsolation(
			character
		)

	elseif equippedVisual
		and equippedVisual.Type == "Gun" then

		-- Other guns keep the existing persistence path.
		WeaponPackRuntime.ensurePersistentGunFallback(
			character,
			wantedName,
			true
		)

		WeaponPackRuntime.hideCurrentGunHost(
			character,
			wantedName
		)
	else
		-- Knife slot: leave the selected gun/Gingerscope persistence alone.
	end

	weaponBox.Text =
		wantedName

	local gunLabel =
		WeaponPackRuntime.selectedGunName
		or "-"

	local knifeLabel =
		WeaponPackRuntime.selectedKnifeName
		or "-"

	weaponStatus.Text =
		"Gun: " ..
		gunLabel ..
		"  |  Knife: " ..
		knifeLabel

	weaponStatus.TextColor3 =
		Color3.fromRGB(90, 235, 130)
end

local function removePlayerWeaponReplacement()
	-- Stop automatic reapplication first.
	selectedWeaponName = nil
	WeaponPackRuntime.selectedGunName = nil
	WeaponPackRuntime.selectedKnifeName = nil

	WeaponPackRuntime.disconnectPlayerWeaponPersistence()

	WeaponPackRuntime.restoreGingerscopeHardHiddenParts()
	WeaponPackRuntime.destroyGingerscopeNativeAccessory()

	-- Explicit removal is the only time we intentionally forget the
	-- saved persistent pose.
	WeaponPackRuntime.persistentGunFallbackLocalCFrame =
		nil

	WeaponPackRuntime.persistentGunFallbackPoseWeapon =
		nil

	WeaponPackRuntime.persistentGunFallbackUsesRootLock =
		false

	WeaponPackRuntime.weaponPersistenceGeneration += 1

	WeaponPackRuntime.roundReequipGeneration =
		(WeaponPackRuntime.roundReequipGeneration or 0)
		+ 1

	WeaponPackRuntime.destroyPersistentGunFallback()

	WeaponPackRuntime.currentReplacementPart =
		nil

	WeaponPackRuntime.currentReplacementWeaponName =
		nil

	WeaponPackRuntime.currentReplacementCharacter =
		nil

	local restored =
		restoreOriginalWeaponMeshes()

	-- The Gingerscope mask hides original gun/knife geometry directly.
	-- On explicit removal, restore those local visibility modifiers.
	local characterForVisibility =
		player.Character

	if characterForVisibility then
		for _, object in ipairs(
			characterForVisibility:GetDescendants()
		) do
			if object:IsA("BasePart")
				and isOriginalWeaponPart(
					object,
					characterForVisibility
				) then

				pcall(function()
					object.LocalTransparencyModifier = 0
				end)
			end
		end
	end

	-- Remove any leftover cosmetic weapon from older script versions.
	local character =
		player.Character

	if character then
		local old =
			character:FindFirstChild("HolsteredWeapon")

		if old then
			old:Destroy()
		end

		if WeaponPackRuntime.removePersistentGunFallback then
			WeaponPackRuntime.removePersistentGunFallback(
				character
			)
		end
	end

	weaponBox.Text = ""

	if restored > 0 then
		weaponStatus.Text = "Original weapon restored"
		weaponStatus.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(90, 235, 130)
	else
		weaponStatus.Text = "Weapon replacement removed"
		weaponStatus.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 210)
	end
end

removeWeaponButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
	removePlayerWeaponReplacement()
end)

equipWeaponButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
	equipPlayerWeapon(weaponBox.Text)
end)

weaponBox.FocusLost:Connect(function(enterPressed)
	if enterPressed then
		equipPlayerWeapon(weaponBox.Text)
	end
end)

scanWeapons()


rebuildWeaponButtons()

-- Safe post-build font pass.
task.defer(function()
	if gui
		and gui.Parent
		and WeaponPackRuntime.ONE_SLICE_FONT then

		WeaponPackRuntime.applyOneSliceToGui(
			gui
		)
	end
end)

--------------------------------------------------
-- KEEP PLAYER GUN VISIBLE THROUGH ROUND TRANSITIONS
--
-- IMPORTANT DESIGN:
-- * The backup gun lives in WORKSPACE, not inside Character.
--   MM2's round cleanup can delete Character weapon descendants.
-- * The backup is created BEFORE it is needed and normally hidden.
-- * If the real MM2 gun host starts disappearing, the backup becomes
--   visible immediately.
-- * The real MM2 gun is always preferred. Once it returns and gets
--   its mesh replaced again, the backup becomes invisible.
--------------------------------------------------

local nextWeaponMeshRefresh = 0

WeaponPackRuntime.currentReplacementPart =
	WeaponPackRuntime.currentReplacementPart
	or nil

WeaponPackRuntime.currentReplacementWeaponName =
	WeaponPackRuntime.currentReplacementWeaponName
	or nil

WeaponPackRuntime.currentReplacementCharacter =
	WeaponPackRuntime.currentReplacementCharacter
	or nil

WeaponPackRuntime.persistentGunFallback =
	WeaponPackRuntime.persistentGunFallback
	or nil

WeaponPackRuntime.persistentGunFallbackCharacter =
	WeaponPackRuntime.persistentGunFallbackCharacter
	or nil

WeaponPackRuntime.persistentGunFallbackWeapon =
	WeaponPackRuntime.persistentGunFallbackWeapon
	or nil

-- Exact pose of the visible persistent gun relative to its torso anchor.
-- This survives round/map transitions and is reused if the visual must
-- ever be rebuilt.
WeaponPackRuntime.persistentGunFallbackLocalCFrame =
	WeaponPackRuntime.persistentGunFallbackLocalCFrame
	or nil

WeaponPackRuntime.persistentGunFallbackPoseWeapon =
	WeaponPackRuntime.persistentGunFallbackPoseWeapon
	or nil

WeaponPackRuntime.persistentGunFallbackUsesRootLock =
	WeaponPackRuntime.persistentGunFallbackUsesRootLock
	or false

WeaponPackRuntime.lastGunBeltLocalCFrame =
	WeaponPackRuntime.lastGunBeltLocalCFrame
	or nil

WeaponPackRuntime.playerWeaponPersistenceConnection =
	WeaponPackRuntime.playerWeaponPersistenceConnection
	or nil

WeaponPackRuntime.playerWeaponRemovingConnection =
	WeaponPackRuntime.playerWeaponRemovingConnection
	or nil

WeaponPackRuntime.weaponPersistenceGeneration =
	WeaponPackRuntime.weaponPersistenceGeneration
	or 0

WeaponPackRuntime.getPersistentVisualFolder =
	function()
		-- IMPORTANT:
		-- Do NOT parent this to CurrentCamera. MM2 can replace the camera
		-- between lobby and round, which destroys/reparents camera children
		-- and previously forced the gun to rebuild with a different pose.
		local folder =
			workspace:FindFirstChild(
				"SlaveControllerPersistentVisuals"
			)

		if not folder then
			folder =
				Instance.new(
					"Folder"
				)

			folder.Name =
				"SlaveControllerPersistentVisuals"

			folder:SetAttribute(
				"ScriptOwnedPersistentVisuals",
				true
			)

			folder.Parent =
				workspace
		end

		return folder
	end

WeaponPackRuntime.findBodyGunBelt =
	function(character)
		if not character then
			return nil
		end

		local torso =
			character:FindFirstChild(
				"LowerTorso"
			)
			or character:FindFirstChild(
				"Torso"
			)

		if torso then
			local direct =
				torso:FindFirstChild(
					"GunBelt"
				)

			if direct
				and direct:IsA(
					"Attachment"
				) then

				return direct
			end
		end

		-- Fallback recursive search, but ONLY accept an attachment
		-- whose parent is an actual body part directly under Character.
		for _, object in ipairs(
			character:GetDescendants()
		) do
			if object:IsA("Attachment")
				and object.Name == "GunBelt"
				and object.Parent
				and object.Parent:IsA("BasePart")
				and object.Parent.Parent == character
				and PLAYER_BODY_NAMES[
					object.Parent.Name
				] then

				return object
			end
		end

		return nil
	end

WeaponPackRuntime.isPersistentGunFallbackHealthy =
	function(character, weaponName)
		local model =
			WeaponPackRuntime.persistentGunFallback

		if not model
			or not model.Parent
			or WeaponPackRuntime.persistentGunFallbackCharacter
				~= character
			or WeaponPackRuntime.persistentGunFallbackWeapon
				~= weaponName then

			return false
		end

		local root =
			model.PrimaryPart

		if not root
			or not root.Parent
			or not root:IsDescendantOf(model) then

			return false
		end

		if weaponName == "Gingerscope" then
			-- Gingerscope is an anchored client-only visual driven from
			-- HumanoidRootPart every rendered frame. It intentionally has
			-- NO body weld, so LowerTorso/GunBelt changes cannot move it.
			local hrp =
				character:FindFirstChild(
					"HumanoidRootPart"
				)

			if not hrp
				or not hrp:IsA("BasePart")
				or root.Anchored ~= true
				or not WeaponPackRuntime.persistentGunFallbackLocalCFrame
				or WeaponPackRuntime.persistentGunFallbackPoseWeapon
					~= "Gingerscope" then

				return false
			end
		else
			local weld =
				root:FindFirstChild(
					"PersistentGunFallbackWeld"
				)

			if not weld
				or not weld:IsA("WeldConstraint")
				or weld.Part1 ~= root
				or not weld.Part0
				or not weld.Part0.Parent
				or not weld.Part0:IsDescendantOf(character) then

				return false
			end
		end

		local expectedFolder =
			WeaponPackRuntime.getPersistentVisualFolder()

		if model.Parent ~= expectedFolder then
			return false
		end

		return true
	end


WeaponPackRuntime.destroyPersistentGunFallback =
	function()
		local model =
			WeaponPackRuntime.persistentGunFallback

		if model
			and model.Parent then

			model:Destroy()
		end

		WeaponPackRuntime.persistentGunFallback =
			nil

		WeaponPackRuntime.persistentGunFallbackCharacter =
			nil

		WeaponPackRuntime.persistentGunFallbackWeapon =
			nil

		WeaponPackRuntime.persistentGunFallbackUsesRootLock =
			false
	end

-- Backwards-compatible name used by REMOVE WEAPON in older code.
WeaponPackRuntime.removePersistentGunFallback =
	function(character)
		WeaponPackRuntime.destroyPersistentGunFallback()
	end

WeaponPackRuntime.setPersistentGunFallbackVisible =
	function(visible)
		local model =
			WeaponPackRuntime.persistentGunFallback

		if not model
			or not model.Parent then

			return false
		end

		for _, object in ipairs(
			model:GetDescendants()
		) do
			if object:IsA("BasePart") then
				object.LocalTransparencyModifier =
					visible
					and 0
					or 1
			elseif object:IsA("ParticleEmitter")
				or object:IsA("Trail")
				or object:IsA("Beam") then

				object.Enabled =
					visible
			end
		end

		model:SetAttribute(
			"FallbackVisible",
			visible
		)

		return true
	end

WeaponPackRuntime.ensurePersistentGunFallback =
	function(character, weaponName, visible)
		if weaponName == "Gingerscope" then
			WeaponPackRuntime.destroyPersistentGunFallback()

			local ok =
				WeaponPackRuntime.ensureGingerscopeNativeAccessory(
					character
				)

			if ok then
				WeaponPackRuntime.setGingerscopeNativeVisible(
					visible ~= false
				)
			end

			return ok == true
		end

		if not character
			or character ~= player.Character
			or not weaponName then

			return false
		end

		local visual =
			getWeaponVisualData(
				weaponName
			)

		if not visual
			or visual.Type ~= "Gun" then

			WeaponPackRuntime.destroyPersistentGunFallback()
			return false
		end

		if WeaponPackRuntime.isPersistentGunFallbackHealthy(
			character,
			weaponName
		) then

			WeaponPackRuntime.setPersistentGunFallbackVisible(
				visible == true
			)

			return true
		end

		-- If MM2 deleted only the Handle/weld but left the Model behind,
		-- the old version got stuck here forever. Rebuild damaged backups.
		WeaponPackRuntime.destroyPersistentGunFallback()

		local source =
			cloneWeaponAsTool(
				weaponName
			)

		if not source then
			return false
		end

		local model =
			prepareWeaponVisual(
				source,
				weaponName,
				"PersistentGunFallback"
			)

		if not model
			or not model.PrimaryPart then

			if model then
				model:Destroy()
			end

			return false
		end

		model:SetAttribute(
			"ScriptOwnedWeapon",
			true
		)

		model:SetAttribute(
			"OwnerUserId",
			player.UserId
		)

		local bodyAttachment =
			WeaponPackRuntime.findBodyGunBelt(
				character
			)

		local placementAnchor =
			bodyAttachment
			and bodyAttachment.Parent
			or character:FindFirstChild(
				"LowerTorso"
			)
			or character:FindFirstChild(
				"Torso"
			)
			or character:FindFirstChild(
				"UpperTorso"
			)
			or character:FindFirstChild(
				"HumanoidRootPart"
			)

		-- Gingerscope must not follow LowerTorso/GunBelt after placement.
		-- HumanoidRootPart is the stable reference through animations and
		-- lobby/round teleports. Other guns retain their existing behavior.
		local anchor =
			weaponName == "Gingerscope"
			and character:FindFirstChild(
				"HumanoidRootPart"
			)
			or placementAnchor

		if not placementAnchor
			or not placementAnchor:IsA("BasePart")
			or not anchor
			or not anchor:IsA("BasePart") then

			model:Destroy()
			return false
		end

		local targetWorldCFrame

		if bodyAttachment then
			WeaponPackRuntime.lastGunBeltLocalCFrame =
				bodyAttachment.CFrame

			targetWorldCFrame =
				bodyAttachment.WorldCFrame

		elseif WeaponPackRuntime.lastGunBeltLocalCFrame then
			targetWorldCFrame =
				placementAnchor.CFrame
				* WeaponPackRuntime.lastGunBeltLocalCFrame

		else
			targetWorldCFrame =
				getFallbackBeltWorldCFrame(
					placementAnchor,
					"WaistRight"
				)
		end

		local data =
			WEAPON_HOLSTER_DATA[
				weaponName
			]
			or FALLBACK_WEAPON_VISUALS[
				weaponName
			]
			or {
				AttachmentPosition =
					Vector3.zero,
				AttachmentOrientation =
					Vector3.zero,
			}

		local weaponAttachmentCFrame =
			buildAttachmentCFrame(
				data
			)

		local finalWorldCFrame =
			targetWorldCFrame
			* weaponAttachmentCFrame:Inverse()

		if weaponName == "Harvester"
			or weaponName == "Icepiercer" then

			finalWorldCFrame =
				finalWorldCFrame
				* CFrame.Angles(
					math.rad(90),
					0,
					0
				)

		elseif weaponName == "Gingerscope" then
			-- First build uses the tuned hip pose. Every later rebuild uses
			-- the EXACT saved HumanoidRootPart-relative pose.
			if WeaponPackRuntime.persistentGunFallbackLocalCFrame
				and WeaponPackRuntime.persistentGunFallbackPoseWeapon
					== weaponName then

				finalWorldCFrame =
					anchor.CFrame
					* WeaponPackRuntime.persistentGunFallbackLocalCFrame
			else
				finalWorldCFrame =
					getGingerscopeWorldCFrame(
						targetWorldCFrame
					)
			end
		end

		local root =
			model.PrimaryPart

		-- If MM2's current gun host still exists, copy its exact transform.
		-- This prevents the persistent cosmetic from shifting slightly
		-- when the round-owned host gets deleted at round end.
		local liveHost =
			WeaponPackRuntime.currentReplacementPart

		if weaponName ~= "Gingerscope"
			and liveHost
			and liveHost.Parent
			and WeaponPackRuntime.currentReplacementCharacter == character
			and WeaponPackRuntime.currentReplacementWeaponName == weaponName then

			finalWorldCFrame =
				liveHost.CFrame
		end

		root.CFrame =
			finalWorldCFrame

		if weaponName == "Gingerscope" then
			-- Save ONCE. Do not overwrite this during a round transition or
			-- rebuild; that would allow a temporary shifted pose to become
			-- the new permanent pose. Anchor is HumanoidRootPart here.
			if not WeaponPackRuntime.persistentGunFallbackLocalCFrame
				or WeaponPackRuntime.persistentGunFallbackPoseWeapon
					~= weaponName then

				WeaponPackRuntime.persistentGunFallbackLocalCFrame =
					anchor.CFrame:ToObjectSpace(
						root.CFrame
					)

				WeaponPackRuntime.persistentGunFallbackPoseWeapon =
					weaponName
			end

			-- Purely visual, so remove physics/body-joint dependency entirely.
			root.Anchored = true
			WeaponPackRuntime.persistentGunFallbackUsesRootLock = true

			model:SetAttribute(
				"UnifiedGingerscopePlacement",
				true
			)
		else
			WeaponPackRuntime.persistentGunFallbackUsesRootLock = false
		end

		-- Parent to the persistent client-only visual folder.
		-- MM2 can clear Character weapon descendants at round end.
		model.Parent =
			WeaponPackRuntime.getPersistentVisualFolder()

		if weaponName ~= "Gingerscope" then
			local weld =
				Instance.new(
					"WeldConstraint"
				)

			weld.Name =
				"PersistentGunFallbackWeld"

			weld.Part0 =
				anchor

			weld.Part1 =
				root

			weld.Parent =
				root
		end

		WeaponPackRuntime.persistentGunFallback =
			model

		WeaponPackRuntime.persistentGunFallbackCharacter =
			character

		WeaponPackRuntime.persistentGunFallbackWeapon =
			weaponName

		WeaponPackRuntime.setPersistentGunFallbackVisible(
			visible == true
		)

		return true
	end

WeaponPackRuntime.isCurrentReplacementAlive =
	function(character, weaponName)
		local part =
			WeaponPackRuntime.currentReplacementPart

		if not part
			or not part.Parent
			or WeaponPackRuntime.currentReplacementCharacter
				~= character
			or WeaponPackRuntime.currentReplacementWeaponName
				~= weaponName then

			return false
		end

		return part:GetAttribute(
			"BotControllerWeaponReplacement"
		) == weaponName
	end

WeaponPackRuntime.hideCurrentGunHost =
	function(character, weaponName)
		if weaponName == "Gingerscope" then
			WeaponPackRuntime.enforceGingerscopeVisualIsolation(
				character
			)
			return true
		end

		local part =
			WeaponPackRuntime.currentReplacementPart

		if not part
			or not part.Parent
			or WeaponPackRuntime.currentReplacementCharacter
				~= character
			or WeaponPackRuntime.currentReplacementWeaponName
				~= weaponName then

			return false
		end

		-- Gingerscope uses the persistent cosmetic as its ONLY visible model.
		-- MM2 may reset LocalTransparencyModifier during its own render/update
		-- loops, so this function is also enforced every rendered frame below.
		local container =
			part:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Tool")
			or part:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Accessory")
			or part

		local function hideObject(object)
			if object:IsA("BasePart") then
				-- Never touch one of our own persistent cosmetic parts.
				if not object:FindFirstAncestor(
					"SlaveControllerPersistentVisuals"
				) then
					pcall(function()
						object.LocalTransparencyModifier = 1
					end)
				end

			elseif object:IsA("Decal")
				or object:IsA("Texture") then

				pcall(function()
					object.Transparency = 1
				end)

			elseif object:IsA("ParticleEmitter")
				or object:IsA("Trail")
				or object:IsA("Beam")
				or object:IsA("Smoke")
				or object:IsA("Fire")
				or object:IsA("Sparkles") then

				pcall(function()
					object.Enabled = false
				end)
			end
		end

		hideObject(container)

		for _, object in ipairs(
			container:GetDescendants()
		) do
			hideObject(object)
		end

		return true
	end

WeaponPackRuntime.tryRestoreSelectedWeapon =
	function(character)
		if selectedWeaponName == "Gingerscope" then
			if not character
				or character ~= player.Character
				or not character.Parent then

				return false
			end

			local ok =
				WeaponPackRuntime.ensureGingerscopeNativeAccessory(
					character
				)

			WeaponPackRuntime.enforceGingerscopeVisualIsolation(
				character
			)

			return ok == true
		end

		if not selectedWeaponName
			or not character
			or character ~= player.Character
			or not character.Parent then

			return false
		end

		local visual =
			getWeaponVisualData(
				selectedWeaponName
			)

		if not visual then
			return false
		end

		if visual.Type == "Gun" then
			-- Permanent visible cosmetic. Do NOT hide this during round changes.
			WeaponPackRuntime.ensurePersistentGunFallback(
				character,
				selectedWeaponName,
				true
			)
		end

		if WeaponPackRuntime.isCurrentReplacementAlive(
			character,
			selectedWeaponName
		) then

			if visual.Type == "Gun" then
				WeaponPackRuntime.ensurePersistentGunFallback(
					character,
					selectedWeaponName,
					true
				)
				WeaponPackRuntime.hideCurrentGunHost(
					character,
					selectedWeaponName
				)
			end

			return true
		end

		local success =
			replaceCurrentEquippedWeaponMesh(
				character,
				selectedWeaponName
			)

		if success then
			if visual.Type == "Gun" then
				WeaponPackRuntime.ensurePersistentGunFallback(
					character,
					selectedWeaponName,
					true
				)
				WeaponPackRuntime.hideCurrentGunHost(
					character,
					selectedWeaponName
				)
			end

			weaponBox.Text =
				selectedWeaponName

			return true
		end

		if visual.Type == "Gun" then
			WeaponPackRuntime.setPersistentGunFallbackVisible(
				true
			)
		end

		return false
	end

WeaponPackRuntime.roundReequipGeneration =
	WeaponPackRuntime.roundReequipGeneration
	or 0

WeaponPackRuntime.forceRoundWeaponReequip =
	function(character, reason)
		if selectedWeaponName == "Gingerscope" then
			if character
				and character == player.Character
				and character.Parent then

				WeaponPackRuntime.ensureGingerscopeNativeAccessory(
					character
				)

				WeaponPackRuntime.enforceGingerscopeVisualIsolation(
					character
				)
			end

			return
		end

		if not selectedWeaponName
			or not character
			or character ~= player.Character
			or not character.Parent then

			return
		end

		WeaponPackRuntime.roundReequipGeneration += 1

		local generation =
			WeaponPackRuntime.roundReequipGeneration

		task.spawn(function()
			-- Do NOT stop after the first success.
			-- During a round transition MM2 can:
			--   1. keep the old gun briefly
			--   2. delete it
			--   3. create the lobby/new-round gun later
			--
			-- Re-applying for several seconds guarantees we eventually
			-- hit the FINAL gun object that survives the transition.
			for attempt = 1, 32 do
				if generation
						~= WeaponPackRuntime.roundReequipGeneration
					or not selectedWeaponName
					or player.Character ~= character
					or not character.Parent then

					return
				end

				local visual =
					getWeaponVisualData(
						selectedWeaponName
					)

				if not visual then
					return
				end

				local realStillAlive =
					WeaponPackRuntime.isCurrentReplacementAlive(
						character,
						selectedWeaponName
					)

				-- If the replacement that belongs to the CURRENT selection is
				-- already alive, leave its mesh/joint completely alone.
				-- This is especially important for knives: repeatedly rewriting
				-- the host every 0.15s caused the gun/knife flicker.
				if realStillAlive then
					if visual.Type == "Gun" then
						WeaponPackRuntime.ensurePersistentGunFallback(
							character,
							selectedWeaponName,
							true
						)

						WeaponPackRuntime.hideCurrentGunHost(
							character,
							selectedWeaponName
						)
					end

					task.wait(
						attempt <= 12
						and 0.15
						or 0.25
					)

					continue
				end

				if visual.Type == "Gun" then
					WeaponPackRuntime.ensurePersistentGunFallback(
						character,
						selectedWeaponName,
						true
					)
				end

				local success =
					replaceCurrentEquippedWeaponMesh(
						character,
						selectedWeaponName
					)

				if success then
					if visual.Type == "Gun" then
						WeaponPackRuntime.ensurePersistentGunFallback(
							character,
							selectedWeaponName,
							true
						)
						WeaponPackRuntime.hideCurrentGunHost(
							character,
							selectedWeaponName
						)
					end

					weaponBox.Text =
						selectedWeaponName

					weaponStatus.Text =
						"Equipped " ..
						selectedWeaponName

					weaponStatus.TextColor3 =
						Color3.fromRGB(
							90,
							235,
							130
						)
				else
					if visual.Type == "Gun" then
						WeaponPackRuntime.ensurePersistentGunFallback(
							character,
							selectedWeaponName,
							true
						)
					end
				end

				task.wait(
					attempt <= 12
					and 0.15
					or 0.25
				)
			end
		end)
	end

WeaponPackRuntime.bindPlayerWeaponPersistence =
	function(character)
		WeaponPackRuntime.disconnectPlayerWeaponPersistence()

		if not character then
			return
		end

		WeaponPackRuntime.playerWeaponPersistenceConnection =
			character.DescendantAdded:Connect(
				function(descendant)
					if not selectedWeaponName then
						return
					end

					if WeaponPackRuntime.selectedGunName == "Gingerscope" then
						-- Always hide the newly-created MM2 gun immediately.
						task.defer(function()
							if WeaponPackRuntime.selectedGunName == "Gingerscope"
								and player.Character == character
								and character.Parent then

								WeaponPackRuntime.enforceGingerscopeVisualIsolation(
									character
								)
							end
						end)

						-- If the last-changed slot is the knife, do not let a newly
						-- spawned GUN object trigger the generic knife replacement.
						if selectedWeaponName == WeaponPackRuntime.selectedKnifeName then
							local current =
								descendant

							local looksKnife =
								false

							while current
								and current ~= character do

								local lower =
									string.lower(
										tostring(
											current.Name
										)
									)

								if string.find(
									lower,
									"knife",
									1,
									true
								)
									or string.find(
										lower,
										"blade",
										1,
										true
									)
									or lower == "knifebelt"
									or lower == "knifeback" then

									looksKnife =
										true
									break
								end

								current =
									current.Parent
							end

							if not looksKnife then
								return
							end
						end
					end

					if selectedWeaponName == "Gingerscope" then
						-- MM2 may add a new Gun Tool/display mid-round.
						-- Do NOT reacquire/mesh-swap it; just hide definite guns.
						task.defer(function()
							if selectedWeaponName == "Gingerscope"
								and player.Character == character
								and character.Parent then

								WeaponPackRuntime.enforceGingerscopeVisualIsolation(
									character
								)
							end
						end)

						return
					end

					-- New MM2 gun parts can arrive in several stages.
					if descendant:IsA("SpecialMesh")
						or descendant:IsA("BasePart")
						or descendant:IsA("Attachment")
						or descendant:IsA("Accessory")
						or descendant:IsA("Tool") then

						nextWeaponMeshRefresh =
							0

						WeaponPackRuntime.forceRoundWeaponReequip(
							character,
							"current gun removed"
						)
					end
				end
			)

		WeaponPackRuntime.playerWeaponRemovingConnection =
			character.DescendantRemoving:Connect(
				function(descendant)
					if not selectedWeaponName then
						return
					end

					if selectedWeaponName == "Gingerscope" then
						-- Native Gingerscope lives in workspace, not Character.
						-- Character weapon removals must never alter its state.
						return
					end

					local visual =
						getWeaponVisualData(
							selectedWeaponName
						)

					if not visual
						or visual.Type ~= "Gun" then

						return
					end

					local current =
						WeaponPackRuntime.currentReplacementPart

					local removingCurrent =
						false

					if current
						and current.Parent then

						if descendant == current then
							removingCurrent =
								true

						elseif descendant:IsA("SpecialMesh")
							and descendant.Parent == current then

							removingCurrent =
								true

						elseif current:IsDescendantOf(
							descendant
						) then

							removingCurrent =
								true
						end
					end

					if removingCurrent then
